




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saṃyuttanikāyau


sagāthāvaggau


1. daivatāsaṃyuttaṃ

1. naḷavaggau

1. aughataraṇasuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘kathaṃ nu tvaṃ, mārisa, aughamatarī’ti? ‘appatiṭṭhaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, anāyūhaṃ  aughamatari’nti| ‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, mārisa, appatiṭṭhaṃ anāyūhaṃ aughamatarī’ti? ‘yadākhvāhaṃ, āvusau, santiṭṭhāmi tadāssu saṃsīdāmi; yadākhvāhaṃ, āvusau, āyūhāmi tadāssu nibbuyhāmi nivuyhāmi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, appatiṭṭhaṃ anāyūhaṃ aughamatari’’’nti|

‘‘cirassaṃ vata passāmi, brāhmaṇaṃ parinibbutaṃ|

appatiṭṭhaṃ anāyūhaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ laukai visattika’’nti|| –

idamavauca  sā daivatā| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau sā daivatā – ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

2. nimaukkhasuttaṃ

2. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘jānāsi nau tvaṃ, mārisa, sattānaṃ nimaukkhaṃ pamaukkhaṃ vivaika’’nti?

‘‘jānāmi khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sattānaṃ nimaukkhaṃ pamaukkhaṃ vivaika’’nti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, mārisa, jānāsi sattānaṃ nimaukkhaṃ pamaukkhaṃ vivaika’’nti?

‘‘nandībhavaparikkhayā  nandibhavaparikkhayā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sañ‍ñāviñ‍ñāṇasaṅkhayā, vaidanānaṃ niraudhā upasamā – aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, jānāmi sattānaṃ nimaukkhaṃ pamaukkhaṃ vivaika’’nti|

3. upanīyasuttaṃ

3. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘upanīyati jīvitamappamāyu,

jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

puñ‍ñāni kayirātha sukhāvahānī’’ti||

‘‘upanīyati jīvitamappamāyu,

jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

laukāmisaṃ pajahai santipaikkhau’’ti||

4. ac‍caintisuttaṃ

4. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘ac‍cainti kālā tarayanti rattiyau,

vayauguṇā anupubbaṃ jahanti|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

puñ‍ñāni kayirātha sukhāvahānī’’ti||

‘‘ac‍cainti kālā tarayanti rattiyau,

vayauguṇā anupubbaṃ jahanti|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

laukāmisaṃ pajahai santipaikkhau’’ti||

5. katichindasuttaṃ

5. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘kati chindai kati jahai, kati cuttari bhāvayai|

kati saṅgātigau bhikkhu, aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘pañ‍ca chindai pañ‍ca jahai, pañ‍ca cuttari bhāvayai|

pañ‍ca saṅgātigau bhikkhu, aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍catī’’ti||

6. jāgarasuttaṃ

6. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘kati jāgarataṃ suttā, kati suttaisu jāgarā|

katibhi katīhi (sī॰) rajamādaiti, katibhi katīhi (sī॰) parisujjhatī’’ti||

‘‘pañ‍ca jāgarataṃ suttā, pañ‍ca suttaisu jāgarā|

pañ‍cabhi pañ‍cahi (sī॰) rajamādaiti, pañ‍cabhi pañ‍cahi (sī॰) parisujjhatī’’ti||

7. appaṭividitasuttaṃ

7. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘yaisaṃ dhammā appaṭividitā, paravādaisu nīyarai niyyarai (ka॰)|

suttā tai nappabujjhanti, kālau taisaṃ pabujjhitu’’nti||

‘‘yaisaṃ dhammā suppaṭividitā, paravādaisu na nīyarai|

tai sambuddhā sammadañ‍ñā, caranti visamai sama’’nti||

8. susammuṭṭhasuttaṃ

8. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘yaisaṃ  dhammā susammuṭṭhā, paravādaisu nīyarai|

suttā tai nappabujjhanti, kālau taisaṃ pabujjhitu’’nti||

‘‘yaisaṃ  dhammā asammuṭṭhā, paravādaisu na nīyarai|

tai sambuddhā sammadañ‍ñā, caranti visamai sama’’nti||

9. mānakāmasuttaṃ

9. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘na mānakāmassa damau idhatthi,

na maunamatthi asamāhitassa|

aikau arañ‍ñai viharaṃ pamattau,

na mac‍cudhaiyyassa taraiyya pāra’’nti||

‘‘mānaṃ pahāya susamāhitattau,

sucaitasau sabbadhi vippamuttau|

aikau arañ‍ñai viharaṃ appamattau,

sa mac‍cudhaiyyassa taraiyya pāra’’nti||

10. arañ‍ñasuttaṃ

10. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘arañ‍ñai  viharantānaṃ, santānaṃ brahmacārinaṃ|

aikabhattaṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ, kaina vaṇṇau pasīdatī’’ti||

‘‘atītaṃ nānusaucanti, nappajappanti nāgataṃ|

pac‍cuppan‍naina yāpainti, taina vaṇṇau pasīdati’’||

‘‘anāgatappajappāya, atītassānusaucanā|

aitaina bālā sussanti, naḷauva haritau lutau’’ti||

naḷavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aughaṃ nimaukkhaṃ upanaiyyaṃ, ac‍cainti katichindi ca|

jāgaraṃ appaṭividitā, susammuṭṭhā mānakāminā|

arañ‍ñai dasamau vuttau, vaggau taina pavuc‍cati||

2. nandanavaggau

1. nandanasuttaṃ

11. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa  ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarā tāvatiṃsakāyikā daivatā nandanai vanai accharāsaṅghaparivutā dibbaihi pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitā samaṅgībhūtā paricāriyamānā paricāriyamānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘na  tai sukhaṃ pajānanti, yai na passanti nandanaṃ|

āvāsaṃ naradaivānaṃ, tidasānaṃ yasassina’’nti||

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarā daivatā taṃ daivataṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘na tvaṃ bālai pajānāsi, yathā arahataṃ vacau|

anic‍cā sabbasaṅkhārā sabbai saṅkhārā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau’’ti||

2. nandatisuttaṃ

12. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘nandati puttaihi puttimā,

gaumā gaumikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gauhi tathaiva nandati|

upadhīhi  narassa nandanā,

na hi sau nandati yau nirūpadhī’’ti||

‘‘saucati puttaihi puttimā,

gaumā gauhi tathaiva saucati|

upadhīhi narassa saucanā,

na hi sau saucati yau nirūpadhī’’ti||

3. natthiputtasamasuttaṃ

13. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘natthi puttasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi gausamitaṃ dhanaṃ|

natthi sūriyasamā suriyasamā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ābhā, samuddaparamā sarā’’ti||

‘‘natthi attasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi dhañ‍ñasamaṃ dhanaṃ|

natthi pañ‍ñāsamā ābhā, vuṭṭhi vai paramā sarā’’ti||

4. khattiyasuttaṃ

14. ‘‘khattiyau  dvipadaṃ saiṭṭhau, balībaddau balivaddau (sī॰ pī॰), balibaddau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) catuppadaṃ|

kaumārī saiṭṭhā bhariyānaṃ, yau ca puttāna pubbajau’’ti||

‘‘sambuddhau dvipadaṃ saiṭṭhau, ājānīyau catuppadaṃ|

sussūsā saiṭṭhā bhariyānaṃ, yau ca puttānamassavau’’ti||

5. saṇamānasuttaṃ

15. ‘‘ṭhitai  majjhanhikai majjhantikai (sabbattha) kālai, san‍nisīvaisu pakkhisu|

saṇataiva brahārañ‍ñaṃ mahārañ‍ñaṃ (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), taṃ bhayaṃ paṭibhāti ma’’nti||

‘‘ṭhitai majjhanhikai kālai, san‍nisīvaisu pakkhisu|

saṇataiva brahārañ‍ñaṃ, sā rati paṭibhāti ma’’nti||

6. niddātandīsuttaṃ

16. ‘‘niddā  tandī vijambhitā tandi vijambhikā (sī॰ pī॰), aratī bhattasammadau|

aitaina nappakāsati, ariyamaggau idha pāṇina’’nti||

‘‘niddaṃ tandiṃ vijambhitaṃ, aratiṃ bhattasammadaṃ|

vīriyaina viriyaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) naṃ paṇāmaitvā, ariyamaggau visujjhatī’’ti||

7. duk‍karasuttaṃ

17. ‘‘duk‍karaṃ  duttitikkhañ‍ca, abyattaina ca sāmañ‍ñaṃ|

bahūhi tattha sambādhā, yattha bālau visīdatī’’ti||

‘‘katihaṃ caraiyya sāmañ‍ñaṃ, cittaṃ cai na nivārayai|

padai padai visīdaiyya, saṅkappānaṃ vasānugau’’ti||

‘‘kummauva aṅgāni sakai kapālai,

samaudahaṃ bhikkhu manauvitak‍kai|

anissitau añ‍ñamahaiṭhayānau,

parinibbutau nūpavadaiyya kañ‍cī’’ti||

8. hirīsuttaṃ

18. ‘‘hirīnisaidhau  purisau, kauci laukasmiṃ vij‍jati|

yau nindaṃ apabaudhati apabaudhaiti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), assau bhadrau kasāmivā’’ti||

‘‘hirīnisaidhā tanuyā, yai caranti sadā satā|

antaṃ dukkhassa pappuyya, caranti visamai sama’’nti||

9. kuṭikāsuttaṃ

19.

 ‘‘kac‍ci  tai kuṭikā natthi, kac‍ci natthi kulāvakā|

kac‍ci santānakā natthi, kac‍ci muttausi bandhanā’’ti||

‘‘taggha mai kuṭikā natthi, taggha natthi kulāvakā|

taggha santānakā natthi, taggha muttaumhi bandhanā’’ti||

‘‘kintāhaṃ  kuṭikaṃ brūmi, kiṃ tai brūmi kulāvakaṃ|

kiṃ tai santānakaṃ brūmi, kintāhaṃ brūmi bandhana’’nti||

‘‘mātaraṃ kuṭikaṃ brūsi, bhariyaṃ brūsi kulāvakaṃ|

puttai santānakai brūsi, taṇhaṃ mai brūsi bandhana’’nti||

‘‘sāhu tai kuṭikā natthi, sāhu natthi kulāvakā|

sāhu santānakā natthi, sāhu muttausi bandhanā’’ti||

10. samiddhisuttaṃ

20. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati tapaudārāmai| atha khau āyasmā samiddhi rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya yaina tapaudā tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| tapaudai gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamānau| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ tapaudaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina āyasmā samiddhi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vaihāsaṃ ṭhitā āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘abhutvā  bhikkhasi bhikkhu, na hi bhutvāna bhikkhasi|

bhutvāna bhikkhu bhikkhassu, mā taṃ kālau upac‍cagā’’ti||

‘‘kālaṃ  vauhaṃ na jānāmi, chan‍nau kālau na dissati|

tasmā abhutvā bhikkhāmi, mā maṃ kālau upac‍cagā’’ti||

atha khau sā daivatā pathaviyaṃ paṭhaviyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) patiṭṭhahitvā āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daharau tvaṃ bhikkhu, pabbajitau susu kāḷakaisau, bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau, paṭhamaina vayasā, anik‍kīḷitāvī kāmaisu| bhuñ‍ja, bhikkhu, mānusakai kāmai; mā sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvī’’ti|

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvāmi| kālikañ‍ca khvāhaṃ, āvusau, hitvā sandiṭṭhikaṃ anudhāvāmi| kālikā hi, āvusau, kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā; ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| sandiṭṭhikau ayaṃ dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, kālikā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau? kathaṃ sandiṭṭhikau ayaṃ dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti?

‘‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, navau acirapabbajitau adhunāgatau imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ| na tāhaṃ na khvāhaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) sak‍kaumi vitthāraina ācikkhituṃ| ayaṃ sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau rājagahai viharati tapaudārāmai| taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ puccha| yathā tai bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāsī’’ti|

‘‘na khau, bhikkhu, sukarau sau bhagavā amhaihi upasaṅkamituṃ, añ‍ñāhi mahaisakkhāhi daivatāhi parivutau| sacai khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyāsi, mayampi āgacchaiyyāma dhammassavanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā samiddhi tassā daivatāya paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau āyasmā samiddhi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya yaina tapaudā tainupasaṅkamiṃ gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| tapaudai gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsiṃ gattāni pubbāpayamānau| atha khau, bhantai, añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ tapaudaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vaihāsaṃ ṭhitā imāya gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘abhutvā bhikkhasi bhikkhu, na hi bhutvāna bhikkhasi|

bhutvāna bhikkhu bhikkhassu, mā taṃ kālau upac‍cagā’’ti||

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhantai, taṃ daivataṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsiṃ –

‘‘kālaṃ vauhaṃ na jānāmi, chan‍nau kālau na dissati|

tasmā abhutvā bhikkhāmi, mā maṃ kālau upac‍cagā’’ti||

‘‘atha khau, bhantai, sā daivatā pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhahitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘daharau  tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pabbajitau susu kāḷakaisau, bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau, paṭhamaina vayasā, anik‍kīḷitāvī kāmaisu| bhuñ‍ja, bhikkhu, mānusakai kāmai; mā sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvī’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, taṃ daivataṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvāmi; kālikañ‍ca khvāhaṃ, āvusau, hitvā sandiṭṭhikaṃ anudhāvāmi| kālikā hi, āvusau, kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā; ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| sandiṭṭhikau ayaṃ dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhu, kālikā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā; ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau? kathaṃ sandiṭṭhikau  ayaṃ dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti? aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, taṃ daivataṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau, āvusau, navau acirapabbajitau adhunāgatau imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, na tāhaṃ sak‍kaumi vitthāraina ācikkhituṃ| ayaṃ sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau rājagahai viharati tapaudārāmai| taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ puccha| yathā tai bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāsī’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhantai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘na khau, bhikkhu, sukarau sau bhagavā amhaihi upasaṅkamituṃ, añ‍ñāhi mahaisakkhāhi daivatāhi parivutau| sacai khau, tvaṃ bhikkhu, taṃ bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyāsi, mayampi āgacchaiyyāma  dhammassavanāyā’ti| sacai, bhantai, tassā daivatāya sac‍caṃ vacanaṃ, idhaiva sā daivatā avidūrai’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, sā daivatā āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘puccha, bhikkhu, puccha, bhikkhu, yamahaṃ anuppattā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā taṃ daivataṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘akkhaiyyasañ‍ñinau sattā, akkhaiyyasmiṃ patiṭṭhitā|

akkhaiyyaṃ apariñ‍ñāya, yaugamāyanti mac‍cunau||

‘‘akkhaiyyañ‍ca pariñ‍ñāya, akkhātāraṃ na mañ‍ñati|

tañhi tassa na hautīti, yaina naṃ vaj‍jā na tassa atthi|

sacai vijānāsi vadaihi yakkhā’’ti yakkhīti (pī॰ ka॰)||

‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhantai, imassa bhagavatā saṅkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tathā bhāsatu yathāhaṃ imassa bhagavatā saṅkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ jānaiyya’’nti|

‘‘samau  visaisī uda vā athavā (sī॰ pī॰) nihīnau,

yau mañ‍ñatī sau vivadaitha saupi vadaitha (ka॰) taina|

tīsu  vidhāsu avikampamānau,

samau visaisīti na tassa hauti|

sacai vijānāsi vadaihi yakkhā’’ti||

‘‘imassāpi  khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṅkhittaina bhāsitassa na vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā tathā bhāsatu yathāhaṃ imassa bhagavatā saṅkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ jānaiyya’’nti|

‘‘pahāsi saṅkhaṃ na vimānamajjhagā, acchaicchi acchaij‍ji (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) taṇhaṃ idha nāmarūpai|

taṃ chin‍naganthaṃ anighaṃ nirāsaṃ, pariyaisamānā nājjhagamuṃ|

daivā manussā idha vā huraṃ vā, saggaisu vā sabbanivaisanaisu|

sacai vijānāsi vadaihi yakkhā’’ti||

‘‘imassa  khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṅkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi –

‘‘pāpaṃ na kayirā vacasā manasā,

kāyaina vā kiñ‍cana sabbalaukai|

kāmai pahāya satimā sampajānau,

dukkhaṃ na saivaitha anatthasaṃhita’’nti||

nandanavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nandanā  nandati caiva, natthiputtasamaina ca|

khattiyau saṇamānau ca, niddātandī ca duk‍karaṃ|

hirī kuṭikā navamau, dasamau vuttau samiddhināti||

3. sattivaggau

1. sattisuttaṃ

21. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva ḍayhamānaiva (sabbattha) matthakai|

kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva matthakai|

sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

2. phusatisuttaṃ

22.

 ‘‘nāphusantaṃ phusati ca, phusantañ‍ca tatau phusai|

tasmā phusantaṃ phusati, appaduṭṭhapadausina’’nti||

‘‘yau  appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati,

suddhassa pausassa anaṅgaṇassa|

tamaiva bālaṃ pac‍caiti pāpaṃ,

sukhumau rajau paṭivātaṃva khittau’’ti||

3. jaṭāsuttaṃ

23.

 ‘‘antau  jaṭā bahi jaṭā, jaṭāya jaṭitā pajā|

taṃ taṃ gautama pucchāmi, kau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭa’’nti||

‘‘sīlai  patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

ātāpī nipakau bhikkhu, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭaṃ||

‘‘yaisaṃ rāgau ca dausau ca, avij‍jā ca virājitā|

khīṇāsavā arahantau, taisaṃ vijaṭitā jaṭā||

‘‘yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

paṭighaṃ rūpasañ‍ñā ca, aitthaisā chij‍jatai vijaṭai (ka॰) jaṭā’’ti||

4. manaunivāraṇasuttaṃ

24. ‘‘yatau  yatau manau nivārayai,

na dukkhamaiti naṃ tatau tatau|

sa sabbatau manau nivārayai,

sa sabbatau dukkhā pamuc‍cati’’||

‘‘na sabbatau manau nivārayai,

na manau saṃyatattamāgataṃ|

yatau yatau ca pāpakaṃ,

tatau tatau manau nivārayai’’ti||

5. arahantasuttaṃ

25.

 ‘‘yau hauti bhikkhu arahaṃ katāvī,

khīṇāsavau antimadaihadhārī|

ahaṃ  vadāmītipi sau vadaiyya,

mamaṃ vadantītipi sau vadaiyyā’’ti||

‘‘yau  hauti bhikkhu arahaṃ katāvī,

khīṇāsavau antimadaihadhārī|

ahaṃ vadāmītipi sau vadaiyya,

mamaṃ vadantītipi sau vadaiyya|

laukai samañ‍ñaṃ kusalau viditvā,

vauhāramattaina sau sa (?) vauharaiyyā’’ti||

‘‘yau hauti bhikkhu arahaṃ katāvī,

khīṇāsavau antimadaihadhārī|

mānaṃ nu khau sau upagamma bhikkhu,

ahaṃ vadāmītipi sau vadaiyya|

mamaṃ vadantītipi sau vadaiyyā’’ti||

‘‘pahīnamānassa na santi ganthā,

vidhūpitā mānaganthassa sabbai|

sa vītivattau mañ‍ñataṃ mānanaṃ (sī॰), mañ‍ñītaṃ (?) sumaidhau,

ahaṃ  vadāmītipi sau vadaiyya||

‘‘mamaṃ vadantītipi sau vadaiyya|

laukai samañ‍ñaṃ kusalau viditvā|

vauhāramattaina sau vauharaiyyā’’ti||

6. paj‍jautasuttaṃ

26.

 ‘‘kati  laukasmiṃ paj‍jautā, yaihi laukau pakāsati pabhāsati (ka॰ sī॰)|

bhagavantaṃ bhavantaṃ (ka॰) puṭṭhumāgamma, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ maya’’nti||

‘‘cattārau  laukai paj‍jautā, pañ‍camaittha na vij‍jati|

divā tapati ādic‍cau, rattimābhāti candimā||

‘‘atha aggi divārattiṃ, tattha tattha pakāsati|

sambuddhau tapataṃ saiṭṭhau, aisā ābhā anuttarā’’ti||

7. sarasuttaṃ

27.

 ‘‘kutau  sarā nivattanti, kattha vaṭṭaṃ na vattati|

kattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

‘‘yattha āpau ca pathavī, taijau vāyau na gādhati|

atau sarā nivattanti, aittha vaṭṭaṃ na vattati|

aittha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

8. mahaddhanasuttaṃ

28.

 ‘‘mahaddhanā mahābhaugā, raṭṭhavantaupi khattiyā|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñābhigijjhanti, kāmaisu analaṅkatā||

‘‘taisu ussuk‍kajātaisu, bhavasautānusārisu|

kaidha taṇhaṃ raudhataṇhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), gaidhataṇhaṃ (ka॰) pajahiṃsu pavāhiṃsu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), kai laukasmiṃ anussukā’’ti||

‘‘hitvā  agāraṃ pabbajitā, hitvā puttaṃ pasuṃ piyaṃ|

hitvā rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca, avij‍jañ‍ca virājiya|

khīṇāsavā arahantau, tai laukasmiṃ anussukā’’ti||

9. catucak‍kasuttaṃ

29.

 ‘‘catucak‍kaṃ  navadvāraṃ, puṇṇaṃ laubhaina saṃyutaṃ|

paṅkajātaṃ mahāvīra, kathaṃ yātrā bhavissatī’’ti||

‘‘chaitvā naddhiṃ varattañ‍ca, icchā laubhañ‍ca pāpakaṃ|

samūlaṃ taṇhamabbuyha, aivaṃ yātrā bhavissatī’’ti||

10. aiṇijaṅghasuttaṃ

30.

 ‘‘aiṇijaṅghaṃ kisaṃ vīraṃ, appāhāraṃ alaulupaṃ|

sīhaṃ vaikacaraṃ nāgaṃ, kāmaisu anapaikkhinaṃ|

upasaṅkamma pucchāma, kathaṃ dukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘pañ‍ca  kāmaguṇā laukai, manauchaṭṭhā pavaiditā|

aittha chandaṃ virājaitvā, aivaṃ dukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

sattivaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sattiyā phusati caiva, jaṭā manaunivāraṇā|

arahantaina paj‍jautau, sarā mahaddhanaina ca|

catucak‍kaina navamaṃ, aiṇijaṅghaina tai dasāti||

4. satul‍lapakāyikavaggau

1. sabbhisuttaṃ

31. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā satul‍lapakāyikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha krubbaitha (ka॰) santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, saiyyau hauti na pāpiyau’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, pañ‍ñā labbhati pañ‍ñaṃ labhati (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nāñ‍ñatau’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, saukamajjhai na saucatī’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva  samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, ñātimajjhai viraucatī’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, sattā gacchanti suggati’’nti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, sattā tiṭṭhanti sātata’’nti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa nu khau, bhagavā, subhāsita’’nti? sabbāsaṃ vau subhāsitaṃ pariyāyaina, api ca mamapi suṇātha –

‘‘sabbhiraiva  samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tā daivatāyau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsūti|

2. maccharisuttaṃ

32. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā satul‍lapakāyikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ  gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘macchairā  ca pamādā ca, aivaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati diyyati (ka॰)|

puñ‍ñaṃ ākaṅkhamānaina, daiyyaṃ hauti vijānatā’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘yassaiva bhītau na dadāti maccharī, tadaivādadatau bhayaṃ|

jighacchā ca pipāsā ca, yassa bhāyati maccharī|

tamaiva bālaṃ phusati, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca||

‘‘tasmā vinaiyya macchairaṃ, daj‍jā dānaṃ malābhibhū|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘tai mataisu na mīyanti, panthānaṃva sahabbajaṃ|

appasmiṃ yai pavaicchanti, aisa dhammau sanantanau||

‘‘appasmaikai pavaicchanti, bahunaikai na diccharai|

appasmā dakkhiṇā din‍nā, sahassaina samaṃ mitā’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘duddadaṃ dadamānānaṃ, duk‍karaṃ kamma kubbataṃ|

asantau nānukubbanti, sataṃ dhammau duranvayau duran‍nayau (sī॰)||

‘‘tasmā  satañ‍ca asataṃ asatañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), nānā hauti itau gati|

asantau nirayaṃ yanti, santau saggaparāyanā’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa nu khau, bhagavā, subhāsita’’nti?

‘‘sabbāsaṃ vau subhāsitaṃ pariyāyaina; api ca mamapi suṇātha –

‘‘dhammaṃ carai yaupi samuñ‍jakaṃ carai,

dārañ‍ca pausaṃ dadamappakasmiṃ|

sataṃ sahassānaṃ sahassayāginaṃ,

kalampi nāgghanti tathāvidhassa tai’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kainaisa yañ‍ñau vipulau mahaggatau,

samaina din‍nassa na agghamaiti|

kathaṃ idaṃ padaṃ katthaci sīhaḷapautthakai natthi sataṃ sahassānaṃ sahassayāginaṃ,

kalampi nāgghanti tathāvidhassa tai’’ti||

‘‘dadanti haikai visamai niviṭṭhā,

chaitvā vadhitvā atha saucayitvā|

sā  dakkhiṇā assumukhā sadaṇḍā,

samaina din‍nassa na agghamaiti||

‘‘aivaṃ sataṃ sahassānaṃ sahassayāginaṃ|

kalampi nāgghanti tathāvidhassa tai’’ti||

3. sādhusuttaṃ

33. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau sambahulā satul‍lapakāyikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā  abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa, dānaṃ|

macchairā ca pamādā ca, aivaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati|

puñ‍ñaṃ ākaṅkhamānaina, daiyyaṃ hauti vijānatā’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa, dānaṃ|

api ca appakasmimpi sāhu dānaṃ’’||

‘‘appasmaikai pavaicchanti, bahunaikai na diccharai|

appasmā dakkhiṇā din‍nā, sahassaina samaṃ mitā’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa, dānaṃ; appakasmimpi sāhu dānaṃ|

api ca saddhāyapi sāhu dānaṃ’’||

‘‘dānañ‍ca yuddhañ‍ca samānamāhu,

appāpi santā bahukai jinanti|

appampi cai saddahānau dadāti,

tainaiva sau hauti sukhī paratthā’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sādhu  khau, mārisa, dānaṃ; appakasmimpi sāhu dānaṃ|

saddhāyapi  sāhu dānaṃ; api ca dhammaladdhassāpi sāhu dānaṃ’’||

‘‘yau dhammaladdhassa dadāti dānaṃ,

uṭṭhānavīriyādhigatassa jantu|

atik‍kamma sau vaitaraṇiṃ yamassa,

dibbāni ṭhānāni upaiti mac‍cau’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa, dānaṃ; appakasmimpi sāhu dānaṃ|

saddhāyapi sāhu dānaṃ; dhammaladdhassāpi sāhu dānaṃ|

api  ca vicaiyya dānampi sāhu dānaṃ’’||

‘‘vicaiyya dānaṃ sugatappasatthaṃ,

yai dakkhiṇaiyyā idha jīvalaukai|

aitaisu din‍nāni mahapphalāni,

bījāni vuttāni yathā sukhaittai’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sādhu khau, mārisa, dānaṃ; appakasmimpi sāhu dānaṃ|

saddhāyapi sāhu dānaṃ; dhammaladdhassāpi sāhu dānaṃ|

vicaiyya dānampi sāhu dānaṃ; api ca pāṇaisupi sādhu saṃyamau’’||

‘‘yau  pāṇabhūtāni pāṇabhūtaisu (sī॰ pī॰) ahaiṭhayaṃ caraṃ,

parūpavādā na karaunti pāpaṃ|

bhīruṃ  pasaṃsanti na hi tattha sūraṃ,

bhayā hi santau na karaunti pāpa’’nti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa  nu khau, bhagavā, subhāsita’’nti?

‘‘sabbāsaṃ vau subhāsitaṃ pariyāyaina, api ca mamapi suṇātha –

‘‘saddhā hi dānaṃ bahudhā pasatthaṃ,

dānā ca khau dhammapadaṃva saiyyau|

pubbai  ca hi pubbatarai ca santau,

nibbānamaivajjhagamuṃ sapañ‍ñā’’ti||

4. nasantisuttaṃ

34. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā satul‍lapakāyikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘na santi kāmā manujaisu nic‍cā,

santīdha kamanīyāni yaisu kāmaisu (ka॰) baddhau|

yaisu pamattau apunāgamanaṃ,

anāgantā purisau mac‍cudhaiyyā’’ti||

‘‘chandajaṃ aghaṃ chandajaṃ dukkhaṃ|

chandavinayā aghavinayau|

aghavinayā dukkhavinayau’’ti||

‘‘na  tai kāmā yāni citrāni laukai,

saṅkapparāgau  purisassa kāmau|

tiṭṭhanti citrāni tathaiva laukai,

athaittha dhīrā vinayanti chandaṃ||

‘‘kaudhaṃ  jahai vippajahaiyya mānaṃ,

saṃyaujanaṃ sabbamatik‍kamaiyya|

taṃ nāmarūpasmimasaj‍jamānaṃ,

akiñ‍canaṃ nānupatanti dukkhā||

‘‘pahāsi saṅkhaṃ na vimānamajjhagā na ca mānamajjhagā (ka॰ sī॰), na vimānamāgā (syā॰ kaṃ॰),

acchaicchi taṇhaṃ idha nāmarūpai|

taṃ chin‍naganthaṃ anighaṃ nirāsaṃ,

pariyaisamānā nājjhagamuṃ|

daivā manussā idha vā huraṃ vā,

saggaisu vā sabbanivaisanaisū’’ti||

‘‘taṃ cai hi nāddakkhuṃ tathāvimuttaṃ (ic‍cāyasmā maugharājā),

daivā manussā idha vā huraṃ vā|

naruttamaṃ atthacaraṃ narānaṃ,

yai taṃ namassanti pasaṃsiyā tai’’ti||

‘‘pasaṃsiyā taipi bhavanti bhikkhū (maugharājāti bhagavā),

yai taṃ namassanti tathāvimuttaṃ|

añ‍ñāya  dhammaṃ vicikicchaṃ pahāya,

saṅgātigā taipi bhavanti bhikkhū’’ti||

5. ujjhānasañ‍ñisuttaṃ

35. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā ujjhānasañ‍ñikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā vaihāsaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| vaihāsaṃ  ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘añ‍ñathā santamattānaṃ, añ‍ñathā yau pavaidayai|

nikac‍ca kitavassaiva, bhuttaṃ thaiyyaina tassa taṃ||

‘‘yañhi kayirā tañhi vadai, yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vadai|

akarauntaṃ bhāsamānānaṃ, parijānanti paṇḍitā’’ti||

‘‘na yidaṃ bhāsitamattaina, aikantasavanaina vā|

anuk‍kamitavai sak‍kā, yāyaṃ paṭipadā daḷhā|

yāya dhīrā pamuc‍canti, jhāyinau mārabandhanā||

‘‘na  vai dhīrā pakubbanti, viditvā laukapariyāyaṃ|

añ‍ñāya nibbutā dhīrā, tiṇṇā laukai visattika’’nti||

atha  khau tā daivatāyau pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhahitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ac‍cayau nau, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ yathābālā yathāmūḷhā yathāakusalā (sabbattha), yā mayaṃ bhagavantaṃ āsādaitabbaṃ amañ‍ñimhā| tāsaṃ nau, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhātu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā sitaṃ pātvākāsi| atha khau tā daivatāyau bhiyyausaumattāya ujjhāyantiyau vaihāsaṃ abbhuggañchuṃ| aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘ac‍cayaṃ  daisayantīnaṃ, yau cai na paṭigaṇhati|

kaupantarau dausagaru, sa vairaṃ paṭimuñ‍catī’’ti||

‘‘ac‍cayau cai na vij‍jaitha, naucidhāpagataṃ naucīdha apahataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), naucidhāpakataṃ (?) siyā|

vairāni na ca sammaiyyuṃ, kainīdha vairāni ca sammaiyyuṃ, tainidha (sī॰) kusalau siyā’’ti||

‘‘kassac‍cayā na vij‍janti, kassa natthi apāgataṃ|

kau na sammauhamāpādi, kau ca dhīrau kaudha dhīrau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sadā satau’’ti||

‘‘tathāgatassa  buddhassa, sabbabhūtānukampinau|

tassac‍cayā na vij‍janti, tassa natthi apāgataṃ|

sau na sammauhamāpādi, sauva saudha (syā॰ kaṃ॰) dhīrau sadā satau’’ti||

‘‘ac‍cayaṃ daisayantīnaṃ, yau cai na paṭigaṇhati|

kaupantarau  dausagaru, sa vairaṃ paṭimuñ‍cati|

taṃ vairaṃ nābhinandāmi, paṭiggaṇhāmi vauc‍caya’’nti||

6. saddhāsuttaṃ

36. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sambahulā satul‍lapakāyikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ  aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘saddhā dutiyā purisassa hauti,

nau cai assaddhiyaṃ avatiṭṭhati|

yasau  ca kittī ca tatvassa hauti,

saggañ‍ca sau gacchati sarīraṃ vihāyā’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘kaudhaṃ jahai vippajahaiyya mānaṃ,

saṃyaujanaṃ sabbamatik‍kamaiyya|

taṃ nāmarūpasmimasaj‍jamānaṃ,

akiñ‍canaṃ nānupatanti saṅgā’’ti||

‘‘pamādamanuyuñ‍janti, bālā dummaidhinau janā|

appamādañ‍ca maidhāvī, dhanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃva rakkhati||

‘‘mā pamādamanuyuñ‍jaitha, mā kāmarati santhavaṃ|

appamattau hi jhāyantau, pappauti paramaṃ sukha’’nti||

7. samayasuttaṃ

37. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ mahāvanai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sabbaihaiva arahantaihi; dasahi ca laukadhātūhi daivatā yaibhuyyaina san‍nipatitā haunti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| atha khau catun‍naṃ suddhāvāsakāyikānaṃ daivatānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ mahāvanai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sabbaihaiva arahantaihi; dasahi ca laukadhātūhi daivatā yaibhuyyaina san‍nipatitā haunti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| yaṃnūna mayampi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau santikai pac‍caikaṃ gāthaṃ pac‍caikagāthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhāsaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha  khau tā daivatā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya pasāritaṃ  vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya| aivamaiva – suddhāvāsaisu daivaisu antarahitā bhagavatau puratau pāturahaisuṃ| atha khau tā daivatā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ  gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘mahāsamayau pavanasmiṃ, daivakāyā samāgatā|

āgatamha imaṃ dhammasamayaṃ, dakkhitāyai aparājitasaṅgha’’nti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘tatra bhikkhavau samādahaṃsu, cittamattanau ujukaṃ akaṃsu ujukamakaṃsu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

sārathīva naittāni gahaitvā, indriyāni rakkhanti paṇḍitā’’ti||

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘chaitvā khīlaṃ chaitvā palighaṃ, indakhīlaṃ ūhac‍ca manaijā|

tai caranti suddhā vimalā, cakkhumatā sudantā susunāgā’’ti||

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘yai kaici buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatāsai, na tai gamissanti apāyabhūmiṃ|

pahāya mānusaṃ daihaṃ, daivakāyaṃ paripūraissantī’’ti||

8. sakalikasuttaṃ

38. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati maddakucchismiṃ  migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatau pādau sakalikāya sakkhalikāya (ka॰) khatau hauti| bhusā sudaṃ bhagavatau vaidanā vattanti sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā tippā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā; tā sudaṃ bhagavā satau sampajānau  adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau| atha khau bhagavā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaiti pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau|

atha  khau sattasatā satul‍lapakāyikā daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ maddakucchiṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘nāgau  vata, bhau, samaṇau gautamau; nāgavatā ca samuppan‍nā sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau’’ti|

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘sīhau vata, bhau, samaṇau gautamau; sīhavatā ca samuppan‍nā sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau’’ti|

atha  khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘ājānīyau vata, bhau, samaṇau gautamau; ājānīyavatā ca samuppan‍nā sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau’’ti|

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘nisabhau vata, bhau, samaṇau gautamau; nisabhavatā ca samuppan‍nā sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau’’ti|

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘dhaurayhau vata, bhau, samaṇau gautamau; dhaurayhavatā ca samuppan‍nā sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau’’ti|

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘dantau vata, bhau, samaṇau  gautamau; dantavatā ca samuppan‍nā sārīrikā vaidanā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau’’ti|

atha khau aparā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘passa samādhiṃ subhāvitaṃ cittañ‍ca suvimuttaṃ, na cābhinataṃ na  cāpanataṃ na  ca sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagataṃ sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritavataṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sasaṅkhāraniggayhavārivāvataṃ (ka॰)| yau aivarūpaṃ purisanāgaṃ purisasīhaṃ purisaājānīyaṃ  purisanisabhaṃ purisadhaurayhaṃ purisadantaṃ atik‍kamitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya kimañ‍ñatra adassanā’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍cavaidā sataṃ samaṃ, tapassī brāhmaṇā caraṃ|

cittañ‍ca naisaṃ na sammā vimuttaṃ, hīnattharūpā na pāraṅgamā tai||

‘‘taṇhādhipan‍nā vatasīlabaddhā, lūkhaṃ tapaṃ vassasataṃ carantā|

cittañ‍ca naisaṃ na sammā vimuttaṃ, hīnattharūpā na pāraṅgamā tai||

‘‘na mānakāmassa damau idhatthi, na maunamatthi asamāhitassa|

aikau arañ‍ñai viharaṃ pamattau, na mac‍cudhaiyyassa taraiyya pāra’’nti||

‘‘mānaṃ pahāya susamāhitattau, sucaitasau sabbadhi vippamuttau|

aikau arañ‍ñai viharamappamattau, sa mac‍cudhaiyyassa taraiyya pāra’’nti||

9. paṭhamapaj‍jun‍nadhītusuttaṃ

39. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau kaukanadā paj‍jun‍nassa dhītā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ mahāvanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā kaukanadā paj‍jun‍nassa dhītā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘vaisāliyaṃ  vanai viharantaṃ, aggaṃ sattassa sambuddhaṃ|

kaukanadāhamasmi  abhivandai, kaukanadā paj‍jun‍nassa dhītā||

‘‘sutamaiva purai āsi, dhammau cakkhumatānubuddhau|

sāhaṃ dāni sakkhi jānāmi, muninau daisayatau sugatassa||

‘‘yai kaici ariyaṃ dhammaṃ, vigarahantā caranti dummaidhā|

upainti rauruvaṃ ghauraṃ, cirarattaṃ dukkhaṃ anubhavanti||

‘‘yai ca khau ariyai dhammai, khantiyā upasamaina upaitā|

pahāya mānusaṃ daihaṃ, daivakāya paripūraissantī’’ti||

10. dutiyapaj‍jun‍nadhītusuttaṃ

40. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau cūḷakaukanadā cul‍lakaukanadā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) paj‍jun‍nassa dhītā  abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ mahāvanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā cūḷakaukanadā paj‍jun‍nassa dhītā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘idhāgamā vij‍jupabhāsavaṇṇā, kaukanadā paj‍jun‍nassa dhītā|

buddhañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca namassamānā, gāthācimā atthavatī abhāsi||

‘‘bahunāpi  khau taṃ vibhajaiyyaṃ, pariyāyaina tādisau dhammau|

saṃkhittamatthaṃ saṃkhittamattaṃ (ka॰) lapayissāmi, yāvatā mai manasā pariyattaṃ||

‘‘pāpaṃ na kayirā vacasā manasā,

kāyaina vā kiñ‍cana sabbalaukai|

kāmai  pahāya satimā sampajānau,

dukkhaṃ na saivaitha anatthasaṃhita’’nti||

satul‍lapakāyikavaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sabbhimaccharinā sādhu, na santujjhānasañ‍ñinau|

saddhā samayau sakalikaṃ, ubhau paj‍jun‍nadhītarauti||

5. ādittavaggau

1. ādittasuttaṃ

41. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya  rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘ādittasmiṃ agārasmiṃ, yaṃ nīharati bhājanaṃ|

taṃ tassa hauti atthāya, nau ca yaṃ tattha ḍayhati||

‘‘aivaṃ ādittakau laukau, jarāya maraṇaina ca|

nīharaithaiva dānaina, din‍naṃ hauti sunīhataṃ||

‘‘din‍naṃ  sukhaphalaṃ hauti, nādin‍naṃ hauti taṃ tathā|

caurā haranti rājānau, aggi ḍahati nassati||

‘‘atha  antaina jahati, sarīraṃ sapariggahaṃ|

aitadañ‍ñāya maidhāvī, bhuñ‍jaitha ca dadaitha ca|

datvā ca bhutvā ca yathānubhāvaṃ|

aninditau saggamupaiti ṭhāna’’nti||

2. kiṃdadasuttaṃ

42.

 ‘‘kiṃdadau baladau hauti, kiṃdadau hauti vaṇṇadau|

kiṃdadau sukhadau hauti, kiṃdadau hauti cakkhudau|

kau ca sabbadadau hauti, taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitau’’ti||

‘‘an‍nadau baladau hauti, vatthadau hauti vaṇṇadau|

yānadau sukhadau hauti, dīpadau hauti cakkhudau||

‘‘sau ca sabbadadau hauti, yau dadāti upassayaṃ|

amataṃ dadau ca sau hauti, yau dhammamanusāsatī’’ti||

3. an‍nasuttaṃ

43.

 ‘‘an‍namaivābhinandanti, ubhayai daivamānusā|

atha kau nāma sau yakkhau, yaṃ an‍naṃ nābhinandatī’’ti||

‘‘yai  naṃ dadanti saddhāya, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

tamaiva an‍naṃ bhajati, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca||

‘‘tasmā  vinaiyya macchairaṃ, daj‍jā dānaṃ malābhibhū|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti||

4. aikamūlasuttaṃ

44.

 ‘‘aikamūlaṃ dvirāvaṭṭaṃ, timalaṃ pañ‍capattharaṃ|

samuddaṃ dvādasāvaṭṭaṃ, pātālaṃ atarī isī’’ti||

5. anaumasuttaṃ

45.

 ‘‘anaumanāmaṃ  nipuṇatthadassiṃ, pañ‍ñādadaṃ kāmālayai asattaṃ|

taṃ passatha sabbaviduṃ sumaidhaṃ, ariyai pathai kamamānaṃ mahaisi’’nti||

6. accharāsuttaṃ

46.

 ‘‘accharāgaṇasaṅghuṭṭhaṃ, pisācagaṇasaivitaṃ|

vanantaṃ mauhanaṃ nāma, kathaṃ yātrā bhavissatī’’ti||

‘‘ujukau nāma sau maggau, abhayā nāma sā disā|

rathau akūjanau nāma, dhammacak‍kaihi saṃyutau||

‘‘hirī tassa apālambau, satyassa parivāraṇaṃ|

dhammāhaṃ sārathiṃ brūmi, sammādiṭṭhipuraijavaṃ||

‘‘yassa aitādisaṃ yānaṃ, itthiyā purisassa vā|

sa vai aitaina yānaina, nibbānassaiva santikai’’ti||

7. vanaraupasuttaṃ

47.

 ‘‘kaisaṃ  divā ca rattau ca, sadā puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

dhammaṭṭhā sīlasampan‍nā, kai janā saggagāminau’’ti||

‘‘ārāmaraupā vanaraupā, yai janā saitukārakā|

papañ‍ca udapānañ‍ca, yai dadanti upassayaṃ||

‘‘taisaṃ  divā ca rattau ca, sadā puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati|

dhammaṭṭhā sīlasampan‍nā, tai janā saggagāminau’’ti||

8. jaitavanasuttaṃ

48.

 ‘‘idañhi taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ āvuṭṭhaṃ (ka॰) dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama||

‘‘kammaṃ  vij‍jā ca dhammau ca, sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamaṃ|

aitaina mac‍cā sujjhanti, na gauttaina dhanaina vā||

‘‘tasmā hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau|

yaunisau vicinai dhammaṃ, aivaṃ tattha visujjhati||

‘‘sāriputtauva pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina ca|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

9. maccharisuttaṃ

49.

 ‘‘yaidha  maccharinau laukai, kadariyā paribhāsakā|

añ‍ñaisaṃ dadamānānaṃ, antarāyakarā narā||

‘‘kīdisau  taisaṃ vipākau, samparāyau ca kīdisau|

bhagavantaṃ puṭṭhumāgamma, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ maya’’nti||

‘‘yaidha maccharinau laukai, kadariyā paribhāsakā|

añ‍ñaisaṃ dadamānānaṃ, antarāyakarā narā||

‘‘nirayaṃ tiracchānayauniṃ, yamalaukaṃ upapaj‍jarai|

sacai ainti manussattaṃ, daliddai jāyarai kulai||

‘‘cauḷaṃ piṇḍau ratī khiḍḍā, yattha kicchaina labbhati|

paratau āsīsarai āsiṃsarai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bālā, tampi taisaṃ na labbhati|

diṭṭhai dhammaisa vipākau, samparāyai samparāyau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca duggatī’’ti||

‘‘itihaitaṃ vijānāma, añ‍ñaṃ pucchāma gautama|

yaidha laddhā manussattaṃ, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā||

‘‘buddhai  pasan‍nā dhammai ca, saṅghai ca tibbagāravā|

kīdisau taisaṃ vipākau, samparāyau ca kīdisau|

bhagavantaṃ puṭṭhumāgamma, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ maya’’nti||

‘‘yaidha laddhā manussattaṃ, vadañ‍ñū vītamaccharā|

buddhai pasan‍nā dhammai ca, saṅghai ca tibbagāravā|

aitai saggā saggai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) pakāsanti, yattha tai upapaj‍jarai||

‘‘sacai  ainti manussattaṃ, aḍḍhai ājāyarai kulai|

cauḷaṃ piṇḍau ratī khiḍḍā, yatthākicchaina labbhati||

‘‘parasambhataisu  bhaugaisu, vasavattīva maudarai|

diṭṭhai dhammaisa vipākau, samparāyai ca suggatī’’ti||

10. ghaṭīkārasuttaṃ

50.

 ‘‘avihaṃ upapan‍nāsai, vimuttā satta bhikkhavau|

rāgadausaparikkhīṇā, tiṇṇā laukai visattika’’nti||

‘‘kai ca tai ataruṃ paṅkaṃ saṅgaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ|

kai hitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, dibbayaugaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti||

‘‘upakau palagaṇḍau ca, puk‍kusāti ca tai tayau|

bhaddiyau khaṇḍadaivau ca, bāhuraggi ca siṅgiyau bahudantī ca piṅgayau (sī॰)|

tai hitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, dibbayaugaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti||

‘‘kusalī bhāsasī taisaṃ, mārapāsappahāyinaṃ|

kassa tai dhammamañ‍ñāya, acchiduṃ bhavabandhana’’nti||

‘‘na añ‍ñatra bhagavatā, nāñ‍ñatra tava sāsanā|

yassa tai dhammamañ‍ñāya, acchiduṃ bhavabandhanaṃ||

‘‘yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

taṃ tai dhammaṃ idhañ‍ñāya, acchiduṃ bhavabandhana’’nti||

‘‘gambhīraṃ  bhāsasī vācaṃ, dubbijānaṃ sudubbudhaṃ|

kassa tvaṃ  dhammamañ‍ñāya, vācaṃ bhāsasi īdisa’’nti||

‘‘kumbhakārau  purai āsiṃ, vaikaḷiṅgai vaihaḷiṅgai (sī॰), vaibhaḷiṅgai (syā॰ kaṃ॰) ghaṭīkarau|

mātāpaittibharau āsiṃ, kassapassa upāsakau||

‘‘viratau  maithunā dhammā, brahmacārī nirāmisau|

ahuvā tai sagāmaiyyau, ahuvā tai purai sakhā||

‘‘sauhamaitai pajānāmi, vimuttai satta bhikkhavau|

rāgadausaparikkhīṇai, tiṇṇai laukai visattika’’nti||

‘‘aivamaitaṃ tadā āsi, yathā bhāsasi bhaggava|

kumbhakārau purai āsi, vaikaḷiṅgai ghaṭīkarau|

mātāpaittibharau āsi, kassapassa upāsakau||

‘‘viratau maithunā dhammā, brahmacārī nirāmisau|

ahuvā mai sagāmaiyyau, ahuvā mai purai sakhā’’ti||

‘‘aivamaitaṃ purāṇānaṃ, sahāyānaṃ ahu saṅgamau|

ubhin‍naṃ bhāvitattānaṃ, sarīrantimadhārina’’nti||

ādittavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ādittaṃ kiṃdadaṃ an‍naṃ, aikamūlaanaumiyaṃ|

accharāvanaraupajaitaṃ, maccharaina ghaṭīkarauti||

6. jarāvaggau

1. jarāsuttaṃ

51.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  yāva jarā sādhu, kiṃsu sādhu patiṭṭhitaṃ|

kiṃsu narānaṃ ratanaṃ, kiṃsu cauraihi dūhara’’nti||

‘‘sīlaṃ yāva jarā sādhu, saddhā sādhu patiṭṭhitā|

pañ‍ñā narānaṃ ratanaṃ, puñ‍ñaṃ cauraihi dūhara’’nti||

2. ajarasāsuttaṃ

52.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  ajarasā sādhu, kiṃsu sādhu adhiṭṭhitaṃ|

kiṃsu narānaṃ ratanaṃ, kiṃsu cauraihyahāriya’’nti||

‘‘sīlaṃ  ajarasā sādhu, saddhā sādhu adhiṭṭhitā|

pañ‍ñā narānaṃ ratanaṃ, puñ‍ñaṃ cauraihyahāriya’’nti||

3. mittasuttaṃ

53.

 ‘‘kiṃsu pavasatau pathavatau (pī॰ ka॰) mittaṃ, kiṃsu mittaṃ sakai gharai|

kiṃ mittaṃ atthajātassa, kiṃ mittaṃ samparāyika’’nti||

‘‘satthau pavasatau mittaṃ, mātā mittaṃ sakai gharai|

sahāyau  atthajātassa, hauti mittaṃ punappunaṃ|

sayaṃkatāni puñ‍ñāni, taṃ mittaṃ samparāyika’’nti||

4. vatthusuttaṃ

54.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  vatthu manussānaṃ, kiṃsūdha paramau sakhā|

kiṃsu bhūtā upajīvanti, yai pāṇā pathavissitā’’ti pathaviṃ sitāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘puttā vatthu manussānaṃ, bhariyā ca bhariyāva (sī॰), bhariyā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) paramau sakhā|

vuṭṭhiṃ bhūtā upajīvanti, yai pāṇā pathavissitā’’ti||

5. paṭhamajanasuttaṃ

55.

 ‘‘kiṃsu janaiti purisaṃ, kiṃsu tassa vidhāvati|

kiṃsu saṃsāramāpādi, kiṃsu tassa mahabbhaya’’nti||

‘‘taṇhā janaiti purisaṃ, cittamassa vidhāvati|

sattau saṃsāramāpādi, dukkhamassa mahabbhaya’’nti||

6. dutiyajanasuttaṃ

56.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  janaiti purisaṃ, kiṃsu tassa vidhāvati|

kiṃsu saṃsāramāpādi, kismā na parimuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘taṇhā janaiti purisaṃ, cittamassa vidhāvati|

sattau saṃsāramāpādi, dukkhā na parimuc‍catī’’ti||

7. tatiyajanasuttaṃ

57.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  janaiti purisaṃ, kiṃsu tassa vidhāvati|

kiṃsu saṃsāramāpādi, kiṃsu tassa parāyana’’nti||

‘‘taṇhā  janaiti purisaṃ, cittamassa vidhāvati|

sattau saṃsāramāpādi, kammaṃ tassa parāyana’’nti||

8. uppathasuttaṃ

58.

 ‘‘kiṃsu uppathau akkhātau, kiṃsu rattindivakkhayau|

kiṃ malaṃ brahmacariyassa, kiṃ sinānamanaudaka’’nti||

‘‘rāgau uppathau akkhātau, vayau rattindivakkhayau|

itthī malaṃ brahmacariyassa, aitthāyaṃ saj‍jatai pajā|

tapau ca brahmacariyañ‍ca, taṃ sinānamanaudaka’’nti||

9. dutiyasuttaṃ

59.

 ‘‘kiṃsu dutiyā dutiyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) purisassa hauti, kiṃsu cainaṃ pasāsati|

kissa cābhiratau mac‍cau, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘saddhā dutiyā purisassa hauti, pañ‍ñā cainaṃ pasāsati|

nibbānābhiratau mac‍cau, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

10. kavisuttaṃ

60.

 ‘‘kiṃsu nidānaṃ gāthānaṃ, kiṃsu tāsaṃ viyañ‍janaṃ|

kiṃsu san‍nissitā gāthā, kiṃsu gāthānamāsayau’’ti||

‘‘chandau  nidānaṃ gāthānaṃ, akkharā tāsaṃ viyañ‍janaṃ|

nāmasan‍nissitā gāthā, kavi gāthānamāsayau’’ti||

jarāvaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

jarā  ajarasā mittaṃ, vatthu tīṇi janāni ca|

uppathau ca dutiyau ca, kavinā pūritau vaggauti||

7. addhavaggau

1. nāmasuttaṃ

61.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  sabbaṃ addhabhavi anvabhavi (sī॰), kismā bhiyyau na vij‍jati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, sabbaiva vasamanvagū’’ti vasamaddhagū (ka॰)||

‘‘nāmaṃ sabbaṃ addhabhavi, nāmā bhiyyau na vij‍jati|

nāmassa aikadhammassa, sabbaiva vasamanvagū’’ti||

2. cittasuttaṃ

62.

 ‘‘kainassu nīyati laukau, kainassu parikassati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, sabbaiva vasamanvagū’’ti||

‘‘cittaina nīyati laukau, cittaina parikassati|

cittassa aikadhammassa, sabbaiva vasamanvagū’’ti||

3. taṇhāsuttaṃ

63.

 ‘‘kainassu nīyati laukau, kainassu parikassati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, sabbaiva vasamanvagū’’ti||

‘‘taṇhāya  nīyati laukau, taṇhāya parikassati|

taṇhāya aikadhammassa, sabbaiva vasamanvagū’’ti||

4. saṃyaujanasuttaṃ

64.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  saṃyaujanau laukau, kiṃsu tassa vicāraṇaṃ|

kissassu vippahānaina, nibbānaṃ iti vuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘nandīsaṃyaujanau nandisaṃyaujanau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) laukau, vitak‍kassa vicāraṇaṃ|

taṇhāya vippahānaina, nibbānaṃ iti vuc‍catī’’ti||

5. bandhanasuttaṃ

65.

 ‘‘kiṃsu sambandhanau laukau, kiṃsu tassa vicāraṇaṃ|

kissassu vippahānaina, sabbaṃ chindati bandhana’’nti||

‘‘nandīsambandhanau  laukau, vitak‍kassa vicāraṇaṃ|

taṇhāya vippahānaina, sabbaṃ chindati bandhana’’nti||

6. attahatasuttaṃ

66.

 ‘‘kainassubbhāhatau laukau, kainassu parivāritau|

kaina sal‍laina autiṇṇau, kissa dhūpāyitau sadā’’ti||

‘‘mac‍cunābbhāhatau laukau, jarāya parivāritau|

taṇhāsal‍laina autiṇṇau, icchādhūpāyitau sadā’’ti||

7. uḍḍitasuttaṃ

67.

 ‘‘kainassu  uḍḍitau laukau, kainassu parivāritau|

kainassu pihitau laukau, kismiṃ laukau patiṭṭhitau’’ti||

‘‘taṇhāya uḍḍitau laukau, jarāya parivāritau|

mac‍cunā pihitau laukau, dukkhai laukau patiṭṭhitau’’ti||

8. pihitasuttaṃ

68.

 ‘‘kainassu  pihitau laukau, kismiṃ laukau patiṭṭhitau|

kainassu uḍḍitau laukau, kainassu parivāritau’’ti||

‘‘mac‍cunā  pihitau laukau, dukkhai laukau patiṭṭhitau|

taṇhāya uḍḍitau laukau, jarāya parivāritau’’ti||

9. icchāsuttaṃ

69.

 ‘‘kainassu bajjhatī laukau, kissa vinayāya muc‍cati|

kissassu vippahānaina, sabbaṃ chindati bandhana’’nti||

‘‘icchāya bajjhatī laukau, icchāvinayāya muc‍cati|

icchāya vippahānaina, sabbaṃ chindati bandhana’’nti||

10. laukasuttaṃ

70.

 ‘‘kismiṃ  laukau samuppan‍nau, kismiṃ kubbati santhavaṃ|

kissa laukau upādāya, kismiṃ laukau vihañ‍ñatī’’ti||

‘‘chasu  laukau samuppan‍nau, chasu kubbati santhavaṃ|

chan‍namaiva upādāya, chasu laukau vihañ‍ñatī’’ti||

addhavaggau anvavaggau (sī॰) sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nāmaṃ cittañ‍ca taṇhā ca, saṃyaujanañ‍ca bandhanā|

abbhāhatuḍḍitau pihitau, icchā laukaina tai dasāti||

8. chaitvāvaggau

1. chaitvāsuttaṃ

71. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃsu chaitvā jhatvā (sī॰), ghatvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamuparipi sukhaṃ saiti, kiṃsu chaitvā na saucati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, vadhaṃ raucaisi gautamā’’ti||

‘‘kaudhaṃ  chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kaudhaṃ chaitvā na saucati|

kaudhassa visamūlassa, madhuraggassa daivatai|

vadhaṃ ariyā pasaṃsanti, tañhi chaitvā na saucatī’’ti||

2. rathasuttaṃ

72.

 ‘‘kiṃsu rathassa pañ‍ñāṇaṃ, kiṃsu pañ‍ñāṇamagginau|

kiṃsu raṭṭhassa pañ‍ñāṇaṃ, kiṃsu pañ‍ñāṇamitthiyā’’ti||

‘‘dhajau  rathassa pañ‍ñāṇaṃ, dhūmau pañ‍ñāṇamagginau|

rājā raṭṭhassa pañ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhattā pañ‍ñāṇamitthiyā’’ti||

3. vittasuttaṃ

73.

 ‘‘kiṃsūdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, kiṃsu suciṇṇau sukhamāvahati|

kiṃsu  havai sādutaraṃ sādhutaraṃ (ka॰) rasānaṃ, kathaṃjīviṃ kiṃsujīviṃ (ka॰) jīvitamāhu saiṭṭha’’nti||

‘‘saddhīdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, dhammau suciṇṇau sukhamāvahati|

sac‍caṃ havai sādutaraṃ rasānaṃ, pañ‍ñājīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭha’’nti||

4. vuṭṭhisuttaṃ

74.

 ‘‘kiṃsu uppatataṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, kiṃsu nipatataṃ varaṃ|

kiṃsu pavajamānānaṃ, kiṃsu pavadataṃ vara’’nti||

‘‘bījaṃ uppatataṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, vuṭṭhi nipatataṃ varā|

gāvau pavajamānānaṃ, puttau pavadataṃ varauti||

‘‘vij‍jā uppatataṃ saiṭṭhā, avij‍jā nipatataṃ varā|

saṅghau pavajamānānaṃ, buddhau pavadataṃ varau’’ti||

5. bhītāsuttaṃ

75.

 ‘‘kiṃsūdha bhītā janatā anaikā,

maggau canaikāyatanappavuttau|

pucchāmi taṃ gautama bhūripañ‍ña,

kismiṃ ṭhitau paralaukaṃ na bhāyai’’ti||

‘‘vācaṃ  manañ‍ca paṇidhāya sammā,

kāyaina  pāpāni akubbamānau|

bavhan‍napānaṃ gharamāvasantau,

saddhau  mudū saṃvibhāgī vadañ‍ñū|

aitaisu dhammaisu ṭhitau catūsu,

dhammai ṭhitau paralaukaṃ na bhāyai’’ti||

6. najīratisuttaṃ

76.

 ‘‘kiṃ jīrati kiṃ na jīrati, kiṃsu uppathauti vuc‍cati|

kiṃsu dhammānaṃ paripanthau, kiṃsu rattindivakkhayau|

kiṃ malaṃ brahmacariyassa, kiṃ sinānamanaudakaṃ||

‘‘kati laukasmiṃ chiddāni, yattha vittaṃ cittaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) na tiṭṭhati|

bhagavantaṃ puṭṭhumāgamma, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ maya’’nti||

‘‘rūpaṃ jīrati mac‍cānaṃ, nāmagauttaṃ na jīrati|

rāgau uppathauti vuc‍cati||

‘‘laubhau dhammānaṃ paripanthau, vayau rattindivakkhayau|

itthī malaṃ brahmacariyassa, aitthāyaṃ saj‍jatai pajā|

tapau ca brahmacariyañ‍ca, taṃ sinānamanaudakaṃ||

‘‘cha laukasmiṃ chiddāni, yattha vittaṃ na tiṭṭhati|

ālasyañ‍ca ālassañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) pamādau ca, anuṭṭhānaṃ asaṃyamau|

niddā tandī tandi (sī॰) ca tai chiddai, sabbasau taṃ vivaj‍jayai’’ti||

7. issariyasuttaṃ

77.

 ‘‘kiṃsu  issariyaṃ laukai, kiṃsu bhaṇḍānamuttamaṃ|

kiṃsu satthamalaṃ laukai, kiṃsu laukasmimabbudaṃ||

‘‘kiṃsu harantaṃ vārainti, harantau pana kau piyau|

kiṃsu punappunāyantaṃ, abhinandanti paṇḍitā’’ti||

‘‘vasau  issariyaṃ laukai, itthī bhaṇḍānamuttamaṃ|

kaudhau satthamalaṃ laukai, caurā laukasmimabbudā||

‘‘cauraṃ harantaṃ vārainti, harantau samaṇau piyau|

samaṇaṃ punappunāyantaṃ, abhinandanti paṇḍitā’’ti||

8. kāmasuttaṃ

78.

 ‘‘kimatthakāmau  na dadai, kiṃ mac‍cau na paric‍cajai|

kiṃsu muñ‍caiyya kalyāṇaṃ, pāpikaṃ na ca maucayai’’ti||

‘‘attānaṃ na dadai pausau, attānaṃ na paric‍cajai|

vācaṃ muñ‍caiyya kalyāṇaṃ, pāpikañ‍ca na maucayai’’ti||

9. pāthaiyyasuttaṃ

79.

 ‘‘kiṃsu bandhati pāthaiyyaṃ, kiṃsu bhaugānamāsayau|

kiṃsu naraṃ parikassati, kiṃsu laukasmi duj‍jahaṃ|

kismiṃ baddhā puthū sattā, pāsaina sakuṇī yathā’’ti||

‘‘saddhā  bandhati pāthaiyyaṃ, sirī bhaugānamāsayau|

icchā naraṃ parikassati, icchā laukasmi duj‍jahā|

icchābaddhā puthū sattā, pāsaina sakuṇī yathā’’ti||

10. paj‍jautasuttaṃ

80.

 ‘‘kiṃsu laukasmi paj‍jautau, kiṃsu laukasmi jāgarau|

kiṃsu kammai sajīvānaṃ, kimassa iriyāpathau||

‘‘kiṃsu alasaṃ analasañ‍ca kiṃ ālasyānālasyañ‍ca (ka॰), mātā puttaṃva pausati|

kiṃ bhūtā upajīvanti, yai pāṇā pathavissitā’’ti||

‘‘pañ‍ñā laukasmi paj‍jautau, sati laukasmi jāgarau|

gāvau kammai sajīvānaṃ, sītassa iriyāpathau||

‘‘vuṭṭhi alasaṃ analasañ‍ca, mātā puttaṃva pausati|

vuṭṭhiṃ bhūtā upajīvanti, yai pāṇā pathavissitā’’ti||

11. araṇasuttaṃ

81.

 ‘‘kaisūdha  araṇā laukai, kaisaṃ vusitaṃ na nassati|

kaidha icchaṃ parijānanti, kaisaṃ bhaujissiyaṃ sadā||

‘‘kiṃsu  mātā pitā bhātā, vandanti naṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ|

kiṃsu idha jātihīnaṃ, abhivādainti khattiyā’’ti||

‘‘samaṇīdha araṇā laukai, samaṇānaṃ vusitaṃ na nassati|

samaṇā icchaṃ parijānanti, samaṇānaṃ bhaujissiyaṃ sadā||

‘‘samaṇaṃ  mātā pitā bhātā, vandanti naṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ|

samaṇīdha jātihīnaṃ, abhivādainti khattiyā’’ti||

chaitvāvaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

chaitvā rathañ‍ca cittañ‍ca, vuṭṭhi bhītā najīrati|

issaraṃ kāmaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ, paj‍jautau araṇaina cāti||

daivatāsaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



2. daivaputtasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. paṭhamakassapasuttaṃ

82. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau kassapau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kassapau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhuṃ bhagavā pakāsaisi, nau ca bhikkhunau anusāsa’’nti| ‘‘taina hi kassapa, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭibhātū’’ti|

‘‘subhāsitassa sikkhaitha, samaṇūpāsanassa ca|

aikāsanassa ca rahau, cittavūpasamassa cā’’ti||

idamavauca kassapau daivaputtau; samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau kassapau daivaputtau ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

2. dutiyakassapasuttaṃ

83. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kassapau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘bhikkhu  siyā jhāyī vimuttacittau,

ākaṅkhai cai hadayassānupattiṃ|

laukassa ñatvā udayabbayañ‍ca,

sucaitasau anissitau tadānisaṃsau’’ti||

3. māghasuttaṃ

84. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau māghau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau māghau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃsu chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kiṃsu chaitvā na saucati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, vadhaṃ raucaisi gautamā’’ti||

‘‘kaudhaṃ chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kaudhaṃ chaitvā na saucati|

kaudhassa visamūlassa, madhuraggassa vatrabhū|

vadhaṃ ariyā pasaṃsanti, tañhi chaitvā na saucatī’’ti||

4. māgadhasuttaṃ

85. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau māgadhau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kati  laukasmiṃ paj‍jautā, yaihi laukau pakāsati|

bhavantaṃ puṭṭhumāgamma, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ maya’’nti||

‘‘cattārau  laukai paj‍jautā, pañ‍camaittha na vij‍jati|

divā tapati ādic‍cau, rattimābhāti candimā||

‘‘atha aggi divārattiṃ, tattha tattha pakāsati|

sambuddhau tapataṃ saiṭṭhau, aisā ābhā anuttarā’’ti||

5. dāmalisuttaṃ

86. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau dāmali daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau dāmali daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘karaṇīyamaitaṃ brāhmaṇaina, padhānaṃ akilāsunā|

kāmānaṃ vippahānaina, na taināsīsatai bhava’’nti||

‘‘natthi kic‍caṃ brāhmaṇassa (dāmalīti bhagavā),

katakic‍cau hi brāhmaṇau||

‘‘yāva  na gādhaṃ labhati nadīsu,

āyūhati  sabbagattaibhi jantu|

gādhañ‍ca laddhāna thalai ṭhitau yau,

nāyūhatī  pāragatau hi sauva sauti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), hauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sau (?)||

‘‘aisūpamā dāmali brāhmaṇassa,

khīṇāsavassa nipakassa jhāyinau|

pappuyya jātimaraṇassa antaṃ,

nāyūhatī pāragatau hi sau’’ti hautīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

6. kāmadasuttaṃ

87. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kāmadau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘duk‍karaṃ bhagavā, suduk‍karaṃ bhagavā’’ti|

‘‘duk‍karaṃ vāpi karaunti (kāmadāti bhagavā),

saikhā sīlasamāhitā|

ṭhitattā anagāriyupaitassa,

tuṭṭhi hauti sukhāvahā’’ti||

‘‘dul‍labhā bhagavā yadidaṃ tuṭṭhī’’ti|

‘‘dul‍labhaṃ vāpi labhanti (kāmadāti bhagavā),

cittavūpasamai ratā|

yaisaṃ divā ca rattau ca,

bhāvanāya ratau manau’’ti||

‘‘dussamādahaṃ bhagavā yadidaṃ citta’’nti|

‘‘dussamādahaṃ vāpi samādahanti (kāmadāti bhagavā),

indriyūpasamai ratā|

tai chaitvā mac‍cunau jālaṃ,

ariyā gacchanti kāmadā’’ti||

‘‘duggamau  bhagavā visamau maggau’’ti|

‘‘duggamai visamai vāpi, ariyā gacchanti kāmada|

anariyā visamai maggai, papatanti avaṃsirā|

ariyānaṃ samau maggau, ariyā hi visamai samā’’ti||

7. pañ‍cālacaṇḍasuttaṃ

88. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau pañ‍cālacaṇḍau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sambādhai vata aukāsaṃ, avindi bhūrimaidhasau|

yau jhānamabujjhi jhānamabudhā (ka॰ sī॰), jhānamabuddhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) buddhau, paṭilīnanisabhau munī’’ti||

‘‘sambādhai vāpi vindanti (pañ‍cālacaṇḍāti bhagavā),

dhammaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

yai satiṃ pac‍calatthaṃsu,

sammā tai susamāhitā’’ti||

8. tāyanasuttaṃ

89. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha  khau tāyanau daivaputtau purāṇatitthakarau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau tāyanau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘chinda sautaṃ parak‍kamma, kāmai panuda brāhmaṇa|

nappahāya munī kāmai, naikattamupapaj‍jati||

‘‘kayirā cai kayirāthainaṃ, daḷhamainaṃ parak‍kamai|

sithilau hi paribbājau, bhiyyau ākiratai rajaṃ||

‘‘akataṃ  duk‍kaṭaṃ duk‍kataṃ (sī॰ pī॰) saiyyau, pacchā tapati duk‍kaṭaṃ|

katañ‍ca sukataṃ saiyyau, yaṃ katvā nānutappati||

‘‘kusau  yathā duggahitau, hatthamaivānukantati|

sāmañ‍ñaṃ dupparāmaṭṭhaṃ, nirayāyūpakaḍḍhati||

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci sithilaṃ kammaṃ, saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca yaṃ vataṃ|

saṅkassaraṃ brahmacariyaṃ, na taṃ hauti mahapphala’’nti||

idamavauca tāyanau daivaputtau; idaṃ vatvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ tāyanau nāma daivaputtau purāṇatitthakarau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhikkhavai, tāyanau daivaputtau mama santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘chinda sautaṃ parak‍kamma, kāmai panuda brāhmaṇa|

nappahāya munī kāmai, naikattamupapaj‍jati||

‘‘kayirā cai kayirāthainaṃ, daḷhamainaṃ parak‍kamai|

sithilau  hi paribbājau, bhiyyau ākiratai rajaṃ||

‘‘akataṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ saiyyau, pacchā tapati duk‍kaṭaṃ|

katañ‍ca sukataṃ saiyyau, yaṃ katvā nānutappati||

‘‘kusau  yathā duggahitau, hatthamaivānukantati|

sāmañ‍ñaṃ dupparāmaṭṭhaṃ, nirayāyūpakaḍḍhati||

‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci sithilaṃ kammaṃ, saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca yaṃ vataṃ|

saṅkassaraṃ brahmacariyaṃ, na taṃ hauti mahapphala’’nti||

‘‘idamavauca, bhikkhavai, tāyanau daivaputtau, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi| uggaṇhātha, bhikkhavai, tāyanagāthā; pariyāpuṇātha, bhikkhavai, tāyanagāthā; dhāraitha, bhikkhavai, tāyanagāthā| atthasaṃhitā, bhikkhavai, tāyanagāthā ādibrahmacariyikā’’ti|

9. candimasuttaṃ

90. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina candimā daivaputtau rāhunā asurindaina gahitau hauti| atha khau candimā daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ anussaramānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘namau tai buddha vīratthu, vippamuttausi sabbadhi|

sambādhapaṭipan‍nausmi, tassa mai saraṇaṃ bhavā’’ti||

atha khau bhagavā candimaṃ daivaputtaṃ ārabbha rāhuṃ asurindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tathāgataṃ arahantaṃ, candimā saraṇaṃ gatau|

rāhu candaṃ pamuñ‍cassu, buddhā laukānukampakā’’ti||

atha khau rāhu asurindau candimaṃ daivaputtaṃ muñ‍citvā taramānarūpau yaina vaipacitti asurindau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā saṃviggau  laumahaṭṭhajātau aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau rāhuṃ asurindaṃ vaipacitti asurindau gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃ nu santaramānauva, rāhu candaṃ pamuñ‍casi|

saṃviggarūpau āgamma, kiṃ nu bhītauva tiṭṭhasī’’ti||

‘‘sattadhā  mai phalai muddhā, jīvantau na sukhaṃ labhai|

buddhagāthābhigītaumhi, nau cai muñ‍caiyya candima’’nti||

10. sūriyasuttaṃ

91. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina  khau pana samayaina sūriyau daivaputtau rāhunā asurindaina gahitau hauti| atha khau sūriyau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ anussaramānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘namau tai buddha vīratthu, vippamuttausi sabbadhi|

sambādhapaṭipan‍nausmi, tassa mai saraṇaṃ bhavā’’ti||

atha khau bhagavā sūriyaṃ daivaputtaṃ ārabbha rāhuṃ asurindaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tathāgataṃ  arahantaṃ, sūriyau saraṇaṃ gatau|

rāhu sūriyaṃ suriyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pamuñ‍cassu, buddhā laukānukampakā||

‘‘yau andhakārai tamasi pabhaṅkarau,

vairaucanau maṇḍalī uggataijau|

mā  rāhu gilī caramantalikkhai,

pajaṃ mamaṃ rāhu pamuñ‍ca sūriya’’nti||

atha khau rāhu asurindau sūriyaṃ daivaputtaṃ muñ‍citvā taramānarūpau yaina vaipacitti asurindau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau rāhuṃ asurindaṃ vaipacitti asurindau gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃ nu santaramānauva, rāhu sūriyaṃ pamuñ‍casi|

saṃviggarūpau āgamma, kiṃ nu bhītauva tiṭṭhasī’’ti||

‘‘sattadhā  mai phalai muddhā, jīvantau na sukhaṃ labhai|

buddhagāthābhigītaumhi, nau cai muñ‍caiyya sūriya’’nti||

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dvai kassapā ca māghau ca, māgadhau dāmali kāmadau|

pañ‍cālacaṇḍau tāyanau, candimasūriyaina tai dasāti||

2. anāthapiṇḍikavaggau

1. candimasasuttaṃ

92. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau candimasau candimāsau (ka॰) daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau candimasau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘tai hi sautthiṃ gamissanti, kacchaivāmakasai magā|

jhānāni upasampaj‍ja, aikaudi nipakā satā’’ti||

‘‘tai hi pāraṃ gamissanti, chaitvā jālaṃva ambujau|

jhānāni upasampaj‍ja, appamattā raṇañ‍jahā’’ti||

2. vaiṇḍusuttaṃ

93. aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau vaiṇḍu vaiṇhu (sī॰) daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sukhitāva tai sukhitā vata tai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) manujā, sugataṃ payirupāsiya|

yuñ‍jaṃ yuj‍ja (sī॰), yuñ‍ja (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) gautamasāsanai, appamattā nu sikkharai’’ti||

‘‘yai  mai pavuttai siṭṭhipadai satthipadai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) (vaiṇḍūti bhagavā),

anusikkhanti jhāyinau|

kālai tai appamaj‍jantā,

na mac‍cuvasagā siyu’’nti||

3. dīghalaṭṭhisuttaṃ

94. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau dīghalaṭṭhi daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ vaiḷuvanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau dīghalaṭṭhi daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘bhikkhu siyā jhāyī vimuttacittau,

ākaṅkhai cai hadayassānupattiṃ|

laukassa ñatvā udayabbayañ‍ca,

sucaitasau anissitau tadānisaṃsau’’ti||

4. nandanasuttaṃ

95. aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau nandanau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘pucchāmi taṃ gautama bhūripañ‍ña,

anāvaṭaṃ bhagavatau ñāṇadassanaṃ|

kathaṃvidhaṃ  sīlavantaṃ vadanti,

kathaṃvidhaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ vadanti|

kathaṃvidhau dukkhamatic‍ca iriyati,

kathaṃvidhaṃ daivatā pūjayantī’’ti||

‘‘yau sīlavā pañ‍ñavā bhāvitattau,

samāhitau jhānaratau satīmā|

sabbassa saukā vigatā pahīnā,

khīṇāsavau antimadaihadhārī||

‘‘tathāvidhaṃ sīlavantaṃ vadanti,

tathāvidhaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ vadanti|

tathāvidhau dukkhamatic‍ca iriyati,

tathāvidhaṃ daivatā pūjayantī’’ti||

5. candanasuttaṃ

96. aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau candanau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kathaṃsu kausudha (sī॰) tarati aughaṃ, rattindivamatanditau|

appatiṭṭhai anālambai, kau gambhīrai na sīdatī’’ti||

‘‘sabbadā sīlasampan‍nau, pañ‍ñavā susamāhitau|

āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, aughaṃ tarati duttaraṃ||

‘‘viratau  kāmasañ‍ñāya, rūpasaṃyaujanātigau|

nandīrāgaparikkhīṇau, sau gambhīrai na sīdatī’’ti||

6. vāsudattasuttaṃ

97. aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau vāsudattau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva ḍayhamānaiva (sabbattha) matthakai|

kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva matthakai|

sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

7. subrahmasuttaṃ

98. aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau subrahmā daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘nic‍caṃ utrastamidaṃ cittaṃ, nic‍caṃ ubbiggamidaṃ ubbiggidaṃ (mahāsatipaṭṭhānasuttavaṇṇanāyaṃ) manau|

anuppan‍naisu  kicchaisu kic‍caisu (bahūsu), athau uppatitaisu ca|

sacai atthi anutrastaṃ, taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitau’’ti||

‘‘nāñ‍ñatra baujjhā tapasā baujjhaṅgatapasā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), nāñ‍ñatrindriyasaṃvarā|

nāñ‍ñatra sabbanissaggā, sautthiṃ passāmi pāṇina’’nti||

‘‘idamavauca…pai॰… tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

8. kakudhasuttaṃ

99. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sākaitai viharati añ‍janavanai migadāyai| atha khau kakudhau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ añ‍janavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau kakudhau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nandasi, samaṇā’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ laddhā, āvusau’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, samaṇa, saucasī’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ jīyittha, āvusau’’ti? ‘‘taina hi, samaṇa, naiva nandasi na ca naiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) saucasī’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti|

‘‘kac‍ci  tvaṃ anaghau anighau (sabbattha) bhikkhu, kac‍ci nandī nandi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) na vij‍jati|

kac‍ci taṃ aikamāsīnaṃ, aratī nābhikīratī’’ti||

‘‘anaghau vai ahaṃ yakkha, athau nandī na vij‍jati|

athau maṃ aikamāsīnaṃ, aratī nābhikīratī’’ti||

‘‘kathaṃ tvaṃ anaghau bhikkhu, kathaṃ nandī na vij‍jati|

kathaṃ taṃ aikamāsīnaṃ, aratī nābhikīratī’’ti||

‘‘aghajātassa vai nandī, nandījātassa vai aghaṃ|

anandī anaghau bhikkhu, aivaṃ jānāhi āvusau’’ti||

‘‘cirassaṃ vata passāmi, brāhmaṇaṃ parinibbutaṃ|

anandiṃ anaghaṃ bhikkhuṃ, tiṇṇaṃ laukai visattika’’nti||

9. uttarasuttaṃ

100. rājagahanidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau uttarau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘upanīyati  jīvitamappamāyu,

jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā|

aitaṃ  bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

puñ‍ñāni kayirātha sukhāvahānī’’ti||

‘‘upanīyati jīvitamappamāyu,

jarūpanītassa na santi tāṇā|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau,

laukāmisaṃ pajahai santipaikkhau’’ti||

10. anāthapiṇḍikasuttaṃ

101. aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘idañhi taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama||

‘‘kammaṃ  vij‍jā ca dhammau ca, sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamaṃ|

aitaina mac‍cā sujjhanti, na gauttaina dhanaina vā||

‘‘tasmā hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau|

yaunisau  vicinai dhammaṃ, aivaṃ tattha visujjhati||

‘‘sāriputtauva pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina ca|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

idamavauca anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

atha  khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhikkhavai, sau daivaputtau mama santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘idañhi taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama||

‘‘kammaṃ vij‍jā ca dhammau ca, sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamaṃ|

aitaina mac‍cā sujjhanti, na gauttaina dhanaina vā||

‘‘tasmā hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau|

yaunisau vicinai dhammaṃ, aivaṃ tattha visujjhati||

‘‘sāriputtauva pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina ca|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

‘‘idamavauca, bhikkhavai, sau daivaputtau| idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sau hi nūna, bhantai, anāthapiṇḍikau daivaputtau bhavissati| anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati āyasmantai sāriputtai abhippasan‍nau ahausī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu  sādhu, ānanda, yāvatakaṃ khau, ānanda, tak‍kāya pattabbaṃ anuppattaṃ taṃ tayā| anāthapiṇḍikau hi sau, ānanda, daivaputtau’’ti|

anāthapiṇḍikavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

candimasau  candimāsau (pī॰ ka॰) ca vaiṇḍu vaiṇhu (sī॰ ka॰) ca, dīghalaṭṭhi ca nandanau|

candanau vāsudattau ca, subrahmā kakudhaina ca|

uttarau navamau vuttau, dasamau anāthapiṇḍikauti||

3. nānātitthiyavaggau

1. sivasuttaṃ

102. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau sivau daivaputtau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sivau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, saiyyau hauti na pāpiyau||

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, pañ‍ñā labbhati nāñ‍ñatau||

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, saukamajjhai na saucati||

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ  saddhammamañ‍ñāya, ñātimajjhai viraucati||

‘‘sabbhiraiva  samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ  saddhammamañ‍ñāya, sattā gacchanti suggatiṃ||

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, sattā tiṭṭhanti sātata’’nti||

atha khau bhagavā sivaṃ daivaputtaṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, sabbhi kubbaitha santhavaṃ|

sataṃ saddhammamañ‍ñāya, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

2. khaimasuttaṃ

103. aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau khaimau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘caranti bālā dummaidhā, amittainaiva attanā|

karauntā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, yaṃ hauti kaṭukapphalaṃ||

‘‘na taṃ kammaṃ kataṃ sādhu, yaṃ katvā anutappati|

yassa assumukhau raudaṃ, vipākaṃ paṭisaivati||

‘‘tañ‍ca kammaṃ kataṃ sādhu, yaṃ katvā nānutappati|

yassa patītau sumanau, vipākaṃ paṭisaivati||

‘‘paṭikac‍caiva paṭigac‍caiva (sī॰) taṃ kayirā, yaṃ jañ‍ñā hitamattanau|

na sākaṭikacintāya, mantā dhīrau parak‍kamai||

‘‘yathā sākaṭikau maṭṭhaṃ panthaṃ (sī॰), pasatthaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), samaṃ hitvā mahāpathaṃ|

visamaṃ maggamāruyha, akkhacchin‍nauva jhāyati||

‘‘aivaṃ  dhammā apak‍kamma, adhammamanuvattiya|

mandau mac‍cumukhaṃ pattau, akkhacchin‍nauva jhāyatī’’ti||

3. sairīsuttaṃ

104. aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sairī daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘an‍namaivābhinandanti, ubhayai daivamānusā|

atha kau nāma sau yakkhau, yaṃ an‍naṃ nābhinandatī’’ti||

‘‘yai naṃ dadanti saddhāya, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

tamaiva an‍naṃ bhajati, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca||

‘‘tasmā vinaiyya macchairaṃ, daj‍jā dānaṃ malābhibhū|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti||

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvasubhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā –

‘‘yai naṃ dadanti saddhāya, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

tamaiva an‍naṃ bhajati, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca||

‘‘tasmā vinaiyya macchairaṃ, daj‍jā dānaṃ malābhibhū|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti||

‘‘bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, sirī sairī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nāma rājā ahausiṃ dāyakau dānapati dānassa vaṇṇavādī| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, catūsu dvāraisu dānaṃ dīyittha samaṇa-brāhmaṇa-kapaṇaddhika-vanibbakayācakānaṃ| atha khau  maṃ, bhantai, itthāgāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca itthāgārā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ (ka॰) – ‘daivassa khau daivassaiva khau (ka॰ sī॰) dānaṃ dīyati; amhākaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati| sādhu mayampi daivaṃ nissāya dānāni dadaiyyāma, puñ‍ñāni karaiyyāmā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khausmi dāyakau dānapati dānassa vaṇṇavādī| dānaṃ dassāmāti vadantai kinti vadaiyya’nti? sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, paṭhamaṃ dvāraṃ itthāgārassa adāsiṃ| tattha itthāgārassa dānaṃ dīyittha; mama dānaṃ paṭik‍kami|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, bhantai, khattiyā anuyantā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘daivassa khau dānaṃ dīyati; itthāgārassa dānaṃ dīyati; amhākaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati| sādhu mayampi daivaṃ nissāya dānāni dadaiyyāma, puñ‍ñāni karaiyyāmā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khausmi dāyakau dānapati dānassa vaṇṇavādī| dānaṃ dassāmāti vadantai kinti vadaiyya’nti? sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, dutiyaṃ dvāraṃ khattiyānaṃ anuyantānaṃ adāsiṃ| tattha khattiyānaṃ anuyantānaṃ dānaṃ dīyittha, mama dānaṃ paṭik‍kami|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, bhantai, balakāyau upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘daivassa khau dānaṃ dīyati; itthāgārassa dānaṃ dīyati; khattiyānaṃ anuyantānaṃ dānaṃ dīyati; amhākaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati| sādhu mayampi daivaṃ nissāya dānāni dadaiyyāma, puñ‍ñāni karaiyyāmā’ti| tassa  mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khausmi dāyakau dānapati dānassa vaṇṇavādī| dānaṃ dassāmāti vadantai  kinti vadaiyya’nti? sau khvāhaṃ bhantai, tatiyaṃ dvāraṃ balakāyassa adāsiṃ| tattha balakāyassa dānaṃ dīyittha, mama dānaṃ paṭik‍kami|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, bhantai, brāhmaṇagahapatikā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘daivassa khau dānaṃ dīyati; itthāgārassa dānaṃ dīyati; khattiyānaṃ anuyantānaṃ dānaṃ dīyati; balakāyassa dānaṃ dīyati; amhākaṃ dānaṃ na dīyati| sādhu mayampi daivaṃ nissāya dānāni dadaiyyāma, puñ‍ñāni karaiyyāmā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘ahaṃ khausmi dāyakau dānapati dānassa vaṇṇavādī| dānaṃ dassāmāti vadantai kinti vadaiyya’nti? sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, catutthaṃ dvāraṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikānaṃ adāsiṃ| tattha brāhmaṇagahapatikānaṃ dānaṃ dīyittha, mama dānaṃ paṭik‍kami|

‘‘atha khau maṃ, bhantai, purisā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘na khau dāni daivassa kauci dānaṃ dīyatī’ti| aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, tai purisai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘taina hi, bhaṇai, yau bāhiraisu janapadaisu āyau sañ‍jāyati tatau upaḍḍhaṃ antaipurai pavaisaitha, upaḍḍhaṃ tatthaiva dānaṃ daitha samaṇa-brāhmaṇa-kapaṇaddhika-vanibbaka-yācakāna’nti| sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ dīgharattaṃ katānaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ aivaṃ dīgharattaṃ katānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pariyantaṃ nādhigacchāmi – aittakaṃ puñ‍ñanti vā aittakau puñ‍ñavipākauti vā aittakaṃ saggai ṭhātabbanti vāti| acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvasubhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā –

‘‘yai  naṃ dadanti saddhāya, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

tamaiva an‍naṃ bhajati, asmiṃ laukai paramhi ca||

‘‘tasmā vinaiyya macchairaṃ, daj‍jā dānaṃ malābhibhū|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti||

4. ghaṭīkārasuttaṃ

105. aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau ghaṭīkārau daivaputtau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘avihaṃ upapan‍nāsai, vimuttā satta bhikkhavau|

rāgadausaparikkhīṇā, tiṇṇā laukai visattika’’nti||

‘‘kai ca tai ataruṃ paṅkaṃ, mac‍cudhaiyyaṃ suduttaraṃ|

kai hitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, dibbayaugaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti||

‘‘upakau palagaṇḍau phalagaṇḍau (ka॰) ca, puk‍kusāti ca tai tayau|

bhaddiyau khaṇḍadaivau ca, bāhuraggi ca saṅgiyau bāhudantī ca piṅgiyau (sī॰ syā॰)|

tai hitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, dibbayaugaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti||

‘‘kusalī bhāsasī taisaṃ, mārapāsappahāyinaṃ|

kassa tai dhammamañ‍ñāya, acchiduṃ bhavabandhana’’nti||

‘‘na  añ‍ñatra bhagavatā, nāñ‍ñatra tava sāsanā|

yassa tai dhammamañ‍ñāya, acchiduṃ bhavabandhanaṃ||

‘‘yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

taṃ tai dhammaṃ idhañ‍ñāya, acchiduṃ bhavabandhana’’nti||

‘‘gambhīraṃ  bhāsasī vācaṃ, dubbijānaṃ sudubbudhaṃ|

kassa tvaṃ dhammamañ‍ñāya, vācaṃ bhāsasi īdisa’’nti||

‘‘kumbhakārau purai āsiṃ, vaikaḷiṅgai ghaṭīkarau|

mātāpaittibharau āsiṃ, kassapassa upāsakau||

‘‘viratau maithunā dhammā, brahmacārī nirāmisau|

ahuvā tai sagāmaiyyau, ahuvā tai purai sakhā||

‘‘sauhamaitai pajānāmi, vimuttai satta bhikkhavau|

rāgadausaparikkhīṇai, tiṇṇai laukai visattika’’nti||

‘‘aivamaitaṃ tadā āsi, yathā bhāsasi bhaggava|

kumbhakārau purai āsi, vaikaḷiṅgai ghaṭīkarau||

‘‘mātāpaittibharau  āsi, kassapassa upāsakau|

viratau maithunā dhammā, brahmacārī nirāmisau|

ahuvā mai sagāmaiyyau, ahuvā mai purai sakhā’’ti||

‘‘aivamaitaṃ purāṇānaṃ, sahāyānaṃ ahu saṅgamau|

ubhin‍naṃ bhāvitattānaṃ, sarīrantimadhārina’’nti||

5. jantusuttaṃ

106. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā bhikkhū, kausalaisu viharanti himavantapassai arañ‍ñakuṭikāya uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā pākatindriyā|

atha khau  jantu daivaputtau tadahupausathai pan‍narasai yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘sukhajīvinau purai āsuṃ, bhikkhū gautamasāvakā|

anicchā piṇḍamaisanā piṇḍamaisānā (?), anicchā sayanāsanaṃ|

laukai anic‍cataṃ ñatvā, dukkhassantaṃ akaṃsu tai||

‘‘duppausaṃ katvā attānaṃ, gāmai gāmaṇikā viya|

bhutvā bhutvā nipaj‍janti, parāgāraisu mucchitā||

‘‘saṅghassa añ‍jaliṃ katvā, idhaikac‍cai vadāmahaṃ vandāmahaṃ (ka॰)|

apaviddhā anāthā tai, yathā paitā tathaiva tai tathaiva ca (sī॰)||

‘‘yai khau pamattā viharanti, tai mai sandhāya bhāsitaṃ|

yai appamattā viharanti, namau taisaṃ karaumaha’’nti||

6. rauhitassasuttaṃ

107. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau rauhitassau daivaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yattha nu khau, bhantai, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na jiyyati na miyyati (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) na cavati na upapaj‍jati, sak‍kā nu khau sau, bhantai, gamanaina laukassa  antau ñātuṃ vā daṭṭhuṃ vā pāpuṇituṃ  vā’’ti? ‘‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ  pattaiyyanti vadāmī’’ti|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvasubhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhantai, rauhitassau nāma isi ahausiṃ bhaujaputtau iddhimā vaihāsaṅgamau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpau  javau ahausi; saiyyathāpi nāma daḷhadhammā daḷhadhammau (sabbattha) ṭīkā ca mauggal‍lānabyākaraṇaṃ ca aulaukaitabbaṃ dhanuggahau susikkhitau katahatthau katayauggau katūpāsanau lahukaina asanaina appakasirainaiva tiriyaṃ tālacchāyaṃ atipātaiyya| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpau padavītihārau ahausi; saiyyathāpi nāma puratthimā samuddā pacchimau samuddau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpaṃ icchāgataṃ uppaj‍ji – ‘ahaṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, aivarūpaina javaina saman‍nāgatau aivarūpaina ca padavītihāraina añ‍ñatraiva asita-pīta-khāyita-sāyitā añ‍ñatra uc‍cāra-passāvakammā añ‍ñatra niddākilamathapaṭivinaudanā vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvāva laukassa antaṃ antarāva kālaṅkatau|

‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhantai, abbhutaṃ, bhantai! yāvasubhāsitamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā – ‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmī’’’ti|

‘‘na khau  panāhaṃ, āvusau, appatvā laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi| api ca khvāhaṃ, āvusau, imasmiṃyaiva byāmamattai kaḷaivarai sasañ‍ñimhi samanakai laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaimi laukasamudayañ‍ca laukaniraudhañ‍ca laukaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadanti|

‘‘gamanaina na pattabbau, laukassantau kudācanaṃ|

na ca appatvā laukantaṃ, dukkhā atthi pamaucanaṃ||

‘‘tasmā  havai laukavidū sumaidhau,

laukantagū vusitabrahmacariyau|

laukassa antaṃ samitāvi ñatvā,

nāsīsati laukamimaṃ parañ‍cā’’ti||
