atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘nāc‍cayanti  ahaurattā, jīvitaṃ nūparujjhati|

āyu anupariyāyati, mac‍cānaṃ naimīva rathakubbara’’nti||

‘‘ac‍cayanti ahaurattā, jīvitaṃ uparujjhati|

āyu khīyati mac‍cānaṃ, kun‍nadīnaṃva audaka’’nti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

tapaukammañ‍ca nāgau ca, subhaṃ pāsaina tai duvai|

sappau supati nandanaṃ, āyunā aparai duvaiti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. pāsāṇasuttaṃ

147. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti, daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusāyati| atha khau mārau pāpimā bhagavatau bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau avidūrai mahantai pāsāṇai padālaisi|

atha khau bhagavā ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘sacaipi kaivalaṃ sabbaṃ, gijjhakūṭaṃ calaissasi gaḷaiyyasi (syā॰ kaṃ॰), calaiyyāsi (ka॰)|

naiva sammāvimuttānaṃ, buddhānaṃ atthi iñ‍jita’’nti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

2. kin‍nusīhasuttaṃ

148. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa  ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaiti|

atha  khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaiti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ vicakkhukammāyā’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kin‍nu  sīhauva nadasi, parisāyaṃ visāradau|

paṭimal‍lau hi tai atthi, vijitāvī nu mañ‍ñasī’’ti||

‘‘nadanti vai mahāvīrā, parisāsu visāradā|

tathāgatā balappattā, tiṇṇā laukai visattika’’nti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

3. sakalikasuttaṃ

149. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati maddakucchismiṃ migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavatau pādau sakalikāya  khatau hauti, bhusā sudaṃ bhagavatau vaidanā vattanti sārīrikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā asātā amanāpā| tā sudaṃ bhagavā satau sampajānau adhivāsaiti avihañ‍ñamānau| atha khau bhagavā catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaisi  pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya satau sampajānau| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘mandiyā nu khau saisi udāhu kāvaiyyamattau,

atthā nu tai sampacurā na santi|

aikau vivittai sayanāsanamhi,

niddāmukhau kimidaṃ sauppasai vā’’ti||

‘‘na mandiyā sayāmi nāpi kāvaiyyamattau,

atthaṃ samaic‍cāhamapaitasaukau|

aikau vivittai sayanāsanamhi,

sayāmahaṃ sabbabhūtānukampī||

‘‘yaisampi sal‍laṃ urasi paviṭṭhaṃ,

muhuṃ muhuṃ hadayaṃ vaidhamānaṃ|

taipīdha  sauppaṃ labharai sasal‍lā,

tasmā  ahaṃ na supai vītasal‍lau||

‘‘jaggaṃ na saṅkai napi bhaimi sauttuṃ,

rattindivā nānutapanti māmaṃ|

hāniṃ na passāmi kuhiñ‍ci laukai,

tasmā supai sabbabhūtānukampī’’ti||

atha  khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

4. patirūpasuttaṃ

150. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati aikasālāyaṃ brāhmaṇagāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā gihiparisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaiti|

atha  khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau mahatiyā gihiparisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaiti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ vicakkhukammāyā’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘naitaṃ tava patirūpaṃ, yadañ‍ñamanusāsasi|

anuraudhaviraudhaisu, mā saj‍jitthau tadācara’’nti||

‘‘hitānukampī sambuddhau, yadañ‍ñamanusāsati|

anuraudhaviraudhaihi, vippamuttau tathāgatau’’ti||

atha  khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

5. mānasasuttaṃ

151. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha  khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘antalikkhacarau pāsau, yvāyaṃ carati mānasau|

taina taṃ bādhayissāmi, na mai samaṇa maukkhasī’’ti||

‘‘rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phauṭṭhabbā ca manauramā|

aittha mai vigatau chandau, nihatau tvamasi antakā’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

6. pattasuttaṃ

152. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ upādāya bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samādāpaiti (?) samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā aṭṭhikatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) manasi  katvā sabbacaitasā sabbacaitasau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbaṃ caitasā (ka॰) saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti|

atha khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ upādāya bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā  dhammaṃ suṇanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ vicakkhukammāyā’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā pattā abbhaukāsai nikkhittā haunti| atha  khau mārau pāpimā balībaddavaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā yaina tai pattā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhu, bhikkhu, aisau balībaddau pattai bhindaiyyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na sau, bhikkhu, balībaddau| mārau aisau pāpimā tumhākaṃ vicakkhukammāya āgatau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘rūpaṃ vaidayitaṃ sañ‍ñā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yañ‍ca saṅkhataṃ|

naisauhamasmi naitaṃ mai, aivaṃ tattha viraj‍jati||

‘‘aivaṃ virattaṃ khaimattaṃ, sabbasaṃyaujanātigaṃ|

anvaisaṃ sabbaṭṭhānaisu, mārasaināpi nājjhagā’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā…pai॰… tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

7. chaphassāyatanasuttaṃ

153. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina  khau pana samayaina bhagavā chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ upādāya bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti|

atha  khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ upādāya bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sappahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā  dhammaṃ suṇanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ vicakkhukammāyā’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau avidūrai mahantaṃ bhayabhairavaṃ saddamakāsi, apissudaṃ pathavī mañ‍ñai undrīyati udrīyati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī) u + dara + ya + ti = udrīyati| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhikkhu, bhikkhu, aisā pathavī mañ‍ñai undrīyatī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘naisā bhikkhu pathavī undrīyati| mārau aisau pāpimā tumhākaṃ vicakkhukammāya āgatau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phassā dhammā ca kaivalā|

aitaṃ laukāmisaṃ ghauraṃ, aittha laukau vimucchitau||

‘‘aitañ‍ca samatik‍kamma, satau buddhassa sāvakau|

māradhaiyyaṃ atik‍kamma, ādic‍cauva viraucatī’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā…pai॰… tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

8. piṇḍasuttaṃ

154. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu viharati pañ‍casālāyaṃ brāhmaṇagāmai| taina  khau pana samayaina pañ‍casālāyaṃ brāhmaṇagāmai kumārikānaṃ  pāhunakāni bhavanti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya pañ‍casālaṃ brāhmaṇagāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍casālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā māraina pāpimatā anvāviṭṭhā bhavanti – mā samaṇau gautamau piṇḍamalatthāti|

atha khau bhagavā yathādhautaina pattaina pañ‍casālaṃ brāhmaṇagāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi tathādhautaina yathādhautaina (?) pattaina paṭik‍kami| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘api  tvaṃ, samaṇa, piṇḍamalatthā’’ti? ‘‘tathā nu tvaṃ, pāpima, akāsi yathāhaṃ piṇḍaṃ  na labhaiyya’’nti| ‘‘taina hi, bhantai, bhagavā dutiyampi pañ‍casālaṃ brāhmaṇagāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisatu| tathāhaṃ karissāmi yathā bhagavā piṇḍaṃ lacchatī’’ti|

‘‘apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavi mārau, āsaj‍ja naṃ tathāgataṃ|

kiṃ nu mañ‍ñasi pāpima, na mai pāpaṃ vipac‍cati||

‘‘susukhaṃ vata jīvāma, yaisaṃ nau natthi kiñ‍canaṃ|

pītibhakkhā bhavissāma, daivā ābhassarā yathā’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

9. kassakasuttaṃ

155. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ nibbānapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti|

atha  khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau bhikkhūnaṃ nibbānapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya…pai॰… yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ vicakkhukammāyā’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā kassakavaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā mahantaṃ naṅgalaṃ khandhai karitvā dīghapācanayaṭṭhiṃ gahaitvā haṭahaṭakaisau sāṇasāṭinivatthau kaddamamakkhitaihi pādaihi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘api, samaṇa, balībaddai addasā’’ti? ‘‘kiṃ pana, pāpima, tai balībaddaihī’’ti? ‘‘mamaiva, samaṇa, cakkhu, mama rūpā, mama cakkhusamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ| kuhiṃ mai, samaṇa, gantvā maukkhasi? mamaiva, samaṇa, sautaṃ, mama saddā…pai॰… mamaiva, samaṇa, ghānaṃ, mama gandhā; mamaiva, samaṇa, jivhā, mama rasā; mamaiva, samaṇa, kāyau, mama phauṭṭhabbā; mamaiva, samaṇa, manau, mama dhammā, mama manausamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ| kuhiṃ mai, samaṇa, gantvā maukkhasī’’ti?

‘‘tavaiva, pāpima, cakkhu, tava rūpā, tava cakkhusamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ| yattha ca khau, pāpima, natthi cakkhu, natthi rūpā, natthi cakkhusamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ, agati tava tattha, pāpima| tavaiva, pāpima, sautaṃ, tava saddā, tava sautasamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ| yattha ca khau, pāpima, natthi sautaṃ, natthi saddā, natthi sautasamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ, agati tava tattha, pāpima| tavaiva, pāpima, ghānaṃ, tava gandhā, tava ghānasamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ| yattha ca khau, pāpima, natthi ghānaṃ, natthi gandhā, natthi ghānasamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ, agati tava tattha, pāpima| tavaiva, pāpima, jivhā, tava rasā, tava jivhāsamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ…pai॰… tavaiva, pāpima, kāyau, tava phauṭṭhabbā, tava kāyasamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ…pai॰… tavaiva, pāpima, manau, tava dhammā, tava manausamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ| yattha ca khau, pāpima, natthi manau, natthi dhammā, natthi manausamphassaviñ‍ñāṇāyatanaṃ, agati tava tattha, pāpimā’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ vadanti mama yidanti, yai vadanti mamanti ca|

aittha cai tai manau atthi, na mai samaṇa maukkhasī’’ti||

‘‘yaṃ vadanti na taṃ mayhaṃ, yai vadanti na tai ahaṃ|

aivaṃ pāpima jānāhi, na mai maggampi dakkhasī’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā…pai॰… tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

10. raj‍jasuttaṃ

156. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati himavantapadaisai himavantapassai (sī॰) arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau  parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘sak‍kā nu khau raj‍jaṃ kāraituṃ ahanaṃ aghātayaṃ ajinaṃ ajāpayaṃ asaucaṃ asaucāpayaṃ dhammainā’’ti?

atha khau mārau pāpimā bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kāraitu, bhantai, bhagavā raj‍jaṃ, kāraitu, sugatau, raj‍jaṃ ahanaṃ aghātayaṃ ajinaṃ ajāpayaṃ asaucaṃ asaucāpayaṃ dhammainā’’ti| ‘‘kiṃ pana mai tvaṃ, pāpima, passasi yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘kāraitu, bhantai, bhagavā raj‍jaṃ, kāraitu sugatau, raj‍jaṃ ahanaṃ aghātayaṃ ajinaṃ ajāpayaṃ asaucaṃ asaucāpayaṃ  dhammainā’’’ti? ‘‘bhagavatā khau, bhantai, cattārau iddhipādā bhāvitā bahulīkatā yānīkatā vatthukatā anuṭṭhitā paricitā susamāraddhā| ākaṅkhamānau ca, bhantai, bhagavā himavantaṃ pabbatarājaṃ suvaṇṇaṃ tvaiva adhimuc‍caiyya suvaṇṇañ‍ca panassā’’ti suvaṇṇapabbatassāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), suvaṇṇañ‍ca pabbatassāti (pī॰)|

‘‘pabbatassa  suvaṇṇassa, jātarūpassa kaivalau|

dvittāva nālamaikassa, iti vidvā samañ‍carai||

‘‘yau dukkhamaddakkhi yataunidānaṃ,

kāmaisu sau jantu kathaṃ namaiyya|

upadhiṃ viditvā saṅgauti laukai,

tassaiva jantu vinayāya sikkhai’’ti||

atha khau  mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ bhagavā, jānāti maṃ sugatau’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

pāsāṇau sīhau sakalikaṃ sakkhalikaṃ (ka॰), patirūpañ‍ca mānasaṃ|

pattaṃ āyatanaṃ piṇḍaṃ, kassakaṃ raj‍jaina tai dasāti||

3. tatiyavaggau

1. sambahulasuttaṃ

157. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati silāvatiyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavatau avidūrai appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharanti| atha khau mārau pāpimā brāhmaṇavaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā mahantaina jaṭaṇḍuvaina ajinakkhipanivatthau jiṇṇau gaupānasivaṅkau ghurughurupassāsī udumbaradaṇḍaṃ gahaitvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘daharā bhavantau  pabbajitā susū kāḷakaisā bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā anik‍kīḷitāvinau kāmaisu| bhuñ‍jantu bhavantau mānusakai kāmai| mā sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvitthā’’ti| ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvāma| kālikañ‍ca khau mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, hitvā sandiṭṭhikaṃ anudhāvāma| kālikā hi, brāhmaṇa, kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| sandiṭṭhikau ayaṃ dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti| aivaṃ  vuttai, mārau pāpimā sīsaṃ aukampaitvā jivhaṃ nil‍lālaitvā tivisākhaṃ nalāṭai nalāṭikaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā daṇḍamaulubbha pak‍kāmi|

atha  khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau avidūrai appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharāma| atha khau, bhantai, añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇau mahantaina jaṭaṇḍuvaina ajinakkhipanivatthau jiṇṇau gaupānasivaṅkau ghurughurupassāsī udumbaradaṇḍaṃ gahaitvā yaina mayaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā amhai aitadavauca – ‘daharā bhavantau pabbajitā susū kāḷakaisā bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatā paṭhamaina vayasā anik‍kīḷitāvinau kāmaisu| bhuñ‍jantu bhavantau mānusakai kāmai| mā sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvitthā’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, mayaṃ, bhantai, taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucumha – ‘na khau mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikaṃ hitvā kālikaṃ anudhāvāma| kālikañ‍ca khau mayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, hitvā sandiṭṭhikaṃ anudhāvāma| kālikā hi, brāhmaṇa, kāmā  vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| sandiṭṭhikau ayaṃ dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sau brāhmaṇau sīsaṃ aukampaitvā jivhaṃ nil‍lālaitvā tivisākhaṃ nalāṭai nalāṭikaṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā daṇḍamaulubbha pak‍kantau’’ti|

‘‘naisau, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇau| mārau aisau pāpimā tumhākaṃ vicakkhukammāya āgatau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā  tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘yau  dukkhamaddakkhi yataunidānaṃ,

kāmaisu sau jantu kathaṃ namaiyya|

upadhiṃ viditvā saṅgauti laukai,

tassaiva jantu vinayāya sikkhai’’ti||

2. samiddhisuttaṃ

158. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati silāvatiyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā samiddhi bhagavatau avidūrai appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharati| atha khau āyasmatau samiddhissa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yassa mai satthā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yvāhaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitau| lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yassa mai sabrahmacārinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā āyasmatau samiddhissa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya yaināyasmā samiddhi tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatau samiddhissa avidūrai mahantaṃ bhayabhairavaṃ saddamakāsi, apissudaṃ pathavī mañ‍ñai undrīyati|

atha khau āyasmā samiddhi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau  āyasmā samiddhi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau avidūrai appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa  paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yassa mai satthā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yvāhaṃ aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitau| lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yassa mai sabrahmacārinau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, avidūrai mahābhayabhairavasaddau ahausi, apissudaṃ pathavī mañ‍ñai undrīyatī’’ti|

‘‘naisā, samiddhi, pathavī undrīyati| mārau aisau pāpimā tuyhaṃ vicakkhukammāya āgatau| gaccha tvaṃ, samiddhi, tatthaiva appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā samiddhi bhagavatau paṭissutvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā samiddhi tatthaiva appamattau ātāpī pahitattau vihāsi| dutiyampi khau āyasmatau samiddhissa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa…pai॰… dutiyampi khau mārau pāpimā āyasmatau samiddhissa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya…pai॰… apissudaṃ pathavī mañ‍ñai undrīyati| atha khau āyasmā samiddhi māraṃ  pāpimantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘saddhāyāhaṃ pabbajitau, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ|

sati pañ‍ñā ca mai buddhā, cittañ‍ca susamāhitaṃ|

kāmaṃ karassu rūpāni, naiva maṃ byādhayissasī’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ samiddhi bhikkhū’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

3. gaudhikasuttaṃ

159. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā gaudhikau isigilipassai viharati kāḷasilāyaṃ| atha khau āyasmā gaudhikau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau sāmayikaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ phusi| atha khau āyasmā gaudhikau tamhā sāmayikāya caitauvimuttiyā parihāyi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau sāmayikaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ phusi| dutiyampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau tamhā sāmayikāya caitauvimuttiyā parihāyi| tatiyampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau sāmayikaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ phusi| tatiyampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau tamhā…pai॰… parihāyi| catutthampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau appamattau…pai॰… vimuttiṃ phusi| catutthampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau tamhā…pai॰… parihāyi| pañ‍camampi  khau āyasmā gaudhikau…pai॰… caitauvimuttiṃ phusi| pañ‍camampi khau āyasmā…pai॰… vimuttiyā parihāyi| chaṭṭhampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau sāmayikaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ phusi| chaṭṭhampi khau āyasmā gaudhikau tamhā sāmayikāya caitauvimuttiyā parihāyi| sattamampi  khau āyasmā gaudhikau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau sāmayikaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ phusi|

atha khau āyasmatau gaudhikassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yāva chaṭṭhaṃ khvāhaṃ sāmayikāya caitauvimuttiyā parihīnau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ satthaṃ āharaiyya’’nti| atha khau mārau pāpimā āyasmatau gaudhikassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘mahāvīra mahāpañ‍ña, iddhiyā yasasā jala|

sabbavairabhayātīta, pādai vandāmi cakkhuma||

‘‘sāvakau tai mahāvīra, maraṇaṃ maraṇābhibhū|

ākaṅkhati caitayati, taṃ nisaidha jutindhara||

‘‘kathañhi bhagavā tuyhaṃ, sāvakau sāsanai ratau|

appattamānasau saikkhau, kālaṃ kayirā janaisutā’’ti||

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau gaudhikaina satthaṃ āharitaṃ hauti| atha khau bhagavā ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ  pāpimantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘aivañhi  dhīrā kubbanti, nāvakaṅkhanti jīvitaṃ|

samūlaṃ taṇhamabbuyha, gaudhikau parinibbutau’’ti||

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāma, bhikkhavai, yaina isigilipassaṃ kāḷasilā tainupasaṅkamissāma yattha gaudhikaina kulaputtaina satthaṃ āharita’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina isigilipassaṃ kāḷasilā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ gaudhikaṃ dūratauva mañ‍cakai vivattakkhandhaṃ saimānaṃ saiyyamānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), sauppamānaṃ (ka॰)| taina  khau pana samayaina dhūmāyitattaṃ timirāyitattaṃ gacchataiva purimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati pacchimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uttaraṃ disaṃ, gacchati dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uddhaṃ, gacchati adhau, gacchati anudisaṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ dhūmāyitattaṃ timirāyitattaṃ gacchataiva purimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati pacchimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uttaraṃ disaṃ, gacchati dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uddhaṃ, gacchati adhau, gacchati anudisa’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aisau khau, bhikkhavai, mārau pāpimā gaudhikassa kulaputtassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanvaisati – ‘kattha gaudhikassa kulaputtassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ patiṭṭhita’nti? appatiṭṭhitaina ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaina gaudhikau kulaputtau parinibbutau’’ti| atha  khau mārau pāpimā bailuvapaṇḍuvīṇaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘uddhaṃ adhau ca tiriyaṃ, disā anudisā svahaṃ|

anvaisaṃ nādhigacchāmi, gaudhikau sau kuhiṃ gatau’’ti||

‘‘yau sau (sī॰ pī॰) dhīrau dhitisampan‍nau, jhāyī jhānaratau sadā|

ahaurattaṃ anuyuñ‍jaṃ, jīvitaṃ anikāmayaṃ||

‘‘jaitvāna  mac‍cunau bhaitvā namucinau (sī॰) sainaṃ, anāgantvā punabbhavaṃ|

samūlaṃ taṇhamabbuyha, gaudhikau parinibbutau’’ti||

‘‘tassa saukaparaitassa, vīṇā kacchā abhassatha|

tatau sau dummanau yakkhau, tatthaivantaradhāyathā’’ti tatthaivantaradhāyithāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tatthaiva antaradhāyīti (ka॰)||

4. sattavassānubandhasuttaṃ

160. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai| taina khau pana samayaina mārau pāpimā sattavassāni bhagavantaṃ anubandhau hauti autārāpaikkhau autāraṃ alabhamānau| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘saukāvatiṇṇau  nu vanamhi jhāyasi,

vittaṃ nu jīnau uda patthayānau|

āguṃ nu gāmasmimakāsi kiñ‍ci,

kasmā  janaina na karausi sakkhiṃ|

sakkhī na sampaj‍jati kainaci tai’’ti||

‘‘saukassa  mūlaṃ palikhāya sabbaṃ,

anāgu jhāyāmi asaucamānau|

chaitvāna sabbaṃ bhavalaubhajappaṃ,

anāsavau jhāyāmi pamattabandhū’’ti||

‘‘yaṃ vadanti mama yidanti, yai vadanti mamanti ca|

aittha cai tai manau atthi, na mai samaṇa maukkhasī’’ti||

‘‘yaṃ  vadanti na taṃ mayhaṃ, yai vadanti na tai ahaṃ|

aivaṃ pāpima jānāhi, na mai maggampi dakkhasī’’ti||

‘‘sacai maggaṃ anubuddhaṃ, khaimaṃ amatagāminaṃ|

apaihi gaccha tvamaivaikau, kimañ‍ñamanusāsasī’’ti||

‘‘amac‍cudhaiyyaṃ pucchanti, yai janā pāragāminau|

taisāhaṃ puṭṭhau akkhāmi, yaṃ sac‍caṃ taṃ nirūpadhi’’nti||

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai paukkharaṇī| tatrassa kak‍kaṭakau| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā kumārakā vā kumārikāyau vā tamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā nikkhamitvā yaina sā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkamaiyyuṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ kak‍kaṭakaṃ udakā uddharitvā thalai patiṭṭhapaiyyuṃ| yaṃ yadaiva hi sau, bhantai, kak‍kaṭakau aḷaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiyya taṃ tadaiva tai kumārakā vā  kumārikāyau vā kaṭṭhaina vā kathalāya vā sañchindaiyyuṃ sambhañ‍jaiyyuṃ sampalibhañ‍jaiyyuṃ| aivañhi sau, bhantai, kak‍kaṭakau sabbaihi aḷaihi sañchin‍naihi sambhaggaihi sampalibhaggaihi abhabbau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ autarituṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, yāni kānici visūkāyikāni yāni visukāyikāni (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) visaivitāni vipphanditāni, sabbāni tāni kānici kānici sabbāni (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhagavatā sañchin‍nāni sambhaggāni  sampalibhaggāni| abhabbau dānāhaṃ, bhantai, puna bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamituṃ yadidaṃ autārāpaikkhau’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā bhagavatau santikai imā nibbaijanīyā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘maidavaṇṇañ‍ca pāsāṇaṃ, vāyasau anupariyagā|

apaittha muduṃ vindaima, api assādanā siyā||

‘‘aladdhā  tattha assādaṃ, vāyasaittau apak‍kamai|

kākauva sailamāsaj‍ja, nibbij‍jāpaima gautamā’’ti||

5. māradhītusuttaṃ

161. atha  khau mārau pāpimā bhagavatau santikai imā nibbaijanīyā gāthāyau abhāsitvā tamhā ṭhānā apak‍kamma bhagavatau avidūrai pathaviyaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisīdi tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau kaṭṭhaina bhūmiṃ vilikhantau| atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau yaina mārau pāpimā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāya  ajjhabhāsiṃsu –

‘‘kaināsi dummanau tāta, purisaṃ kaṃ nu saucasi|

mayaṃ taṃ rāgapāsaina, ārañ‍ñamiva kuñ‍jaraṃ|

bandhitvā ānayissāma, vasagau tai bhavissatī’’ti||

‘‘arahaṃ sugatau laukai, na rāgaina suvānayau|

māradhaiyyaṃ atik‍kantau, tasmā saucāmahaṃ bhusa’’nti||

atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pādai tai, samaṇa, paricāraimā’’ti| atha khau bhagavā na manasākāsi, yathā taṃ anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau|

atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau aikamantaṃ apak‍kamma aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘‘uc‍cāvacā khau purisānaṃ adhippāyā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ aikasataṃ aikasataṃ kumārivaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminaiyyāmā’’ti| atha  khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau aikasataṃ aikasataṃ kumārivaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pādai tai, samaṇa, paricāraimā’’ti| tampi bhagavā na manasākāsi, yathā taṃ anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau|

atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau aikamantaṃ apak‍kamma aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘‘uc‍cāvacā khau purisānaṃ adhippāyā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ aikasataṃ aikasataṃ avijātavaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminaiyyāmā’’ti| atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau aikasataṃ aikasataṃ  avijātavaṇṇasataṃ  abhinimminitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pādai tai, samaṇa, paricāraimā’’ti| tampi bhagavā na manasākāsi, yathā taṃ anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau|

atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… yaṃnūna mayaṃ aikasataṃ aikasataṃ sakiṃ vijātavaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminaiyyāmāti| atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… sakiṃ vijātavaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘pādai tai, samaṇa, paricāraimā’’ti| tampi bhagavā na manasākāsi, yathā taṃ anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau|

atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… yaṃnūna mayaṃ aikasataṃ aikasataṃ duvijātavaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminaiyyāmāti| atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… duvijātavaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminitvā yaina bhagavā…pai॰… yathā taṃ anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau| atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… majjhimitthivaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminaiyyāmāti| atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… majjhimitthivaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminitvā…pai॰… anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau|

atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… mahitthivaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminaiyyāmāti| atha khau taṇhā ca…pai॰… mahitthivaṇṇasataṃ abhinimminitvā yaina bhagavā…pai॰… anuttarai upadhisaṅkhayai vimuttau| atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau aikamantaṃ apak‍kamma aitadavaucuṃ – sac‍caṃ kira nau pitā avauca –

‘‘arahaṃ sugatau laukai, na rāgaina suvānayau|

māradhaiyyaṃ atik‍kantau, tasmā saucāmahaṃ bhusa’’nti||

‘‘yañhi mayaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā avītarāgaṃ iminā upak‍kamaina upak‍kamaiyyāma hadayaṃ vāssa phalaiyya, uṇhaṃ lauhitaṃ vā mukhatau uggacchaiyya, ummādaṃ  vā pāpuṇaiyya cittakkhaipaṃ  vā| saiyyathā vā pana naḷau haritau lutau ussussati visussati milāyati; aivamaiva ussussaiyya visussaiyya milāyaiyyā’’ti|

atha  khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau taṇhā māradhītā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘saukāvatiṇṇau nu vanamhi jhāyasi,

vittaṃ nu jīnau uda patthayānau|

āguṃ nu gāmasmimakāsi kiñ‍ci,

kasmā janaina na karausi sakkhiṃ|

sakkhī na sampaj‍jati kainaci tai’’ti||

‘‘atthassa  pattiṃ hadayassa santiṃ,

jaitvāna sainaṃ piyasātarūpaṃ|

aikauhaṃ aikāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) jhāyaṃ sukhamanubaudhiṃ,

tasmā janaina na karaumi sakkhiṃ|

sakkhī na sampaj‍jati kainaci mai’’ti||

atha khau arati arati ca (ka॰) māradhītā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kathaṃ vihārībahulaudha bhikkhu,

pañ‍caughatiṇṇau atarīdha chaṭṭhaṃ|

kathaṃ jhāyiṃ kathaṃ jhāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), kathajjhāyaṃ (ka॰) bahulaṃ kāmasañ‍ñā,

paribāhirā haunti aladdha yau ta’’nti||

‘‘passaddhakāyau suvimuttacittau,

asaṅkharānau satimā anaukau|

añ‍ñāya  dhammaṃ avitak‍kajhāyī,

na kuppati na sarati na thinau na kuppatī nassaratī na thīnau (sī॰)||

‘‘aivaṃvihārībahulaudha bhikkhu,

pañ‍caughatiṇṇau atarīdha chaṭṭhaṃ|

aivaṃ jhāyiṃ bahulaṃ kāmasañ‍ñā,

paribāhirā haunti aladdha yau ta’’nti||

atha  khau ragā ragāca (ka॰) māradhītā bhagavatau santikai gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘acchaij‍ja  taṇhaṃ gaṇasaṅghacārī,

addhā carissanti tarissanti (sī॰) bahū ca saddhā|

bahuṃ vatāyaṃ janataṃ anaukau,

acchaij‍ja naissati mac‍curājassa pāra’’nti||

‘‘nayanti vai mahāvīrā, saddhammaina tathāgatā|

dhammaina nayamānānaṃ, kā usūyā vijānata’’nti||

atha khau taṇhā ca arati ca ragā ca māradhītarau yaina mārau pāpimā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasā khau mārau pāpimā taṇhañ‍ca aratiñ‍ca ragañ‍ca māradhītarau dūratauva āgacchantiyau| disvāna gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘bālā kumudanāḷaihi, pabbataṃ abhimatthatha abhimanthatha (sī॰)|

giriṃ nakhaina khanatha, ayau dantaihi khādatha||

‘‘sailaṃva sirasūhac‍ca sirasi ūhac‍ca (sī॰), sirasi auhac‍ca (syā॰ kaṃ॰), pātālai gādhamaisatha|

khāṇuṃva urasāsaj‍ja, nibbij‍jāpaitha gautamā’’ti||

‘‘daddal‍lamānā  āgañchuṃ, taṇhā ca aratī ragā|

tā tattha panudī satthā, tūlaṃ bhaṭṭhaṃva mālutau’’ti||

tatiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sambahulā  samiddhi ca, gaudhikaṃ sattavassāni|

dhītaraṃ daisitaṃ buddha, saiṭṭhaina imaṃ mārapañ‍cakanti||

mārasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



5. bhikkhunīsaṃyuttaṃ

1. āḷavikāsuttaṃ

162. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āḷavikā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami vivaikatthinī| atha khau mārau pāpimā āḷavikāya bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau vivaikamhā cāvaitukāmau yaina āḷavikā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āḷavikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘natthi nissaraṇaṃ laukai, kiṃ vivaikaina kāhasi|

bhuñ‍jassu kāmaratiyau, māhu pacchānutāpinī’’ti||

atha khau āḷavikāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau āḷavikāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau vivaikamhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| atha khau āḷavikā bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ  gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘atthi nissaraṇaṃ laukai, pañ‍ñāya mai suphussitaṃ suphassitaṃ (sī॰ pī॰)|

pamattabandhu pāpima, na tvaṃ jānāsi taṃ padaṃ||

‘‘sattisūlūpamā  kāmā, khandhāsaṃ adhikuṭṭanā|

yaṃ tvaṃ kāmaratiṃ brūsi, arati mayha sā ahū’’ti||

atha  khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ āḷavikā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

2. saumāsuttaṃ

163. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau saumā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā saumāya bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau yaina saumā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā saumaṃ bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘yaṃ taṃ isīhi pattabbaṃ, ṭhānaṃ durabhisambhavaṃ|

na taṃ dvaṅgulapañ‍ñāya, sak‍kā pappautumitthiyā’’ti||

atha khau saumāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau saumāya bhikkhuniyā  aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| atha khau saumā bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘itthibhāvau kiṃ kayirā, cittamhi susamāhitai|

ñāṇamhi vattamānamhi, sammā dhammaṃ vipassatau||

‘‘yassa  nūna siyā aivaṃ, itthāhaṃ purisauti vā|

kiñ‍ci vā pana añ‍ñasmi asmīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), taṃ mārau vattumarahatī’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ saumā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

3. kisāgautamīsuttaṃ

164. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau kisāgautamī bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā  yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami, divāvihārāya| andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā kisāgautamiyā bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau yaina kisāgautamī bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā kisāgautamiṃ bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃ  nu tvaṃ mataputtāva, aikamāsi rudammukhī|

vanamajjhagatā aikā, purisaṃ nu gavaisasī’’ti||

atha khau kisāgautamiyā bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau kisāgautamiyā bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti|

atha khau kisāgautamī bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘ac‍cantaṃ  mataputtāmhi, purisā aitadantikā|

na saucāmi na raudāmi, na taṃ bhāyāmi āvusau||

‘‘sabbattha vihatā nandī, tamaukkhandhau padālitau|

jaitvāna mac‍cunau jaitvā namucinau (sī॰) sainaṃ, viharāmi anāsavā’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ kisāgautamī bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

4. vijayāsuttaṃ

165. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau vijayā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā vijayāya bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau yaina vijayā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vijayaṃ bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘daharā  tvaṃ rūpavatī, ahañ‍ca daharau susu|

pañ‍caṅgikaina turiyaina, aihayyaibhiramāmasai’’ti aihi ayyai ramāmasaiti (sī॰)||

atha khau vijayāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau vijayāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| atha khau vijayā bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘rūpā saddā rasā gandhā, phauṭṭhabbā ca manauramā|

niyyātayāmi tuyhaiva, māra nāhaṃ tainatthikā||

‘‘iminā  pūtikāyaina, bhindanaina pabhaṅgunā|

aṭṭīyāmi harāyāmi, kāmataṇhā samūhatā||

‘‘yai ca rūpūpagā sattā, yai ca arūpaṭṭhāyinau āruppaṭṭhāyinau (sī॰ pī॰)|

yā ca santā samāpatti, sabbattha vihatau tamau’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ vijayā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

5. uppalavaṇṇāsuttaṃ

166. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau uppalavaṇṇā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarasmiṃ supupphitasālarukkhamūlai aṭṭhāsi| atha  khau mārau pāpimā uppalavaṇṇāya bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau yaina uppalavaṇṇā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā uppalavaṇṇaṃ bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘supupphitaggaṃ upagamma bhikkhuni,

aikā tuvaṃ tiṭṭhasi sālamūlai|

na catthi tai dutiyā vaṇṇadhātu,

bālai na tvaṃ bhāyasi dhuttakāna’’nti||

atha  khau uppalavaṇṇāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau  nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau uppalavaṇṇāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| atha khau uppalavaṇṇā bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘sataṃ  sahassānipi dhuttakānaṃ,

idhāgatā tādisakā bhavaiyyuṃ|

laumaṃ na iñ‍jāmi na santasāmi,

na māra bhāyāmi tamaikikāpi||

‘‘aisā antaradhāyāmi, kucchiṃ vā pavisāmi tai|

pakhumantarikāyampi, tiṭṭhantiṃ maṃ na dakkhasi||

‘‘cittasmiṃ  vasībhūtāmhi, iddhipādā subhāvitā|

sabbabandhanamuttāmhi, na taṃ bhāyāmi āvusau’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ uppalavaṇṇā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

6. cālāsuttaṃ

167. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau cālā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina cālā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā cālaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu tvaṃ, bhikkhuni, na raucaisī’’ti? ‘‘jātiṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, na raucaimī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu jātiṃ na raucaisi, jātau kāmāni bhuñ‍jati|

kau nu taṃ idamādapayi, jātiṃ mā rauca mā raucaisi (sī॰ pī॰) bhikkhunī’’ti||

‘‘jātassa maraṇaṃ hauti, jātau dukkhāni phussati passati (sī॰ pī॰)|

bandhaṃ vadhaṃ parik‍laisaṃ, tasmā jātiṃ na raucayai||

‘‘buddhau  dhammamadaisaisi, jātiyā samatik‍kamaṃ|

sabbadukkhappahānāya, sau maṃ sac‍cai nivaisayi||

‘‘yai  ca rūpūpagā sattā, yai ca arūpaṭṭhāyinau|

niraudhaṃ appajānantā, āgantārau punabbhava’’nti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ cālā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

7. upacālāsuttaṃ

168. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau upacālā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina upacālā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā upacālaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kattha nu tvaṃ, bhikkhuni, uppaj‍jitukāmā’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, katthaci uppaj‍jitukāmā’’ti|

‘‘tāvatiṃsā ca yāmā ca, tusitā cāpi daivatā|

nimmānaratinau daivā, yai daivā vasavattinau|

tattha cittaṃ paṇidhaihi, ratiṃ pac‍canubhaussasī’’ti||

‘‘tāvatiṃsā ca yāmā ca, tusitā cāpi daivatā|

nimmānaratinau daivā, yai daivā vasavattinau|

kāmabandhanabaddhā tai, ainti māravasaṃ puna||

‘‘sabbau ādīpitau sabbauva ādittau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) laukau, sabbau laukau padhūpitau|

sabbau paj‍jalitau paj‍jalitau (sabbattha) laukau, sabbau laukau pakampitau||

‘‘akampitaṃ apaj‍jalitaṃ acalitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), aputhuj‍janasaivitaṃ|

agati yattha mārassa, tattha mai niratau manau’’ti||

atha  khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ upacālā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

8. sīsupacālāsuttaṃ

169. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau sīsupacālā sīsūpacālā (sī॰) bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā  …pai॰… añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā yaina sīsupacālā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sīsupacālaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa nu tvaṃ, bhikkhuni, pāsaṇḍaṃ raucaisī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, kassaci pāsaṇḍaṃ raucaimī’’ti|

‘‘kaṃ nu uddissa muṇḍāsi, samaṇī viya dissasi|

na ca raucaisi pāsaṇḍaṃ, kimiva carasi maumūhā’’ti||

‘‘itau bahiddhā pāsaṇḍā, diṭṭhīsu pasīdanti tai|

na taisaṃ dhammaṃ raucaimi, tai dhammassa akauvidā||

‘‘atthi  sakyakulai jātau, buddhau appaṭipuggalau|

sabbābhibhū māranudau, sabbatthamaparājitau||

‘‘sabbattha muttau asitau, sabbaṃ passati cakkhumā|

sabbakammakkhayaṃ pattau, vimuttau upadhisaṅkhayai|

sau mayhaṃ bhagavā satthā, tassa raucaimi sāsana’’nti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ sīsupacālā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

9. sailāsuttaṃ

170. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau sailā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā…pai॰… añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā sailāya bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau…pai॰… sailaṃ  bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kainidaṃ pakataṃ bimbaṃ, k‍vanu k‍van‍nu (sī॰ pī॰), k‍vaci (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) bimbassa kārakau|

k‍vanu bimbaṃ samuppan‍naṃ, k‍vanu bimbaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti||

atha khau sailāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau sailāya bhikkhuniyā  aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| atha khau sailā bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘nayidaṃ attakataṃ nayidaṃ pakataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bimbaṃ, nayidaṃ parakataṃ nayidaṃ pakataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aghaṃ|

haituṃ paṭic‍ca sambhūtaṃ, haitubhaṅgā nirujjhati||

‘‘yathā añ‍ñataraṃ bījaṃ, khaittai vuttaṃ virūhati|

pathavīrasañ‍cāgamma, sinaihañ‍ca tadūbhayaṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ khandhā ca dhātuyau, cha ca āyatanā imai|

haituṃ paṭic‍ca sambhūtā, haitubhaṅgā nirujjharai’’ti||

atha khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ sailā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

10. vajirāsuttaṃ

171. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau vajirā bhikkhunī pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantā  yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau mārau pāpimā vajirāya bhikkhuniyā bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau yaina vajirā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vajiraṃ bhikkhuniṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kaināyaṃ pakatau sattau, kuvaṃ sattassa kārakau|

kuvaṃ sattau samuppan‍nau, kuvaṃ sattau nirujjhatī’’ti||

atha khau vajirāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khvāyaṃ manussau vā amanussau vā gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti? atha khau vajirāya bhikkhuniyā aitadahausi – ‘‘mārau khau ayaṃ pāpimā mama bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau samādhimhā cāvaitukāmau gāthaṃ bhāsatī’’ti| atha khau  vajirā bhikkhunī ‘‘mārau ayaṃ pāpimā’’ iti viditvā, māraṃ pāpimantaṃ gāthāhi pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃ nu sattauti pac‍caisi, māra diṭṭhigataṃ nu tai|

suddhasaṅkhārapuñ‍jauyaṃ, nayidha sattupalabbhati||

‘‘yathā hi aṅgasambhārā, hauti saddau rathau iti|

aivaṃ khandhaisu santaisu, hauti sattauti sammuti sammati (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

‘‘dukkhamaiva  hi sambhauti, dukkhaṃ tiṭṭhati vaiti ca|

nāñ‍ñatra dukkhā sambhauti, nāñ‍ñaṃ dukkhā nirujjhatī’’ti||

atha  khau mārau pāpimā ‘‘jānāti maṃ vajirā bhikkhunī’’ti dukkhī dummanau tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

bhikkhunīsaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

āḷavikā ca saumā ca, gautamī vijayā saha|

uppalavaṇṇā ca cālā, upacālā sīsupacālā ca|

sailā vajirāya tai dasāti||


6. brahmasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. brahmāyācanasuttaṃ

172. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmāya khau pana pajāya ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppādau| idampi khau ṭhānaṃ duddasaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ; sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’’ti| apissu bhagavantaṃ imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘‘paṭisautagāmiṃ  nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’’ti tamaukkhandhaina āvutāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

itiha  bhagavatau paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati, nau dhammadaisanāya|

atha  khau brahmunau sahampatissa bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya aitadahausi – ‘‘nassati vata bhau laukau, vinassati vata bhau laukau, yatra hi nāma tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati namissati (?), nau dhammadaisanāyā’’ti| atha  khau brahmā sahampati – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ pathaviyaṃ nihantvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti| bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pāturahausi magadhaisu pubbai,

dhammau asuddhau samalaihi cintitau|

apāpuraitaṃ avāpuraitaṃ (sī॰) amatassa dvāraṃ,

suṇantu  dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhaṃ||

‘‘sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau,

yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumaidha,

pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu|

saukāvatiṇṇaṃ saukāvakiṇṇaṃ (sī॰) janatamapaitasaukau,

avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ||

‘‘uṭṭhaihi vīra vijitasaṅgāma,

satthavāha anaṇa aṇaṇa (rūpasiddhiṭīkā) vicara laukai|

daisassu daisaitu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhagavā dhammaṃ,

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’’ti||

atha  khau bhagavā brahmunau ca ajjhaisanaṃ viditvā sattaisu ca kāruñ‍ñataṃ paṭic‍ca buddhacakkhunā  laukaṃ vaulaukaisi| addasā khau bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai  dassāvinau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) viharantai| saiyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antau nimuggapausīni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā  padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni samaudakaṃ ṭhitāni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakā ac‍cuggamma ṭhitāni tiṭṭhanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) anupalittāni udakaina; aivamaiva bhagavā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau addasa sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai| disvāna brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘apārutā taisaṃ amatassa dvārā,

yai sautavantau pamuñ‍cantu saddhaṃ|

vihiṃsasañ‍ñī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ,

dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujaisu brahmai’’ti||

atha khau brahmā sahampati ‘‘katāvakāsau khaumhi bhagavatā dhammadaisanāyā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

2. gāravasuttaṃ

173. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| atha  khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘dukkhaṃ khau agāravau viharati appatissau, kaṃ nu khvāhaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā garukatvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) upanissāya viharaiyya’’nti?

atha  khau bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘aparipuṇṇassa khau sīlakkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya attanā sīlasampan‍nataraṃ añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipuṇṇassa khau samādhikkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai…pai॰… attanā samādhisampan‍nataraṃ añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipuṇṇassa pañ‍ñākkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai…pai॰… attanā pañ‍ñāsampan‍nataraṃ añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipuṇṇassa khau vimuttikkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai…pai॰… attanā vimuttisampan‍nataraṃ añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ|

‘‘aparipuṇṇassa khau vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhassa pāripūriyā añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā  sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| na khau panāhaṃ passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya attanā vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nataraṃ añ‍ñaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, yamahaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyyaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yvāyaṃ dhammau mayā abhisambuddhau tamaiva dhammaṃ sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharaiyya’’nti|

atha khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – brahmalaukai  antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā, aivamaitaṃ, sugata! yaipi tai, bhantai, ahaisuṃ atītamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā, taipi  bhagavantau dhammañ‍ñaiva sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya vihariṃsu; yaipi tai, bhantai, bhavissanti anāgatamaddhānaṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā taipi bhagavantau dhammañ‍ñaiva sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharissanti| bhagavāpi, bhantai, aitarahi arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau dhammañ‍ñaiva sak‍katvā garuṃ katvā upanissāya viharatū’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘yai  ca atītā sambuddhā, yai ca buddhā anāgatā|

yau caitarahi sambuddhau, bahūnaṃ bahun‍naṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saukanāsanau||

‘‘sabbai saddhammagarunau, vihaṃsu vihariṃsu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) viharanti ca|

tathāpi viharissanti, aisā buddhāna dhammatā||

‘‘tasmā hi attakāmaina atthakāmaina (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), mahattamabhikaṅkhatā|

saddhammau garukātabbau, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

3. brahmadaivasuttaṃ

174. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarissā brāhmaṇiyā brahmadaivau nāma puttau bhagavatau santikai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti|

atha khau āyasmā brahmadaivau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā brahmadaivau arahataṃ ahausi|

atha khau āyasmā brahmadaivau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina sakamātu nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| taina  khau pana  samayaina āyasmatau brahmadaivassa mātā brāhmaṇī brahmunau āhutiṃ nic‍caṃ paggaṇhāti| atha khau brahmunau sahampatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmatau brahmadaivassa mātā brāhmaṇī brahmunau āhutiṃ nic‍caṃ paggaṇhāti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ upasaṅkamitvā saṃvaijaiyya’’nti| atha khau brahmā sahampati – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau āyasmatau brahmadaivassa mātu nivaisanai pāturahausi| atha khau brahmā sahampati vaihāsaṃ ṭhitau āyasmatau brahmadaivassa mātaraṃ brāhmaṇiṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘dūrai itau brāhmaṇi brahmalaukau,

yassāhutiṃ paggaṇhāsi nic‍caṃ|

naitādisau brāhmaṇi brahmabhakkhau,

kiṃ jappasi brahmapathaṃ ajānaṃ ajānantī (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)||

‘‘aisau hi tai brāhmaṇi brahmadaivau,

nirūpadhikau atidaivapattau|

akiñ‍canau bhikkhu anañ‍ñapausī,

yau tai sau tai sau (sī॰ pī॰), yau tai sa (?) piṇḍāya gharaṃ paviṭṭhau||

‘‘āhunaiyyau vaidagu bhāvitattau,

narānaṃ daivānañ‍ca dakkhiṇaiyyau|

bāhitvā pāpāni anūpalittau,

ghāsaisanaṃ  iriyati sītibhūtau||

‘‘na  tassa pacchā na puratthamatthi,

santau vidhūmau anighau nirāsau|

nikkhittadaṇḍau tasathāvaraisu,

sau tyāhutiṃ bhuñ‍jatu aggapiṇḍaṃ||

‘‘visainibhūtau upasantacittau,

nāgauva dantau carati anaijau|

bhikkhu susīlau suvimuttacittau,

sau tyāhutiṃ bhuñ‍jatu aggapiṇḍaṃ||

‘‘tasmiṃ  pasan‍nā avikampamānā,

patiṭṭhapaihi  dakkhiṇaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyai|

karauhi puñ‍ñaṃ sukhamāyatikaṃ,

disvā muniṃ brāhmaṇi aughatiṇṇa’’nti||

‘‘tasmiṃ pasan‍nā avikampamānā,

patiṭṭhapaisi dakkhiṇaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyai|

akāsi puñ‍ñaṃ sukhamāyatikaṃ,

disvā muniṃ brāhmaṇī aughatiṇṇa’’nti||

4. bakabrahmasuttaṃ

175. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bakassa brahmunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ  diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘idaṃ nic‍caṃ, idaṃ dhuvaṃ, idaṃ sassataṃ, idaṃ kaivalaṃ, idaṃ acavanadhammaṃ, idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, itau ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttariṃ uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) nissaraṇaṃ natthī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā bakassa brahmunau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| addasā khau bakau brahmā bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi khau mārisa, svāgataṃ tai, mārisa! cirassaṃ khau mārisa! imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| idañhi, mārisa, nic‍caṃ, idaṃ dhuvaṃ, idaṃ sassataṃ, idaṃ kaivalaṃ, idaṃ acavanadhammaṃ, idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati| itau ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ natthī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā bakaṃ brahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘avij‍jāgatau vata, bhau, bakau brahmā; avij‍jāgatau vata, bhau, bakau brahmā| yatra  hi nāma anic‍caṃyaiva samānaṃ nic‍canti vakkhati, adhuvaṃyaiva samānaṃ dhuvanti vakkhati, asassataṃyaiva samānaṃ sassatanti vakkhati, akaivalaṃyaiva  samānaṃ kaivalanti vakkhati, cavanadhammaṃyaiva samānaṃ acavanadhammanti vakkhati| yattha ca pana jāyati ca jīyati ca mīyati ca cavati ca upapaj‍jati ca, tañ‍ca tathā vakkhati – ‘idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na  upapaj‍jati’| santañ‍ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ, ‘natthañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇa’nti vakkhatī’’ti|

‘‘dvāsattati gautama puñ‍ñakammā,

vasavattinau jātijaraṃ atītā|

ayamantimā vaidagū brahmupapatti,

asmābhijappanti janā anaikā’’ti||

‘‘appañhi aitaṃ na hi dīghamāyu,

yaṃ tvaṃ baka mañ‍ñasi dīghamāyuṃ|

sataṃ sahassānaṃ sahassāna (syā॰ kaṃ॰) nirabbudānaṃ,

āyuṃ pajānāmi tavāhaṃ brahmai’’ti||

‘‘anantadassī bhagavāhamasmi,

jātijaraṃ saukamupātivattau|

kiṃ  mai purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ,

ācikkha mai taṃ yamahaṃ vijañ‍ñā’’ti||

‘‘yaṃ tvaṃ apāyaisi bahū manussai,

pipāsitai ghammani samparaitai|

taṃ tai purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ,

suttappabuddhauva anussarāmi||

‘‘yaṃ aiṇikūlasmiṃ janaṃ gahītaṃ,

amaucayī gayhakaṃ nīyamānaṃ|

taṃ  tai purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ,

suttappabuddhauva anussarāmi||

‘‘gaṅgāya  sautasmiṃ gahītanāvaṃ,

luddaina nāgaina manussakamyā|

pamaucayittha balasā pasayha,

taṃ tai purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ,

suttappabuddhauva anussarāmi||

‘‘kappau  ca tai baddhacarau ahausiṃ,

sambuddhimantaṃ sambuddhivantaṃ (bahūsu) vatinaṃ amañ‍ñi|

taṃ tai purāṇaṃ vatasīlavattaṃ,

suttappabuddhauva anussarāmī’’ti||

‘‘addhā pajānāsi mamaitamāyuṃ,

añ‍ñaipi añ‍ñampi (sī॰ pī॰) jānāsi tathā hi buddhau|

tathā hi tyāyaṃ jalitānubhāvau,

aubhāsayaṃ tiṭṭhati brahmalauka’’nti||

5. añ‍ñatarabrahmasuttaṃ

176. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa brahmunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘natthi sau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā yau idha āgacchaiyyā’’ti| atha  khau bhagavā tassa brahmunau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau…pai॰… tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| atha khau bhagavā tassa brahmunau upari vaihāsaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisīdi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā|

atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau bhagavā aitarahi viharatī’’ti? addasā khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) bhagavantaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina tassa brahmunau upari vaihāsaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisin‍naṃ taijaudhātuṃ samāpan‍naṃ| disvāna – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau  puratthimaṃ disaṃ nissāya upanissāya (sī॰) tassa brahmunau upari vaihāsaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisīdi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nīcataraṃ bhagavatau|

atha khau āyasmatau mahākassapassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau bhagavā aitarahi viharatī’’ti? addasā khau āyasmā mahākassapau bhagavantaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā…pai॰… disvāna – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau…pai॰… aivamaiva – jaitavanai  antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ nissāya tassa brahmunau upari vaihāsaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisīdi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nīcataraṃ bhagavatau|

atha  khau āyasmatau mahākappinassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau bhagavā aitarahi viharatī’’ti? addasā khau āyasmā mahākappinau bhagavantaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā…pai॰… taijaudhātuṃ samāpan‍naṃ| disvāna – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau…pai॰… aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā mahākappinau pacchimaṃ disaṃ nissāya tassa brahmunau upari vaihāsaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisīdi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nīcataraṃ bhagavatau|

atha  khau āyasmatau anuruddhassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau bhagavā aitarahi viharatī’’ti? addasā khau āyasmā anuruddhau…pai॰… taijaudhātuṃ samāpan‍naṃ| disvāna – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau…pai॰… tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausi| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau uttaraṃ disaṃ nissāya tassa brahmunau upari vaihāsaṃ pal‍laṅkaina nisīdi taijaudhātuṃ samāpaj‍jitvā nīcataraṃ bhagavatau|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ brahmānaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘aj‍jāpi tai āvusau sā diṭṭhi, yā tai diṭṭhi purai ahu|

passasi vītivattantaṃ, brahmalaukai pabhassara’’nti||

‘‘na mai mārisa sā diṭṭhi, yā mai diṭṭhi purai ahu|

passāmi vītivattantaṃ, brahmalaukai pabhassaraṃ|

svāhaṃ aj‍ja kathaṃ vaj‍jaṃ, ahaṃ nic‍caumhi sassatau’’ti||

atha  khau bhagavā taṃ brahmānaṃ saṃvaijaitvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – tasmiṃ brahmalaukai antarahitau jaitavanai pāturahausi| atha khau sau brahmā añ‍ñataraṃ brahmapārisaj‍jaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, mārisa, yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘atthi nu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, añ‍ñaipi tassa bhagavatau sāvakā aivaṃmahiddhikā aivaṃmahānubhāvā; saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ mauggal‍lānau kassapau kappinau anuruddhau’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, mārisā’’ti khau sau brahmapārisaj‍jau tassa brahmunau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, añ‍ñaipi tassa bhagavatau sāvakā aivaṃmahiddhikā aivaṃmahānubhāvā; saiyyathāpi bhavaṃ mauggal‍lānau kassapau kappinau anuruddhau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ brahmapārisaj‍jaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘taivij‍jā  iddhipattā ca, caitaupariyāyakauvidā|

khīṇāsavā arahantau, bahū buddhassa sāvakā’’ti||

atha  khau sau brahmapārisaj‍jau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā yaina sau brahmā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ brahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā mārisa, mahāmauggal‍lānau aivamāha –

‘‘taivij‍jā iddhipattā ca, caitaupariyāyakauvidā|

khīṇāsavā arahantau, bahū buddhassa sāvakā’’ti||

idamavauca sau brahmapārisaj‍jau| attamanau ca sau brahmā tassa brahmapārisaj‍jassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

6. brahmalaukasuttaṃ

177. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā divāvihāragatau hauti paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau subrahmā ca pac‍caikabrahmā suddhāvāsau  ca pac‍caikabrahmā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pac‍caikaṃ dvārabāhaṃ pac‍caikadvārabāhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) upanissāya aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau subrahmā pac‍caikabrahmā suddhāvāsaṃ pac‍caikabrahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘akālau khau tāva, mārisa, bhagavantaṃ payirupāsituṃ; divāvihāragatau bhagavā paṭisal‍līnau ca| asukau ca brahmalaukau iddhau caiva phītau ca, brahmā ca tatra pamādavihāraṃ viharati| āyāma, mārisa, yaina sau brahmalaukau tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ brahmānaṃ saṃvaijaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, mārisā’’ti khau suddhāvāsau pac‍caikabrahmā subrahmunau pac‍caikabrahmunau pac‍cassausi|

atha khau subrahmā ca pac‍caikabrahmā  suddhāvāsau ca pac‍caikabrahmā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau…pai॰… aivamaiva – bhagavatau puratau antarahitā tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahaisuṃ| addasā khau sau brahmā tai brahmānau dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna tai brahmānau aitadavauca – ‘‘handa kutau nu tumhai, mārisā, āgacchathā’’ti? ‘‘āgatā khau mayaṃ, mārisa, amha tassa bhagavatau  santikā arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| gacchaiyyāsi pana tvaṃ, mārisa, tassa bhagavatau upaṭṭhānaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttau aivaṃ vuttai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) khau sau brahmā taṃ vacanaṃ anadhivāsaintau sahassakkhattuṃ attānaṃ abhinimminitvā subrahmānaṃ pac‍caikabrahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘passasi mai nau tvaṃ, mārisa, aivarūpaṃ iddhānubhāva’’nti? ‘‘passāmi khau tyāhaṃ, mārisa, aivarūpaṃ iddhānubhāva’’nti| ‘‘sau khvāhaṃ, mārisa, aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau kassa añ‍ñassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā upaṭṭhānaṃ gamissāmī’’ti?

atha khau subrahmā pac‍caikabrahmā dvisahassakkhattuṃ attānaṃ abhinimminitvā taṃ brahmānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘passasi mai nau tvaṃ, mārisa, aivarūpaṃ iddhānubhāva’’nti? ‘‘passāmi khau tyāhaṃ, mārisa, aivarūpaṃ iddhānubhāva’’nti| ‘‘tayā ca khau, mārisa, mayā ca svaiva bhagavā mahiddhikatarau caiva mahānubhāvatarau ca| gacchaiyyāsi tvaṃ, mārisa, tassa bhagavatau upaṭṭhānaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti? atha  khau sau brahmā subrahmānaṃ pac‍caikabrahmānaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tayau  supaṇṇā caturau ca haṃsā,

byagghīnisā pañ‍casatā ca jhāyinau|

tayidaṃ vimānaṃ jalatai ca jalataiva (pī॰ ka॰) brahmai,

aubhāsayaṃ uttarassaṃ disāya’’nti||

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi tai taṃ jalatai vimānaṃ,

aubhāsayaṃ uttarassaṃ disāyaṃ|

rūpai raṇaṃ disvā sadā pavaidhitaṃ,

tasmā na rūpai ramatī sumaidhau’’ti||

atha khau subrahmā ca pac‍caikabrahmā suddhāvāsau ca pac‍caikabrahmā taṃ brahmānaṃ saṃvaijaitvā tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsu| agamāsi ca khau sau brahmā aparaina samayaina bhagavatau upaṭṭhānaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassāti|

7. kaukālikasuttaṃ

178. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā divāvihāragatau hauti paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau subrahmā ca pac‍caikabrahmā suddhāvāsau ca pac‍caikabrahmā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pac‍caikaṃ dvārabāhaṃ nissāya aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau subrahmā pac‍caikabrahmā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha  bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘appamaiyyaṃ paminantau, kaudha vidvā vikappayai|

appamaiyyaṃ pamāyinaṃ, nivutaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai puthuj‍jana’’nti||

8. katamaudakatissasuttaṃ

179. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā divāvihāragatau hauti paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau subrahmā ca pac‍caikabrahmā suddhāvāsau ca pac‍caikabrahmā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pac‍caikaṃ dvārabāhaṃ nissāya aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau suddhāvāsau pac‍caikabrahmā katamaudakatissakaṃ katamaurakatissakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhikkhuṃ ārabbha bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘appamaiyyaṃ  paminantau, kaudha vidvā vikappayai|

appamaiyyaṃ pamāyinaṃ, nivutaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai akissava’’nti||

9. turūbrahmasuttaṃ

180. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina kaukālikau bhikkhu ābādhikau hauti dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau turū tudu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pac‍caikabrahmā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina kaukālikau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā vaihāsaṃ  ṭhitau kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti| ‘‘kausi tvaṃ, āvusau’’ti? ‘‘ahaṃ turū pac‍caikabrahmā’’ti| ‘‘nanu tvaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā  anāgāmī byākatau, atha kiñ‍carahi idhāgatau? passa, yāvañ‍ca tai idaṃ aparaddha’’nti|

‘‘purisassa hi jātassa, kuṭhārī dudhārī (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ, bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati,

taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ,

kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

‘‘appamattakau ayaṃ kali,

yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā,

ayamaiva mahantatarau kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ,

chattiṃsati pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī yamariyai garahī (syā॰ kaṃ॰), yamariyaṃ garahaṃ (ka॰) nirayaṃ upaiti,

vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti||

10. kaukālikasuttaṃ

181. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kaukālikau bhikkhu  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pāpicchā, bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānā pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| aivaṃ  vuttai, bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, avaca; mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, avaca| pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti| dutiyampi khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi mai, bhantai, bhagavā saddhāyikau pac‍cayikau; atha khau pāpicchāva bhantai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānā pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, avaca; mā haivaṃ, kaukālika, avaca| pasādaihi, kaukālika, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ| paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti| tatiyampi khau kaukālikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi…pai॰… icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatā’’ti| tatiyampi khau bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā haivaṃ…pai॰… paisalā sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’’ti|

atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| acirapak‍kantassa ca kaukālikassa bhikkhunau sāsapamattīhi pīḷakāhi piḷakāhi (sī॰ pī॰) sabbau kāyau phuṭau ahausi| sāsapamattiyau hutvā muggamattiyau ahaisuṃ, muggamattiyau hutvā kalāyamattiyau ahaisuṃ, kalāyamattiyau hutvā kaulaṭṭhimattiyau ahaisuṃ, kaulaṭṭhimattiyau hutvā kaulamattiyau ahaisuṃ, kaulamattiyau hutvā āmalakamattiyau ahaisuṃ, āmalakamattiyau hutvā bailuvasalāṭukamattiyau ahaisuṃ, bailuvasalāṭukamattiyau hutvā bil‍lamattiyau ahaisuṃ, bil‍lamattiyau  hutvā pabhij‍jiṃsu| pubbañ‍ca lauhitañ‍ca pagghariṃsu| atha khau kaukālikau bhikkhu tainaiva ābādhaina kālamakāsi| kālaṅkatau ca kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍ji sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā|

atha  khau brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau brahmā sahampati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaukālikau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau| kālaṅkatau ca, bhantai, kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| idamavauca brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

atha  khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘imaṃ, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kaukālikau, bhantai, bhikkhu kālaṅkatau| kālaṅkatau ca, bhantai, kaukālikau bhikkhu padumaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati, idaṃ vatvā maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kīvadīghaṃ nu khau, bhantai, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇa’’nti? ‘‘dīghaṃ khau, bhikkhu, padumai nirayai āyuppamāṇaṃ| taṃ na sukaraṃ saṅkhātuṃ – aittakāni vassāni iti vā, aittakāni vassasatāni iti vā, aittakāni vassasahassāni iti vā, aittakāni vassasatasahassāni iti vā’’ti| ‘‘sak‍kā pana, bhantai, upamaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘sak‍kā, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau| tatau purisau vassasatassa vassasatassa ac‍cayaina aikamaikaṃ tilaṃ uddharaiyya; khippataraṃ khau sau, bhikkhu, vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau iminā upak‍kamaina parikkhayaṃ pariyādānaṃ gacchaiyya, na tvaiva aikau abbudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati abbudā nirayā, aivamaikau nirabbudanirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati nirabbudā nirayā, aivamaikau ababau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ababā nirayā, aivamaikau  aṭaṭau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati aṭaṭā nirayā, aivamaikau ahahau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ahahā nirayā, aivamaikau kumudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati kumudā nirayā, aivamaikau saugandhikau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati saugandhikā nirayā, aivamaikau uppalanirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati uppalā nirayā, aivamaikau puṇḍarikau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati puṇḍarikā nirayā, aivamaikau padumau nirayau| padumai pana, bhikkhu, nirayai kaukālikau bhikkhu upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘purisassa  hi jātassa,

kuṭhārī jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ,

bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati,

taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ,

kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

‘‘appamattakau ayaṃ kali,

yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā,

ayamaiva mahantarau kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ,

chattiṃsati pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī  nirayaṃ upaiti,

vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti||

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āyācanaṃ  gāravau brahmadaivau,

bakau ca brahmā aparā ca diṭṭhi|

pamādakaukālikatissakau ca,

turū ca brahmā aparau ca kaukālikauti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. sanaṅkumārasuttaṃ

182. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati sappinītīrai| atha khau brahmā sanaṅkumārau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ sappinītīraṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau brahmā sanaṅkumārau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘khattiyau saiṭṭhau janaitasmiṃ, yai gauttapaṭisārinau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, sau saiṭṭhau daivamānusai’’ti||

idamavauca brahmā sanaṅkumārau| samanuñ‍ñau satthā ahausi| atha khau brahmā sanaṅkumārau ‘‘samanuñ‍ñau mai satthā’’ti bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyīti|

2. daivadattasuttaṃ

183. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai acirapak‍kantai daivadattai| atha khau brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ gijjhakūṭaṃ  pabbataṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ  ṭhitau khau brahmā sahampati daivadattaṃ ārabbha bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘phalaṃ  vai kadaliṃ hanti, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷaṃ|

sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hanti, gabbhau assatariṃ yathā’’ti||

3. andhakavindasuttaṃ

184. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā māgadhaisu viharati andhakavindai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti, daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusāyati| atha khau brahmā sahampati abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇau kaivalakappaṃ andhakavindaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘saivaitha pantāni saināsanāni,

caraiyya saṃyaujanavippamaukkhā|

sacai ratiṃ nādhigacchaiyya tattha,

saṅghai vasai rakkhitattau satīmā||

‘‘kulākulaṃ piṇḍikāya carantau,

indriyaguttau  nipakau satīmā|

saivaitha pantāni saināsanāni,

bhayā pamuttau abhayai vimuttau||

‘‘yattha bhairavā sarīsapā siriṃ sapā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰),

vij‍ju sañ‍carati thanayati daivau|

andhakāratimisāya rattiyā,

nisīdi tattha bhikkhu vigatalaumahaṃsau||

‘‘idañhi jātu mai diṭṭhaṃ, nayidaṃ itihītihaṃ|

aikasmiṃ brahmacariyasmiṃ, sahassaṃ mac‍cuhāyinaṃ||

‘‘bhiyyau bhīyau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pañ‍casatā saikkhā, dasā ca dasadhā dasa|

sabbai sautasamāpan‍nā, atiracchānagāminau||

‘‘athāyaṃ  atthāyaṃ-itipi dī॰ ni॰ 2.290 itarā pajā, puñ‍ñabhāgāti mai manau|

saṅkhātuṃ naupi sak‍kaumi, musāvādassa auttapa’’nti auttapaiti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), auttappaiti (ka॰)||

4. aruṇavatīsuttaṃ

185. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati…pai॰… tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, rājā ahausi aruṇavā nāma| rañ‍ñau khau pana, bhikkhavai, aruṇavatau aruṇavatī nāma rājadhānī ahausi| aruṇavatiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, rājadhāniṃ aruṇavatiyaṃ khau pana bhikkhavai rājadhāniyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) sikhī bhagavā  arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau upanissāya vihāsi| sikhissa khau pana, bhikkhavai, bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa abhibhūsambhavaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sikhī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau abhibhuṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘āyāma, brāhmaṇa, yaina añ‍ñatarau brahmalaukau tainupasaṅkamissāma, yāva bhattassa kālau bhavissatī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau bhikkhavai, abhibhū bhikkhu sikhissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sikhī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau abhibhū ca bhikkhu – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – aruṇavatiyā rājadhāniyā antarahitā tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahaisuṃ|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sikhī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau abhibhuṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘paṭibhātu, brāhmaṇa, taṃ brahmunau ca brahmaparisāya ca brahmapārisaj‍jānañ‍ca dhammī kathā’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau, bhikkhavai, abhibhū bhikkhu sikhissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa paṭissutvā, brahmānañ‍ca brahmaparisañ‍ca brahmapārisaj‍jai ca dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmā ca brahmaparisā ca brahmapārisaj‍jā  ca ujjhāyanti khiyyanti khīyanti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vipācainti – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, kathañhi nāma satthari sammukhībhūtai sāvakau dhammaṃ daisaissatī’’’ti!

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sikhī bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau abhibhuṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘ujjhāyanti khau tai, brāhmaṇa, brahmā ca brahmaparisā ca brahmapārisaj‍jā ca – acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau, kathañhi nāma satthari sammukhībhūtai sāvakau dhammaṃ daisaissatīti! taina hi tvaṃ brāhmaṇa, bhiyyausaumattāya brahmānañ‍ca brahmaparisañ‍ca brahmapārisaj‍jai ca saṃvaijaihī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau, bhikkhavai, abhibhū bhikkhu sikhissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa paṭissutvā dissamānainapi kāyaina dhammaṃ daisaisi, adissamānainapi kāyaina dhammaṃ daisaisi, dissamānainapi haiṭṭhimaina upaḍḍhakāyaina adissamānaina uparimaina upaḍḍhakāyaina dhammaṃ daisaisi, dissamānainapi uparimaina upaḍḍhakāyaina adissamānaina haiṭṭhimaina upaḍḍhakāyaina dhammaṃ daisaisi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmā ca brahmaparisā ca brahmapārisaj‍jā ca acchariyabbhutacittajātā ahaisuṃ – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau, samaṇassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā’’’ti!

‘‘atha khau abhibhū bhikkhu sikhiṃ bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘abhijānāmi khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghassa majjhai aivarūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā – pahaumi khvāhaṃ āvusau, brahmalaukai ṭhitau sahassilaukadhātuṃ  sahassīlaukadhātuṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) saraina viñ‍ñāpaitu’nti| ‘aitassa, brāhmaṇa, kālau, aitassa, brāhmaṇa, kālau; yaṃ tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, brahmalaukai ṭhitau sahassilaukadhātuṃ saraina viñ‍ñāpaiyyāsī’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau, bhikkhavai, abhibhū bhikkhu sikhissa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa paṭissutvā brahmalaukai ṭhitau imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘ārambhatha ārabbhatha (sabbattha) nik‍kamatha nikkhamatha (sī॰ pī॰), yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai|

dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau||

‘‘yau  imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, appamattau vihassati|

pahāya jātisaṃsāraṃ, dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’’ti||

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sikhī ca bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau abhibhū ca bhikkhu brahmānañ‍ca brahmaparisañ‍ca brahmapārisaj‍jai ca saṃvaijaitvā – saiyyathāpi nāma…pai॰… tasmiṃ brahmalaukai antarahitā aruṇavatiyā rājadhāniyā pāturahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sikhī bhagavā  arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘assuttha nau, tumhai, bhikkhavai, abhibhussa bhikkhunau brahmalaukai ṭhitassa gāthāyau bhāsamānassā’ti? ‘assumha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, abhibhussa bhikkhunau brahmalaukai ṭhitassa gāthāyau bhāsamānassā’ti| ‘yathā kathaṃ pana tumhai, bhikkhavai, assuttha abhibhussa bhikkhunau brahmalaukai ṭhitassa gāthāyau bhāsamānassā’’’ti? aivaṃ khau mayaṃ, bhantai, assumha abhibhussa bhikkhunau brahmalaukai ṭhitassa gāthāyau bhāsamānassa –

‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamatha, yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai|

dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau||

‘‘yau  imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, appamattau vihassati|

pahāya jātisaṃsāraṃ, dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’’ti||

‘‘‘aivaṃ khau mayaṃ, bhantai, assumha abhibhussa bhikkhunau brahmalaukai ṭhitassa gāthāyau bhāsamānassā’ti| ‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai; sādhu khau tumhai, bhikkhavai! assuttha abhibhussa bhikkhunau brahmalaukai ṭhitassa gāthāyau bhāsamānassā’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā, attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

5. parinibbānasuttaṃ

186. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kusinārāyaṃ viharati upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanai antaraina yamakasālānaṃ parinibbānasamayai| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘handa dāni, bhikkhavai, āmantayāmi vau – ‘vayadhammā saṅkhārā, appamādaina sampādaithā’ti| ayaṃ tathāgatassa pacchimā vācā’’|

atha  khau bhagavā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭhamajjhānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ ic‍cādīsupi samāpaj‍ji| paṭhamā jhānā paṭhamajjhānā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivaṃ dutiyā jhānā ic‍cādīsupi vuṭṭhahitvā dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| dutiyā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tatiyā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā catutthaṃ  jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| catutthā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanā  vuṭṭhahitvā sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍ji|

sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhā vuṭṭhahitvā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpaj‍ji| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanā vuṭṭhahitvā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| catutthā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tatiyā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| dutiyā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| paṭhamā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| dutiyā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| tatiyā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍ji| catutthā jhānā vuṭṭhahitvā samanantaraṃ bhagavā parinibbāyi| parinibbutai bhagavati saha parinibbānā brahmā sahampati imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘sabbaiva nikkhipissanti, bhūtā laukai samussayaṃ|

yattha aitādisau satthā, laukai appaṭipuggalau|

tathāgatau balappattau, sambuddhau parinibbutau’’ti||

parinibbutai bhagavati saha parinibbānā sak‍kau daivānamindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘anic‍cā vata saṅkhārā, uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau’’ti||

parinibbutai  bhagavati saha parinibbānā āyasmā ānandau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ, tadāsi laumahaṃsanaṃ|

sabbākāravarūpaitai, sambuddhai parinibbutai’’ti||

parinibbutai  bhagavati saha parinibbānā āyasmā anuruddhau imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘nāhu assāsapassāsau, ṭhitacittassa tādinau|

anaijau santimārabbha, cakkhumā parinibbutau yaṃ kālamakarī muni (mahāparinibbānasuttai)||
