‘‘asal‍līnaina cittaina, vaidanaṃ ajjhavāsayi|

paj‍jautassaiva nibbānaṃ, vimaukkhau caitasau ahū’’ti||

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

brahmāsanaṃ daivadattau, andhakavindau aruṇavatī|

parinibbānaina ca daisitaṃ, idaṃ brahmapañ‍cakanti||

brahmasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ| itau paraṃ marammapautthakaisu aivampi dissati –§brahmāyācanaṃ agāravañ‍ca, brahmadaivau bakau ca brahmā|§añ‍ñatarau ca brahmākaukālikañ‍ca, tissakañ‍ca turū ca||§brahmā kaukālikabhikkhu, sanaṅkumāraina daivadattaṃ|§andhakavindaṃ aruṇavati, parinibbānaina pan‍narasāti||



7. brāhmaṇasaṃyuttaṃ

1. arahantavaggau

1. dhanañ‍jānīsuttaṃ

187. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa dhanañ‍jānī dhānañ‍jānī (pī॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) nāma brāhmaṇī abhippasan‍nā hauti buddhai ca dhammai ca saṅghai ca| atha khau dhanañ‍jānī brāhmaṇī bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa bhattaṃ upasaṃharantī upakkhalitvā tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassā’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau dhanañ‍jāniṃ brāhmaṇiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaivaṃ panāyaṃ vasalī yasmiṃ vā tasmiṃ vā tassa muṇḍakassa samaṇassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati| idāni tyāhaṃ, vasali, tassa satthunau vādaṃ āraupaissāmī’’ti| ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, brāhmaṇa, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yau tassa bhagavatau vādaṃ āraupaiyya arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| api ca tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, gaccha, gantvā vijānissasī’’ti gantvāpi jānissasīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

atha  khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau kupitau anattamanau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃsu  chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kiṃsu chaitvā na saucati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, vadhaṃ raucaisi gautamā’’ti||

‘‘kaudhaṃ  chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kaudhaṃ chaitvā na saucati|

kaudhassa visamūlassa, madhuraggassa brāhmaṇa|

vadhaṃ ariyā pasaṃsanti, tañhi chaitvā na saucatī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

alattha khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panāyasmā bhāradvājau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau  ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausīti|

2. ak‍kausasuttaṃ

188. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| assausi khau ak‍kausakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘bhāradvājagauttau kira brāhmaṇau samaṇassa gautamassa santikai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’’ti kupitau anattamanau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ asabbhāhi pharusāhi vācāhi ak‍kausati paribhāsati|

aivaṃ  vuttai, bhagavā ak‍kausakabhāradvājaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, api nu khau tai āgacchanti mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā atithiyau atithayau (?)’’ti? ‘‘appaikadā mai, bhau gautama, āgacchanti mittāmac‍cā  ñātisālauhitā atithiyau’’ti| ‘‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, brāhmaṇa, api nu taisaṃ anuppadaisi khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sāyanīyaṃ vā’’’ti? ‘‘‘appaikadā naisāhaṃ, bhau gautama, anuppadaimi khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sāyanīyaṃ vā’’’ti| ‘‘‘sacai khau pana tai, brāhmaṇa, nappaṭiggaṇhanti, kassa taṃ hautī’’’ti? ‘‘‘sacai tai, bhau gautama, nappaṭiggaṇhanti, amhākamaiva taṃ hautī’’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, yaṃ tvaṃ amhai anak‍kausantai ak‍kausasi, arausaintai rausaisi, abhaṇḍantai bhaṇḍasi, taṃ tai mayaṃ nappaṭiggaṇhāma| tavaivaitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, hauti; tavaivaitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, hauti’’|

‘‘yau khau, brāhmaṇa, ak‍kausantaṃ pac‍cak‍kausati, rausaintaṃ paṭirausaiti, bhaṇḍantaṃ paṭibhaṇḍati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, sambhuñ‍jati vītiharatīti| tai mayaṃ tayā naiva sambhuñ‍jāma na vītiharāma| tavaivaitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, hauti; tavaivaitaṃ, brāhmaṇa, hautī’’ti| ‘‘bhavantaṃ khau gautamaṃ sarājikā parisā aivaṃ jānāti – ‘arahaṃ samaṇau gautamau’ti| atha ca pana bhavaṃ gautamau kujjhatī’’ti|

‘‘ak‍kaudhassa kutau kaudhau, dantassa samajīvinau|

sammadañ‍ñā vimuttassa, upasantassa tādinau||

‘‘tassaiva taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati||

‘‘ubhin‍naṃ tikicchantānaṃ, attanau ca parassa ca|

janā mañ‍ñanti bālauti, yai dhammassa akauvidā’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, ak‍kausakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aisāhaṃ  bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

alattha khau ak‍kausakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau khau panāyasmā ak‍kausakabhāradvājau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau  nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausīti|

3. asurindakasuttaṃ

189. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| assausi khau asurindakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau kira samaṇassa gautamassa santikai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’’ti kupitau anattamanau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ asabbhāhi pharusāhi vācāhi ak‍kausati paribhāsati| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau asurindakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘jitausi, samaṇa, jitausi, samaṇā’’ti|

‘‘jayaṃ  vai mañ‍ñati bālau, vācāya pharusaṃ bhaṇaṃ|

jayañ‍caivassa taṃ hauti, yā titikkhā vijānatau||

‘‘tassaiva taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati||

‘‘ubhin‍naṃ  tikicchantānaṃ, attanau ca parassa ca|

janā mañ‍ñanti bālauti, yai dhammassa akauvidā’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, asurindakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

4. bilaṅgikasuttaṃ

190. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| assausi khau bilaṅgikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘bhāradvājagauttau kira brāhmaṇau samaṇassa gautamassa santikai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’’ti kupitau anattamanau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tuṇhībhūtau aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| atha khau bhagavā bilaṅgikassa bhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya bilaṅgikaṃ bhāradvājaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi  –

‘‘yau appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati,

suddhassa pausassa anaṅgaṇassa|

tamaiva bālaṃ pac‍caiti pāpaṃ,

sukhumau rajau paṭivātaṃva khittau’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, vilaṅgikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

5. ahiṃsakasuttaṃ

191. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau ahiṃsakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau ahiṃsakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahiṃsakāhaṃ, bhau gautama, ahiṃsakāhaṃ, bhau gautamā’’ti|

‘‘yathā  nāmaṃ tathā cassa, siyā khau tvaṃ ahiṃsakau|

yau ca kāyaina vācāya, manasā ca na hiṃsati|

sa vai ahiṃsakau hauti, yau paraṃ na vihiṃsatī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, ahiṃsakabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā ahiṃsakabhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

6. jaṭāsuttaṃ

192. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau jaṭābhāradvājau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jaṭābhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘antaujaṭā bahijaṭā, jaṭāya jaṭitā pajā|

taṃ taṃ gautama pucchāmi, kau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭa’’nti||

‘‘sīlai patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

ātāpī nipakau bhikkhu, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭaṃ||

‘‘yaisaṃ rāgau ca dausau ca, avij‍jā ca virājitā|

khīṇāsavā arahantau, taisaṃ vijaṭitā jaṭā||

‘‘yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

paṭighaṃ rūpasañ‍ñā ca, aitthaisā chij‍jatai jaṭā’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, jaṭābhāradvājau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

7. suddhikasuttaṃ

193. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau suddhikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau suddhikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘na brāhmaṇau nābrāhmaṇau (?) sujjhati kauci, laukai sīlavāpi tapaukaraṃ|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, sau sujjhati na añ‍ñā itarā pajā’’ti||

‘‘bahumpi palapaṃ jappaṃ, na jac‍cā hauti brāhmaṇau|

antaukasambu saṅkiliṭṭhau, kuhanaṃ upanissitau||

‘‘khattiyau  brāhmaṇau vaissau, suddau caṇḍālapuk‍kusau|

āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamau|

pappauti paramaṃ suddhiṃ, aivaṃ jānāhi brāhmaṇā’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, suddhikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

8. aggikasuttaṃ

194. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina aggikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa sappinā pāyasau san‍nihitau hauti – ‘‘aggiṃ juhissāmi, aggihuttaṃ paricarissāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| rājagahai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina aggikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| addasā khau aggikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ piṇḍāya ṭhitaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tīhi vij‍jāhi sampan‍nau, jātimā sutavā bahū|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, saumaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya pāyasa’’nti||

‘‘bahumpi palapaṃ jappaṃ, na jac‍cā hauti brāhmaṇau|

antaukasambu saṃkiliṭṭhau, kuhanāparivāritau||

‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ  yau vaidī, saggāpāyañ‍ca passati|

athau jātikkhayaṃ pattau, abhiñ‍ñāvausitau muni||

‘‘aitāhi tīhi vij‍jāhi, taivij‍jau hauti brāhmaṇau|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau, saumaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyya pāyasa’’nti||

‘‘bhuñ‍jatu bhavaṃ gautamau| brāhmaṇau bhava’’nti|

‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ  mai abhaujanaiyyaṃ,

sampassataṃ brāhmaṇa naisa dhammau|

gāthābhigītaṃ panudanti buddhā,

dhammai sati brāhmaṇa vuttiraisā||

‘‘añ‍ñaina ca kaivalinaṃ mahaisiṃ,

khīṇāsavaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cavūpasantaṃ|

an‍naina pānaina upaṭṭhahassu,

khaittañhi taṃ puñ‍ñapaikkhassa hautī’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, aggikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā aggikabhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

9. sundarikasuttaṃ

195. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati sundarikāya nadiyā tīrai| taina khau pana samayaina sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau sundarikāya nadiyā tīrai aggiṃ juhati, aggihuttaṃ paricarati| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau aggiṃ juhitvā aggihuttaṃ paricaritvā uṭṭhāyāsanā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaisi – ‘‘kau nu khau imaṃ habyasaisaṃ bhuñ‍jaiyyā’’ti? addasā khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai sasīsaṃ pārutaṃ nisin‍naṃ| disvāna vāmaina hatthaina  habyasaisaṃ gahaitvā dakkhiṇaina hatthaina kamaṇḍaluṃ gahaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami| atha khau bhagavā sundarikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa padasaddaina sīsaṃ vivari| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau ‘muṇḍau ayaṃ  bhavaṃ, muṇḍakau ayaṃ bhava’nti tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘muṇḍāpi hi idhaikac‍cai brāhmaṇā bhavanti; yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ upasaṅkamitvā jātiṃ pucchaiyya’nti|

atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃjac‍cau bhava’nti?

‘‘mā  jātiṃ puccha caraṇañ‍ca puccha,

kaṭṭhā havai jāyati jātavaidau|

nīcākulīnaupi muni dhitimā,

ājānīyau hauti hirīnisaidhau||

‘‘sac‍caina dantau damasā upaitau,

vaidantagū vusitabrahmacariyau|

yañ‍ñaupanītau  tamupavhayaitha,

kālaina sau juhati dakkhiṇaiyyai’’ti||

‘‘addhā suyiṭṭhaṃ suhutaṃ mama yidaṃ,

yaṃ tādisaṃ vaidagumaddasāmi|

tumhādisānañhi  adassanaina,

añ‍ñau janau bhuñ‍jati habyasaisa’’nti||

‘‘bhuñ‍jatu bhavaṃ gautamau| brāhmaṇau bhava’’nti|

‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ mai abhaujanaiyyaṃ,

sampassataṃ brāhmaṇa naisa dhammau|

gāthābhigītaṃ panudanti buddhā,

dhammai sati brāhmaṇa vuttiraisā||

‘‘añ‍ñaina ca kaivalinaṃ mahaisiṃ,

khīṇāsavaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cavūpasantaṃ|

an‍naina pānaina upaṭṭhahassu,

khaittañhi taṃ puñ‍ñapaikkhassa hautī’’ti||

‘‘atha kassa cāhaṃ, bhau gautama, imaṃ habyasaisaṃ dammī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya yassaisau habyasaisau bhuttau sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchaiyya  añ‍ñatra, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā| taina hi tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, taṃ habyasaisaṃ appaharitai vā chaḍḍaihi appāṇakai vā udakai aupilāpaihī’’ti|

atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau taṃ habyasaisaṃ appāṇakai udakai aupilāpaisi| atha khau sau habyasaisau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati  ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati| saiyyathāpi nāma  phālau lauhau (ka॰) divasaṃsantattau divasasantattau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati; aivamaiva sau habyasaisau udakai pakkhittau cic‍ciṭāyati ciṭiciṭāyati sandhūpāyati sampadhūpāyati|

atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ khau sundarikabhāradvājaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘mā brāhmaṇa dāru samādahānau,

suddhiṃ amañ‍ñi bahiddhā hi aitaṃ|

na hi taina suddhiṃ kusalā vadanti,

yau bāhiraina parisuddhimicchai||

‘‘hitvā ahaṃ brāhmaṇa dārudāhaṃ

ajjhattamaivuj‍jalayāmi ajjhattamaiva jalayāmi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) jautiṃ|

nic‍cagginī nic‍casamāhitattau,

arahaṃ ahaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carāmi||

‘‘mānau hi tai brāhmaṇa khāribhārau,

kaudhau dhumau bhasmani mausavaj‍jaṃ|

jivhā sujā hadayaṃ jautiṭhānaṃ,

attā sudantau purisassa jauti||

‘‘dhammau  rahadau brāhmaṇa sīlatitthau,

anāvilau sabbhi sataṃ pasatthau|

yattha havai vaidagunau sinātā,

anal‍lagattāva anal‍līnagattāva (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) taranti pāraṃ||

‘‘sac‍caṃ dhammau saṃyamau brahmacariyaṃ,

majjhai sitā brāhmaṇa brahmapatti|

sa  tuj‍jubhūtaisu namau karauhi,

tamahaṃ naraṃ dhammasārīti brūmī’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausī’’ti|

10. bahudhītarasuttaṃ

196. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa catuddasa balībaddā naṭṭhā haunti| atha khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau tai balībaddai gavaisantau yaina sau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā addasa bhagavantaṃ tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘na  hi nūnimassa nahanūnimassa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) samaṇassa, balībaddā catuddasa|

aj‍jasaṭṭhiṃ na dissanti, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī||

‘‘na hi nūnimassa samaṇassa, tilākhaittasmi pāpakā|

aikapaṇṇā dupaṇṇā dvipaṇṇā (sī॰ pī॰) ca, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī||

‘‘na hi nūnimassa samaṇassa, tucchakauṭṭhasmi mūsikā|

ussauḷhikāya nac‍canti, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī||

‘‘na hi nūnimassa samaṇassa, santhārau sattamāsikau|

uppāṭakaihi sañchan‍nau, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī||

‘‘na  hi nūnimassa samaṇassa, vidhavā satta dhītarau|

aikaputtā duputtā dviputtā (sī॰ pī॰) ca, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī||

‘‘na hi nūnimassa samaṇassa, piṅgalā tilakāhatā|

sauttaṃ pādaina baudhaiti, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī||

‘‘na hi nūnimassa samaṇassa, pac‍cūsamhi iṇāyikā|

daitha daithāti caudainti, taināyaṃ samaṇau sukhī’’ti||

‘‘na  hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, balībaddā catuddasa|

aj‍jasaṭṭhiṃ na dissanti, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī||

‘‘na  hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, tilākhaittasmi pāpakā|

aikapaṇṇā dupaṇṇā ca, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī||

‘‘na hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, tucchakauṭṭhasmi mūsikā|

ussauḷhikāya nac‍canti, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī||

‘‘na  hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, santhārau sattamāsikau|

uppāṭakaihi sañchan‍nau, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī||

‘‘na hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, vidhavā satta dhītarau|

aikaputtā duputtā ca, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī||

‘‘na hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, piṅgalā tilakāhatā|

sauttaṃ pādaina baudhaiti, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī||

‘‘na  hi mayhaṃ brāhmaṇa, pac‍cūsamhi iṇāyikā|

daitha daithāti caudainti, taināhaṃ brāhmaṇā sukhī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaiva bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti|

alattha khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau panāyasmā bhāradvājau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti, tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ  itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausīti|

arahantavaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

dhanañ‍jānī ca ak‍kausaṃ, asurindaṃ bilaṅgikaṃ|

ahiṃsakaṃ jaṭā caiva, suddhikañ‍caiva aggikā|

sundarikaṃ bahudhītaraina ca tai dasāti||

2. upāsakavaggau

1. kasibhāradvājasuttaṃ

197. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu viharati dakkhiṇāgirismiṃ aikanāḷāyaṃ brāhmaṇagāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kasibhāradvājassa kasikabhāradvājassa (ka॰) brāhmaṇassa pañ‍camattāni naṅgalasatāni payuttāni haunti vappakālai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa kammantau tainupasaṅkami|

taina khau pana samayaina kasibhāradvājassa brāhmaṇassa parivaisanā vattati| atha khau bhagavā yaina parivaisanā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| addasā khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ piṇḍāya ṭhitaṃ| disvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahaṃ khau, samaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca, kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jāmi| tvampi, samaṇa, kasassu ca vapassu ca, kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jassū’’ti| ‘‘ahampi khau, brāhmaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca, kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti| na khau mayaṃ passāma bhautau gautamassa yugaṃ vā naṅgalaṃ vā phālaṃ vā pācanaṃ vā balībaddai vā, atha ca pana  bhavaṃ gautamau aivamāha – ‘‘ahampi khau, brāhmaṇa, kasāmi ca vapāmi ca, kasitvā ca vapitvā ca bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti| atha khau kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kassakau paṭijānāsi, na ca passāmi tai kasiṃ|

kassakau pucchitau brūhi, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ kasi’’nti||

‘‘saddhā bījaṃ tapau vuṭṭhi, pañ‍ñā mai yuganaṅgalaṃ|

hirī īsā manau yauttaṃ, sati mai phālapācanaṃ||

‘‘kāyaguttau  vacīguttau, āhārai udarai yatau|

sac‍caṃ karaumi niddānaṃ, saurac‍caṃ mai pamaucanaṃ||

‘‘vīriyaṃ  mai dhuradhaurayhaṃ, yaugakkhaimādhivāhanaṃ|

gacchati anivattantaṃ, yattha gantvā na saucati||

‘‘aivamaisā kasī kaṭṭhā, sā hauti amatapphalā|

aitaṃ kasiṃ kasitvāna, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘bhuñ‍jatu bhavaṃ gautamau| kassakau bhavaṃ| yañhi bhavaṃ gautamau amatapphalampi kasiṃ kasatī’’ti bhāsatīti (ka॰)|

‘‘gāthābhigītaṃ mai abhaujanaiyyaṃ,

sampassataṃ brāhmaṇa naisa dhammau|

gāthābhigītaṃ panudanti buddhā,

dhammai sati brāhmaṇa vuttiraisā||

‘‘añ‍ñaina  ca kaivalinaṃ mahaisiṃ,

khīṇāsavaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cavūpasantaṃ|

an‍naina pānaina upaṭṭhahassu,

khaittañhi taṃ puñ‍ñapaikkhassa hautī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, kasibhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

2. udayasuttaṃ

198. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina  udayassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| atha  khau udayau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pattaṃ audanaina pūraisi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina udayassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… tatiyampi khau udayau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pattaṃ audanaina  pūraitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pakaṭṭhakauyaṃ samaṇau gautamau punappunaṃ āgacchatī’’ti|

‘‘punappunañ‍caiva  vapanti bījaṃ, punappunaṃ vassati daivarājā|

punappunaṃ khaittaṃ kasanti kassakā, punappunaṃ dhañ‍ñamupaiti raṭṭhaṃ||

‘‘punappunaṃ yācakā yācayanti, punappunaṃ dānapatī dadanti|

punappunaṃ dānapatī daditvā, punappunaṃ saggamupainti ṭhānaṃ||

‘‘punappunaṃ khīranikā duhanti, punappunaṃ vacchau upaiti mātaraṃ|

punappunaṃ kilamati phandati ca, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau||

‘‘punappunaṃ jāyati mīyati ca, punappunaṃ sivathikaṃ sīvathikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) haranti|

maggañ‍ca laddhā apunabbhavāya, na punappunaṃ jāyati bhūripañ‍ñau’’ti punappunaṃ jāyati bhūripañ‍ñauti (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)||

aivaṃ vuttai, udayau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

3. daivahitasuttaṃ

199. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā vātaihābādhikau hauti; āyasmā ca upavāṇau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘iṅgha mai tvaṃ, upavāṇa, uṇhaudakaṃ jānāhī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā upavāṇau bhagavatau paṭissutvā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina daivahitassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tuṇhībhūtau aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| addasā khau daivahitau brāhmaṇau āyasmantaṃ  upavāṇaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ aikamantaṃ ṭhitaṃ| disvāna āyasmantaṃ upavāṇaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tuṇhībhūtau  bhavaṃ tiṭṭhaṃ, muṇḍau saṅghāṭipārutau|

kiṃ patthayānau kiṃ aisaṃ, kiṃ nu yācitumāgatau’’ti||

‘‘arahaṃ sugatau laukai, vātaihābādhikau muni|

sacai uṇhaudakaṃ atthi, muninau daihi brāhmaṇa||

‘‘pūjitau pūjanaiyyānaṃ, sak‍karaiyyāna sak‍katau|

apacitau apacaiyyānaṃ apacinaiyyānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā, tassa icchāmi hātavai’’ti||

atha khau daivahitau brāhmaṇau uṇhaudakassa kājaṃ purisaina gāhāpaitvā phāṇitassa ca puṭaṃ āyasmatau upavāṇassa pādāsi| atha khau āyasmā upavāṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  uṇhaudakaina nhāpaitvā nahāpaitvā (sī॰ pī॰) uṇhaudakaina phāṇitaṃ ālaulaitvā bhagavatau pādāsi| atha khau bhagavatau ābādhau paṭippassambhi|

atha khau daivahitau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau daivahitau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kattha daj‍jā daiyyadhammaṃ, kattha din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ|

kathañhi yajamānassa, kathaṃ ijjhati dakkhiṇā’’ti||

‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ yau vaidī, saggāpāyañ‍ca passati|

athau jātikkhayaṃ pattau, abhiñ‍ñāvausitau muni||

‘‘aittha  daj‍jā daiyyadhammaṃ, aittha din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ|

aivañhi yajamānassa, aivaṃ ijjhati dakkhiṇā’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, daivahitau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

4. mahāsālasuttaṃ

200. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇamahāsālau lūkhau lūkhapāvuraṇau  lūkhapāpuraṇau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālaṃ bhagavā  aitadavauca – ‘‘kin‍nu tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, lūkhau lūkhapāvuraṇau’’ti? ‘‘idha mai, bhau gautama, cattārau puttā| tai maṃ dāraihi saṃpuccha gharā nikkhāmaintī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imā gāthāyau pariyāpuṇitvā sabhāyaṃ mahājanakāyai san‍nipatitai puttaisu ca san‍nisin‍naisu bhāsassu –

‘‘yaihi jātaihi nandissaṃ, yaisañ‍ca bhavamicchisaṃ|

tai maṃ dāraihi saṃpuccha, sāva vārainti sūkaraṃ||

‘‘asantā kira maṃ jammā, tāta tātāti bhāsarai|

rakkhasā puttarūpaina, tai jahanti vayaugataṃ||

‘‘assauva jiṇṇau nibbhaugau, khādanā apanīyati|

bālakānaṃ pitā thairau, parāgāraisu bhikkhati||

‘‘daṇḍauva kira mai saiyyau, yañ‍cai puttā anassavā|

caṇḍampi gauṇaṃ vāraiti, athau caṇḍampi kuk‍kuraṃ||

‘‘andhakārai  purai hauti, gambhīrai gādhamaidhati|

daṇḍassa ānubhāvaina, khalitvā patitiṭṭhatī’’ti||

atha khau sau brāhmaṇamahāsālau bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau pariyāpuṇitvā sabhāyaṃ mahājanakāyai san‍nipatitai puttaisu ca san‍nisin‍naisu abhāsi –

‘‘yaihi jātaihi nandissaṃ, yaisañ‍ca bhavamicchisaṃ|

tai  maṃ dāraihi saṃpuccha, sāva vārainti sūkaraṃ||

‘‘asantā kira maṃ jammā, tāta tātāti bhāsarai|

rakkhasā puttarūpaina, tai jahanti vayaugataṃ||

‘‘assauva  jiṇṇau nibbhaugau, khādanā apanīyati|

bālakānaṃ pitā thairau, parāgāraisu bhikkhati||

‘‘daṇḍauva kira mai saiyyau, yañ‍cai puttā anassavā|

caṇḍampi gauṇaṃ vāraiti, athau caṇḍampi kuk‍kuraṃ||

‘‘andhakārai purai hauti, gambhīrai gādhamaidhati|

daṇḍassa ānubhāvaina, khalitvā patitiṭṭhatī’’ti||

atha  khau naṃ brāhmaṇamahāsālaṃ puttā gharaṃ naitvā nhāpaitvā pac‍caikaṃ dussayugaina acchādaisuṃ| atha khau sau brāhmaṇamahāsālau aikaṃ dussayugaṃ ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau brāhmaṇamahāsālau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayaṃ, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇā nāma ācariyassa ācariyadhanaṃ pariyaisāma| paṭiggaṇhatu mai bhavaṃ gautamau ācariyadhana’’nti| paṭiggahaisi bhagavā anukampaṃ upādāya| atha khau sau brāhmaṇamahāsālau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

5. mānatthaddhasuttaṃ

201. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina mānatthaddhau nāma brāhmaṇau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati| sau naiva mātaraṃ abhivādaiti, na pitaraṃ abhivādaiti, na ācariyaṃ abhivādaiti, na jaiṭṭhabhātaraṃ abhivādaiti| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaiti| atha khau mānatthaddhassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaiti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ| sacai maṃ samaṇau gautamau ālapissati, ahampi taṃ ālapissāmi| nau cai maṃ samaṇau gautamau ālapissati, ahampi nālapissāmī’’ti| atha khau mānatthaddhau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tuṇhībhūtau aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| atha khau bhagavā taṃ nālapi| atha khau mānatthaddhau brāhmaṇau – ‘nāyaṃ samaṇau  gautamau kiñ‍ci jānātī’ti tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| atha  khau bhagavā mānatthaddhassa brāhmaṇassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya mānatthaddhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘na mānaṃ brāhmaṇa sādhu, atthikassīdha brāhmaṇa|

yaina atthaina āgacchi, tamaivamanubrūhayai’’ti||

atha khau mānatthaddhau brāhmaṇau – ‘‘cittaṃ mai samaṇau gautamau jānātī’’ti tatthaiva bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavatau pādāni  mukhaina ca paricumbati pāṇīhi ca parisambāhati, nāmañ‍ca sāvaiti – ‘‘mānatthaddhāhaṃ, bhau gautama, mānatthaddhāhaṃ, bhau gautamā’’ti| atha khau sā parisā abbhutacittajātā abbhutacittajātā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), acchariyabbhutacittajātā (ka॰) ahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! ayañhi mānatthaddhau brāhmaṇau naiva mātaraṃ abhivādaiti, na pitaraṃ abhivādaiti, na ācariyaṃ abhivādaiti, na jaiṭṭhabhātaraṃ abhivādaiti; atha ca pana samaṇai gautamai aivarūpaṃ paramanipac‍cakāraṃ karautī’ti| atha khau bhagavā mānatthaddhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, brāhmaṇa, uṭṭhaihi, sakai āsanai nisīda| yatau tai mayi cittaṃ pasan‍na’’nti| atha khau mānatthaddhau brāhmaṇau sakai āsanai nisīditvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kaisu na mānaṃ kayirātha, kaisu cassa sagāravau|

kyassa apacitā assu, kyassu sādhu supūjitā’’ti||

‘‘mātari pitari cāpi, athau jaiṭṭhamhi bhātari|

ācariyai catutthamhi,

taisu na mānaṃ kayirātha|

taisu assa sagāravau,

tyassa apacitā assu|

tyassu sādhu supūjitā||

‘‘arahantai sītībhūtai, katakic‍cai anāsavai|

nihac‍ca mānaṃ athaddhau, tai namassai anuttarai’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, mānatthaddhau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

6. pac‍canīkasuttaṃ

202. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina pac‍canīkasātau nāma brāhmaṇau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati| atha khau pac‍canīkasātassa brāhmaṇassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamaiyyaṃ| yaṃ yadaiva samaṇau gautamau bhāsissati taṃ tadaivassāhaṃ tadaiva sāhaṃ (ka॰) pac‍canīkāssa’’nti pac‍canīkassanti (pī॰), pac‍canīkasātanti (ka॰)| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā abbhaukāsai caṅkamati| atha khau pac‍canīkasātau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ caṅkamantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhaṇa samaṇadhamma’nti|

‘‘na  pac‍canīkasātaina, suvijānaṃ subhāsitaṃ|

upak‍kiliṭṭhacittaina, sārambhabahulaina ca||

‘‘yau ca vinaiyya sārambhaṃ, appasādañ‍ca caitasau|

āghātaṃ paṭinissaj‍ja, sa vai sacai (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) jañ‍ñā subhāsita’’nti||

aivaṃ vuttai, pac‍canīkasātau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

7. navakammikasuttaṃ

203. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina navakammikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai kammantaṃ kārāpaiti| addasā khau navakammikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ sālarukkhamūlai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ahaṃ khau imasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai kammantaṃ kārāpaintau ramāmi| ayaṃ samaṇau gautamau kiṃ kārāpaintau ramatī’’ti? atha  khau navakammikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kai nu kammantā karīyanti, bhikkhu sālavanai tava|

yadaikakau arañ‍ñasmiṃ, ratiṃ vindati gautamau’’ti||

‘‘na mai vanasmiṃ karaṇīyamatthi,

ucchin‍namūlaṃ mai vanaṃ visūkaṃ|

svāhaṃ vanai nibbanathau visal‍lau,

aikau ramai aratiṃ vippahāyā’’ti||

aivaṃ  vuttai, navakammikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

8. kaṭṭhahārasuttaṃ

204. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhāradvājagauttassa brāhmaṇassa  sambahulā antaivāsikā kaṭṭhahārakā māṇavakā yaina vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā addasaṃsu bhagavantaṃ tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| disvāna yaina bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhāradvājagauttaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yagghai, bhavaṃ jānaiyyāsi! asukasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai samaṇau nisin‍nau pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā’’| atha khau bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau taihi māṇavakaihi saddhiṃ yaina sau vanasaṇḍau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavantaṃ tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘gambhīrarūpai bahubhairavai vanai,

suñ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ vijanaṃ vigāhiya|

aniñ‍jamānaina  ṭhitaina vaggunā,

sucārurūpaṃ vata bhikkhu jhāyasi||

‘‘na  yattha gītaṃ napi yattha vāditaṃ,

aikau arañ‍ñai vanavassitau muni|

acchairarūpaṃ paṭibhāti maṃ idaṃ,

yadaikakau pītimanau vanai vasai||

‘‘mañ‍ñāmahaṃ  laukādhipatisahabyataṃ,

ākaṅkhamānau tidivaṃ anuttaraṃ|

kasmā  bhavaṃ vijanamarañ‍ñamassitau,

tapau idha kubbasi brahmapattiyā’’ti||

‘‘yā kāci kaṅkhā abhinandanā vā,

anaikadhātūsu puthū sadāsitā|

añ‍ñāṇamūlappabhavā pajappitā,

sabbā mayā byantikatā samūlikā||

‘‘svāhaṃ akaṅkhau asitau anūpayau,

sabbaisu dhammaisu visuddhadassanau|

pappuyya sambaudhimanuttaraṃ sivaṃ,

jhāyāmahaṃ brahma rahau visāradau’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, bhāradvājagauttau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

9. mātupausakasuttaṃ

205. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau mātupausakau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mātupausakau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahañhi, bhau  gautama, dhammaina bhikkhaṃ pariyaisāmi, dhammaina bhikkhaṃ pariyaisitvā mātāpitarau pausaimi| kac‍cāhaṃ, bhau gautama, aivaṃkārī kic‍cakārī haumī’’ti? ‘‘taggha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, aivaṃkārī kic‍cakārī hausi| yau khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammaina bhikkhaṃ pariyaisati, dhammaina bhikkhaṃ  pariyaisitvā mātāpitarau pausaiti, bahuṃ sau puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti|

‘‘yau  mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vā, mac‍cau dhammaina pausati|

tāya naṃ pāricariyāya, mātāpitūsu paṇḍitā|

idhaiva naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudatī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, mātupausakau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

10. bhikkhakasuttaṃ

206. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau bhikkhakau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau bhikkhakau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ahampi khau, bhau gautama, bhikkhakau, bhavampi bhikkhakau, idha nau kiṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti?

‘‘na taina bhikkhakau hauti, yāvatā bhikkhatai parai|

vissaṃ dhammaṃ samādāya, bhikkhu hauti na tāvatā||

‘‘yaudha  puñ‍ñañ‍ca pāpañ‍ca, bāhitvā brahmacariyaṃ|

saṅkhāya laukai carati, sa vai bhikkhūti vuc‍catī’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhakau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

11. saṅgāravasuttaṃ

207. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina saṅgāravau nāma brāhmaṇau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati udakasuddhikau, udakaina parisuddhiṃ pac‍caiti, sāyaṃ pātaṃ udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya  pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, saṅgāravau nāma brāhmaṇau sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasati udakasuddhikau, udakaina suddhiṃ pac‍caiti, sāyaṃ pātaṃ udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaina saṅgāravassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina saṅgāravassa brāhmaṇassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau saṅgāravaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, udakasuddhikau, udakaina suddhiṃ pac‍caisi, sāyaṃ pātaṃ udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘kiṃ pana tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau udakasuddhikau, udakasuddhiṃ pac‍caisi, sāyaṃ pātaṃ udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharasī’’ti? ‘‘idha mai, bhau gautama idha mai bhau gautama ahaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), yaṃ divā pāpakammaṃ kataṃ hauti, taṃ sāyaṃ nhānaina nahānaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pavāhaimi, yaṃ rattiṃ pāpakammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ pātaṃ nhānaina pavāhaimi| imaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhau gautama, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau udakasuddhikau, udakaina suddhiṃ pac‍caimi, sāyaṃ pātaṃ udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharāmī’’ti|

‘‘dhammau rahadau brāhmaṇa sīlatitthau,

anāvilau sabbhi sataṃ pasatthau|

yattha havai vaidagunau sinātā,

anal‍lagattāva anal‍līnagattāva (ka॰) taranti pāra’’nti||

aivaṃ vuttai, saṅgāravau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

12. khaumadussasuttaṃ

208. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati khaumadussaṃ nāmaṃ sakyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā  pattacīvaramādāya khaumadussaṃ nigamaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| taina khau pana samayaina khaumadussakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā sabhāyaṃ san‍nipatitā haunti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina, daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusāyati| atha khau bhagavā yaina sā sabhā tainupasaṅkami| addasaṃsu khaumadussakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kai ca muṇḍakā samaṇakā, kai ca sabhādhammaṃ jānissantī’’ti? atha khau bhagavā khaumadussakai brāhmaṇagahapatikai gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘naisā sabhā yattha na santi santau,

santau na tai yai na vadanti dhammaṃ|

rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhaṃ,

dhammaṃ vadantā ca bhavanti santau’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, khaumadussakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama; saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti, aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina  dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

upāsakavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kasi udayau daivahitau, añ‍ñataramahāsālaṃ|

mānathaddhaṃ pac‍canīkaṃ, navakammikakaṭṭhahāraṃ|

mātupausakaṃ bhikkhakau, saṅgāravau ca khaumadussaina dvādasāti||

brāhmaṇasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



8. vaṅgīsasaṃyuttaṃ

1. nikkhantasuttaṃ

209. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā vaṅgīsau āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai āyasmatā nigraudhakappaina upajjhāyaina saddhiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā vaṅgīsau navakau hauti acirapabbajitau auhiyyakau vihārapālau| atha khau sambahulā itthiyau samalaṅkaritvā yaina aggāḷavakau ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu vihārapaikkhikāyau| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa tā itthiyau disvā anabhirati uppaj‍jati, rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yassa mai anabhirati uppan‍nā, rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti, taṃ kutaittha labbhā, yaṃ mai parau anabhiratiṃ vinaudaitvā abhiratiṃ uppādaiyya| yaṃnūnāhaṃ attanāva attanau anabhiratiṃ vinaudaitvā abhiratiṃ uppādaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau attanāva attanau anabhiratiṃ vinaudaitvā abhiratiṃ uppādaitvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘nikkhantaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ, agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

vitak‍kā upadhāvanti, pagabbhā kaṇhatau imai||

‘‘uggaputtā  mahissāsā, sikkhitā daḷhadhamminau|

samantā parikiraiyyuṃ, sahassaṃ apalāyinaṃ||

‘‘sacaipi aitatau aittatau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), aittakā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) bhiyyau, āgamissanti itthiyau|

naiva maṃ byādhayissanti byāthayissanti (?), dhammai samhi patiṭṭhitaṃ||

‘‘sakkhī  hi mai sutaṃ aitaṃ, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau|

nibbānagamanaṃ maggaṃ, tattha mai niratau manau||

‘‘aivañ‍cai maṃ viharantaṃ, pāpima upagacchasi|

tathā mac‍cu karissāmi, na mai maggampi dakkhasī’’ti||

2. aratisuttaṃ

210. aikaṃ  samayaṃ…pai॰… āyasmā vaṅgīsau āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai āyasmatā nigraudhakappaina upajjhāyaina saddhiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nigraudhakappau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau vihāraṃ pavisati, sāyaṃ vā nikkhamati aparaj‍ju vā kālai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa anabhirati uppan‍nā hauti, rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yassa mai anabhirati uppan‍nā, rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti; taṃ kutaittha labbhā, yaṃ mai parau anabhiratiṃ vinaudaitvā abhiratiṃ uppādaiyya| yaṃnūnāhaṃ attanāva attanau anabhiratiṃ vinaudaitvā abhiratiṃ uppādaiyya’’nti| atha  khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau attanāva attanau anabhiratiṃ vinaudaitvā abhiratiṃ uppādaitvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘aratiñ‍ca ratiñ‍ca pahāya, sabbasau gaihasitañ‍ca vitak‍kaṃ|

vanathaṃ na karaiyya kuhiñ‍ci, nibbanathau aratau sa hi bhikkhu sa bhikkhu (ka॰)||

‘‘yamidha pathaviñ‍ca vaihāsaṃ, rūpagatañ‍ca jagataugadhaṃ|

kiñ‍ci parijīyati sabbamanic‍caṃ, aivaṃ samaic‍ca caranti mutattā||

‘‘upadhīsu janā gadhitāsai gathitāsai (sī॰), diṭṭhasutai paṭighai ca mutai ca|

aittha vinaudaya chandamanaijau, yau aittha na limpati taṃ munimāhu||

‘‘atha  saṭṭhinissitā savitak‍kā, puthū janatāya adhammā niviṭṭhā|

na ca vaggagatassa kuhiñ‍ci, nau pana duṭṭhul‍labhāṇī sa bhikkhu||

‘‘dabbau cirarattasamāhitau, akuhakau nipakau apihālu|

santaṃ  padaṃ ajjhagamā muni paṭic‍ca, parinibbutau kaṅkhati kāla’’nti||

3. paisalasuttaṃ

211. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā vaṅgīsau āḷaviyaṃ viharati aggāḷavai caitiyai āyasmatā nigraudhakappaina upajjhāyaina saddhiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina  āyasmā vaṅgīsau attanau paṭibhānaina añ‍ñai paisalai bhikkhū atimañ‍ñati| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā; dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ; yvāhaṃ attanau paṭibhānaina añ‍ñai paisalai bhikkhū atimañ‍ñāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau attanāva attanau vippaṭisāraṃ uppādaitvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘mānaṃ pajahassu gautama, mānapathañ‍ca pajahassu|

asaisaṃ mānapathasmiṃ, samucchitau vippaṭisārīhuvā cirarattaṃ||

‘‘makkhaina makkhitā pajā, mānahatā nirayaṃ papatanti|

saucanti janā cirarattaṃ, mānahatā nirayaṃ upapan‍nā||

‘‘na  hi saucati bhikkhu kadāci, maggajinau sammāpaṭipan‍nau|

kittiñ‍ca sukhañ‍ca anubhauti, dhammadasauti tamāhu pahitattaṃ||

‘‘tasmā  akhilaudha padhānavā, nīvaraṇāni pahāya visuddhau|

mānañ‍ca pahāya asaisaṃ, vij‍jāyantakarau samitāvī’’ti||

4. ānandasuttaṃ

212. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi āyasmatā vaṅgīsaina pacchāsamaṇaina| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa anabhirati uppan‍nā hauti, rāgau cittaṃ anuddhaṃsaiti| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kāmarāgaina ḍayhāmi, cittaṃ mai pariḍayhati|

sādhu nibbāpanaṃ brūhi, anukampāya gautamā’’ti||

‘‘sañ‍ñāya  vipariyaisā, cittaṃ tai pariḍayhati|

nimittaṃ parivaj‍jaihi, subhaṃ rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ||

‘‘saṅkhārai paratau passa, dukkhatau mā ca attatau|

nibbāpaihi mahārāgaṃ, mā ḍayhitthau punappunaṃ||

‘‘asubhāya  cittaṃ bhāvaihi, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ|

sati kāyagatā tyatthu, nibbidābahulau bhava||

‘‘animittañ‍ca bhāvaihi, mānānusayamuj‍jaha|

tatau mānābhisamayā, upasantau carissasī’’ti||

5. subhāsitasuttaṃ

213. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā vācā subhāsitā hauti, nau dubbhāsitā; anavaj‍jā ca ananuvaj‍jā ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  subhāsitaṃyaiva bhāsati nau dubbhāsitaṃ, dhammaṃyaiva bhāsati nau adhammaṃ, piyaṃyaiva  bhāsati nau appiyaṃ, sac‍caṃyaiva bhāsati nau alikaṃ| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā vācā subhāsitā hauti, nau dubbhāsitā, anavaj‍jā ca ananuvaj‍jā ca viñ‍ñūna’’nti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘subhāsitaṃ  uttamamāhu santau,

dhammaṃ bhaṇai nādhammaṃ taṃ dutiyaṃ|

piyaṃ bhaṇai nāppiyaṃ taṃ tatiyaṃ,

sac‍caṃ bhaṇai nālikaṃ taṃ catuttha’’nti||

atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘tamaiva vācaṃ bhāsaiyya, yāyattānaṃ na tāpayai|

parai ca na vihiṃsaiyya, sā vai vācā subhāsitā||

‘‘piyavācaṃva bhāsaiyya, yā vācā paṭinanditā|

yaṃ anādāya pāpāni, paraisaṃ bhāsatai piyaṃ||

‘‘sac‍caṃ  vai amatā vācā, aisa dhammau sanantanau|

sac‍cai atthai ca dhammai ca, āhu santau patiṭṭhitā||

‘‘yaṃ buddhau bhāsatai vācaṃ, khaimaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

dukkhassantakiriyāya, sā vai vācānamuttamā’’ti||

6. sāriputtasuttaṃ

214. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya anaiḷagalāya (sī॰ ka॰), anailagaḷāya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā  sāriputtau bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti pauriyā vācāya vissaṭṭhāya anailagalāya atthassa viñ‍ñāpaniyā| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, āvusau sāriputta, paṭibhāti maṃ, āvusau sāriputtā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, āvusau vaṅgīsā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘gambhīrapañ‍ñau maidhāvī, maggāmaggassa kauvidau|

sāriputtau mahāpañ‍ñau, dhammaṃ daisaiti bhikkhunaṃ||

‘‘saṃkhittainapi daisaiti, vitthārainapi bhāsati|

sāḷikāyiva nigghausau, paṭibhānaṃ udīrayi udīriyi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) udīriyati (sāmañ‍ñaphalasuttaṭīkānurūpaṃ)||

‘‘tassa  taṃ daisayantassa, suṇanti madhuraṃ giraṃ|

saraina  rajanīyaina, savanīyaina vaggunā|

udaggacittā muditā, sautaṃ audhainti bhikkhavau’’ti||

7. pavāraṇāsuttaṃ

215. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sabbaihaiva arahantaihi| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai pavāraṇāya bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau bhagavā tuṇhībhūtaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ anuvilaukaitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘handa dāni, bhikkhavai, pavāraimi vau| na ca mai kiñ‍ci garahatha kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau mayaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau kiñ‍ci garahāma kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā| bhagavā hi, bhantai, anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū maggavidū maggakauvidau| maggānugā ca, bhantai, aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā saman‍nāgatā; ahañ‍ca khau, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ pavāraimi| na ca mai bhagavā kiñ‍ci garahati kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā’’ti|

‘‘na khvāhaṃ tai, sāriputta, kiñ‍ci garahāmi kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā| paṇḍitau tvaṃ, sāriputta, mahāpañ‍ñau tvaṃ, sāriputta, puthupañ‍ñau tvaṃ, sāriputta, hāsapañ‍ñau tvaṃ, sāriputta, javanapañ‍ñau tvaṃ, sāriputta, tikkhapañ‍ñau tvaṃ, sāriputta, nibbaidhikapañ‍ñau tvaṃ, sāriputta| saiyyathāpi, sāriputta, rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa jaiṭṭhaputtau pitarā pavattitaṃ cak‍kaṃ sammadaiva anuppavattaiti; aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, sāriputta, mayā anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ sammadaiva anuppavattaisī’’ti|

‘‘nau cai kira mai, bhantai, bhagavā kiñ‍ci garahati kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā| imaisaṃ pana, bhantai, bhagavā pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ na kiñ‍ci garahati kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā’’ti| ‘‘imaisampi khvāhaṃ, sāriputta, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ na  kiñ‍ci garahāmi kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā| imaisañhi, sāriputta, pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhusatānaṃ saṭṭhi bhikkhū taivij‍jā, saṭṭhi bhikkhū chaḷabhiñ‍ñā, saṭṭhi bhikkhū ubhataubhāgavimuttā, atha itarai pañ‍ñāvimuttā’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘aj‍ja pan‍narasai visuddhiyā, bhikkhū pañ‍casatā samāgatā|

saṃyaujanabandhanacchidā, anīghā khīṇapunabbhavā isī||

‘‘cak‍kavattī  yathā rājā, amac‍caparivāritau|

samantā anupariyaiti, sāgarantaṃ mahiṃ imaṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ vijitasaṅgāmaṃ, satthavāhaṃ anuttaraṃ|

sāvakā payirupāsanti, taivij‍jā mac‍cuhāyinau||

‘‘sabbai bhagavatau puttā, palāpaittha na vij‍jati|

taṇhāsal‍lassa hantāraṃ, vandai ādic‍cabandhuna’’nti||

8. parausahassasuttaṃ

216. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhatailasaihi bhikkhusataihi| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhū nibbānapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau bhagavā bhikkhū nibbānapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘parausahassaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, sugataṃ payirupāsati|

daisaintaṃ virajaṃ dhammaṃ, nibbānaṃ akutaubhayaṃ||

‘‘suṇanti dhammaṃ vimalaṃ, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ|

saubhati vata sambuddhau, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau||

‘‘nāganāmausi bhagavā, isīnaṃ isisattamau|

mahāmaighauva hutvāna, sāvakai abhivassati||

‘‘divāvihārā  nikkhamma, satthudassanakamyatā satthudassanakāmatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

sāvakau tai mahāvīra, pādai vandati vaṅgīsau’’ti||

‘‘kiṃ nu tai, vaṅgīsa, imā gāthāyau pubbai parivitak‍kitā, udāhu ṭhānasauva taṃ paṭibhantī’’ti? ‘na khau mai, bhantai, imā gāthāyau pubbai parivitak‍kitā, atha khau ṭhānasauva maṃ paṭibhantī’ti| ‘taina hi taṃ, vaṅgīsa, bhiyyausaumattāya pubbai aparivitak‍kitā gāthāyau paṭibhantū’ti| ‘aivaṃ, bhantai’ti khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavatau paṭissutvā bhiyyausaumattāya  bhagavantaṃ pubbai aparivitak‍kitāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘ummaggapathaṃ ummaggasataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) mārassa abhibhuyya, carati pabhij‍ja khilāni|

taṃ passatha bandhapamuñ‍cakaraṃ, asitaṃ bhāgasau pavibhajaṃ||

‘‘aughassa nittharaṇatthaṃ, anaikavihitaṃ maggaṃ akkhāsi|

tasmiñ‍cai amatai akkhātai, dhammaddasā ṭhitā asaṃhīrā||

‘‘paj‍jautakarau  ativijjha ativijjha dhammaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), sabbaṭṭhitīnaṃ atik‍kamamaddasa|

ñatvā ca sacchikatvā ca, aggaṃ sau daisayi dasaddhānaṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ  sudaisitai dhammai,

kau pamādau vijānataṃ dhammaṃ kau pamādau vijānataṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

tasmā hi tassa bhagavatau sāsanai|

appamattau sadā namassamanusikkhai’’ti||

9. kauṇḍañ‍ñasuttaṃ

217. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau āyasmā añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau añ‍ñākauṇḍañ‍ñau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) sucirassaiva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā  bhagavatau pādāni mukhaina ca paricumbati, pāṇīhi  ca parisambāhati, nāmañ‍ca sāvaiti – ‘‘kauṇḍañ‍ñauhaṃ, bhagavā, kauṇḍañ‍ñauhaṃ, sugatā’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñau sucirassaiva yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavatau pādāni mukhaina ca paricumbati, pāṇīhi ca parisambāhati, nāmañ‍ca sāvaiti – ‘kauṇḍañ‍ñauhaṃ, bhagavā, kauṇḍañ‍ñauhaṃ, sugatā’ti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ āyasmantaṃ añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñaṃ bhagavatau sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ, sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau āyasmantaṃ añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñaṃ bhagavatau sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘buddhānubuddhau sau thairau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau tibbanik‍kamau|

lābhī sukhavihārānaṃ, vivaikānaṃ abhiṇhasau||

‘‘yaṃ  sāvakaina pattabbaṃ, satthusāsanakārinā|

sabbassa taṃ anuppattaṃ, appamattassa sikkhatau||

‘‘mahānubhāvau  taivij‍jau, caitaupariyāyakauvidau|

kauṇḍañ‍ñau buddhadāyādau buddhasāvakau (pī॰), pādai vandati satthunau’’ti||

10. mauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

218. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati isigilipassai kāḷasilāyaṃ mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sabbaihaiva arahantaihi| taisaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau caitasā cittaṃ saman‍naisati samanvaisati (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) vippamuttaṃ nirupadhiṃ| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau bhagavā rājagahai viharati isigilipassai kāḷasilāyaṃ mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sabbaihaiva arahantaihi| taisaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau caitasā cittaṃ saman‍naisati vippamuttaṃ nirupadhiṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ bhagavatau sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavaiyya’’nti|

atha  khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ, sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ bhagavatau sammukhā sāruppāhi gāthāhi abhitthavi –

‘‘nagassa passai āsīnaṃ, muniṃ dukkhassa pāraguṃ|

sāvakā payirupāsanti, taivij‍jā mac‍cuhāyinau||

‘‘tai  caitasā anupariyaiti anupariyaisati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), mauggal‍lānau mahiddhikau|

cittaṃ naisaṃ saman‍naisaṃ samanvaisaṃ (syā॰ aṭṭha॰), vippamuttaṃ nirūpadhiṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ  sabbaṅgasampan‍naṃ, muniṃ dukkhassa pāraguṃ|

anaikākārasampan‍naṃ, payirupāsanti gautama’’nti||

11. gaggarāsuttaṃ

219. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sattahi ca upāsakasataihi sattahi ca upāsikāsataihi anaikaihi ca daivatāsahassaihi| tyāssudaṃ bhagavā atiraucati ativiraucati (ka॰) vaṇṇaina caiva yasasā ca| atha khau āyasmatau vaṅgīsassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau  bhagavā campāyaṃ viharati gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ pañ‍camattaihi bhikkhusataihi sattahi ca upāsakasataihi sattahi ca upāsikāsataihi anaikaihi ca daivatāsahassaihi| tyāssudaṃ bhagavā atiraucati vaṇṇaina caiva yasasā ca| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāya gāthāya abhitthavaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, bhagavā, paṭibhāti maṃ, sugatā’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, vaṅgīsā’’ti bhagavā avauca| atha khau āyasmā vaṅgīsau bhagavantaṃ sammukhā sāruppāya gāthāya abhitthavi –

‘‘candau  yathā vigatavalāhakai nabhai,

viraucati vigatamalauva bhāṇumā|

aivampi aṅgīrasa tvaṃ mahāmuni,

atiraucasi yasasā sabbalauka’’nti||

12. vaṅgīsasuttaṃ

220. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā vaṅgīsau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā vaṅgīsau aciraarahattappattau hutvā hauti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī (sī॰ pī॰) tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘kāvaiyyamattā vicarimha pubbai, gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ|

athaddasāma sambuddhaṃ, saddhā nau upapaj‍jatha||

‘‘sau mai dhammamadaisaisi, khandhāyatanadhātuyau khandhai āyatanāni dhātuyau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

‘‘bahun‍naṃ vata atthāya, baudhiṃ ajjhagamā muni|

bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnañ‍ca, yai niyāmagataddasā||

‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

‘‘pubbainivāsaṃ jānāmi, dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhitaṃ|

taivij‍jau iddhipattaumhi, caitaupariyāyakauvidau’’ti||

vaṅgīsasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

nikkhantaṃ  arati caiva, paisalā atimañ‍ñanā|

ānandaina subhāsitā, sāriputtapavāraṇā|

parausahassaṃ kauṇḍañ‍ñau, mauggal‍lānaina gaggarā|

vaṅgīsaina dvādasāti||


9. vanasaṃyuttaṃ

1. vivaikasuttaṃ

221. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu divāvihāragatau pāpakai akusalai vitak‍kai vitak‍kaiti gaihanissitai| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau anukampikā atthakāmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘vivaikakāmausi vanaṃ paviṭṭhau,

atha tai manau niccharatī bahiddhā|

janau janasmiṃ vinayassu chandaṃ,

tatau sukhī hauhisi vītarāgau||

‘‘aratiṃ pajahāsi satau, bhavāsi sataṃ taṃ sārayāmasai|

pātālarajau hi duttarau, mā taṃ kāmarajau avāhari||

‘‘sakuṇau yathā paṃsukunthitau paṃsukuṇṭhitau (ka॰), paṃsukuṇḍitau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), vidhunaṃ pātayati sitaṃ rajaṃ|

aivaṃ  bhikkhu padhānavā satimā, vidhunaṃ pātayati sitaṃ raja’’nti||

atha khau sau bhikkhu tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

2. upaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

222. aikaṃ  samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina  khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu divāvihāragatau supati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau anukampikā atthakāmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘uṭṭhaihi bhikkhu kiṃ saisi, kau atthau supitaina supinaina (sī॰) tai|

āturassa hi kā niddā, sal‍laviddhassa ruppatau||

‘‘yāya  saddhāya pabbajitau yāya saddhāpabbajitau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), agārasmānagāriyaṃ|

tamaiva saddhaṃ brūhaihi, mā niddāya vasaṃ gamī’’ti||

‘‘anic‍cā addhuvā kāmā, yaisu mandauva mucchitau|

baddhaisu khandhaisu (sī॰) muttaṃ asitaṃ, kasmā pabbajitaṃ tapai||

‘‘chandarāgassa vinayā, avij‍jāsamatik‍kamā|

taṃ ñāṇaṃ paramaudānaṃ pariyaudātaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), paramaudātaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰), paramavaudānaṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), kasmā pabbajitaṃ tapai||

‘‘chaitvā  bhaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) avij‍jaṃ vij‍jāya, āsavānaṃ parikkhayā|

asaukaṃ anupāyāsaṃ, kasmā pabbajitaṃ tapai||

‘‘āraddhavīriyaṃ pahitattaṃ, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamaṃ|

nibbānaṃ abhikaṅkhantaṃ, kasmā pabbajitaṃ tapai’’ti||

3. kassapagauttasuttaṃ

223. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā kassapagauttau kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā kassapagauttau divāvihāragatau añ‍ñataraṃ chaitaṃ auvadati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā āyasmantaṃ kassapagauttaṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaināyasmā kassapagauttau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ kassapagauttaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘giriduggacaraṃ chaitaṃ, appapañ‍ñaṃ acaitasaṃ|

akālai auvadaṃ bhikkhu, mandauva paṭibhāti maṃ||

‘‘suṇāti na vijānāti, ālaukaiti na passati|

dhammasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānasmiṃ, atthaṃ bālau na bujjhati||

‘‘sacaipi  dasa paj‍jautai, dhārayissasi kassapa|

naiva dakkhati rūpāni, cakkhu hissa na vij‍jatī’’ti||

atha khau āyasmā kassapagauttau tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

4. sambahulasuttaṃ

224. aikaṃ  samayaṃ sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu viharanti añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| atha khau tai bhikkhū vassaṃvuṭṭhā vassaṃvutthā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tai bhikkhū apassantī paridaivamānā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘arati viya maij‍ja khāyati,

bahukai disvāna vivittai āsanai|

tai cittakathā bahussutā,

kaumai gautamasāvakā gatā’’ti||

aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarā daivatā taṃ daivataṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsi –

‘‘māgadhaṃ  gatā kausalaṃ gatā, aikac‍ciyā pana vaj‍jibhūmiyā|

magā viya asaṅgacārinau, anikaitā viharanti bhikkhavau’’ti||

5. ānandasuttaṃ

225. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ānandau ativailaṃ gihisañ‍ñattibahulau viharati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā āyasmatau ānandassa  anukampikā atthakāmā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘rukkhamūlagahanaṃ pasak‍kiya, nibbānaṃ hadayasmiṃ aupiya|

jhā  gautama mā pamādau mā ca pamādau (sī॰ pī॰), kiṃ tai biḷibiḷikā karissatī’’ti||

atha khau āyasmā ānandau tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

6. anuruddhasuttaṃ

226. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| atha khau añ‍ñatarā tāvatiṃsakāyikā daivatā jālinī  nāma āyasmatau anuruddhassa purāṇadutiyikā yaināyasmā anuruddhau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tattha cittaṃ paṇidhaihi, yattha tai vusitaṃ purai|

tāvatiṃsaisu daivaisu, sabbakāmasamiddhisu|

purakkhatau parivutau, daivakañ‍ñāhi saubhasī’’ti||

‘‘duggatā  daivakañ‍ñāyau, sak‍kāyasmiṃ patiṭṭhitā|

tai cāpi duggatā sattā, daivakañ‍ñāhi patthitā’’ti||

‘‘na  tai sukhaṃ pajānanti, yai na passanti nandanaṃ|

āvāsaṃ naradaivānaṃ, tidasānaṃ yasassina’’nti||

‘‘na tvaṃ bālai vijānāsi, yathā arahataṃ vacau|

anic‍cā sabbasaṅkhārā, uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau||

‘‘natthi dāni punāvāsau, daivakāyasmi jālini|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

7. nāgadattasuttaṃ

227. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā nāgadattau kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nāgadattau atikālaina gāmaṃ pavisati, atidivā paṭik‍kamati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā āyasmatau nāgadattassa anukampikā atthakāmā āyasmantaṃ nāgadattaṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaināyasmā nāgadattau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ nāgadattaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kālai pavisa nāgadatta, divā ca āgantvā ativailacārī|

saṃsaṭṭhau gahaṭṭhaihi, samānasukhadukkhau||

‘‘bhāyāmi nāgadattaṃ suppagabbhaṃ, kulaisu vinibaddhaṃ|

mā haiva mac‍curañ‍ñau balavatau, antakassa vasaṃ upaisī’’ti vasamaiyyāti (sī॰ pī॰), vasamaisīti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

atha  khau āyasmā nāgadattau tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

8. kulagharaṇīsuttaṃ

228. aikaṃ  samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu añ‍ñatarasmiṃ kulai ativailaṃ ajjhaugāḷhappattau viharati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau anukampikā atthakāmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yā tasmiṃ kulai kulagharaṇī, tassā vaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘nadītīraisu saṇṭhānai, sabhāsu rathiyāsu ca|

janā saṅgamma mantainti, mañ‍ca tañ‍ca tvañ‍ca (ka॰) kimantara’’nti||

‘‘bahūhi saddā pac‍cūhā, khamitabbā tapassinā|

na taina maṅku hautabbaṃ, na hi taina kilissati||

‘‘yau ca saddaparittāsī, vanai vātamigau yathā|

lahucittauti taṃ āhu, nāssa sampaj‍jatai vata’’nti||

9. vaj‍jiputtasuttaṃ

229. aikaṃ samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau vaj‍jiputtakau bhikkhu vaisāliyaṃ viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina vaisāliyaṃ vaj‍jiputtakau sabbaratticārau hauti| atha  khau sau bhikkhu vaisāliyā tūriya-tāḷita-vādita-nigghausasaddaṃ  sutvā paridaivamānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘aikakā mayaṃ arañ‍ñai viharāma,

apaviddhaṃva apaviṭṭhaṃva (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vanasmiṃ dārukaṃ|

aitādisikāya rattiyā,

kau su nāmamhaihi nāma amhaihi (sī॰ pī॰) pāpiyau’’ti||

atha  khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau anukampikā atthakāmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘aikakauva  tvaṃ arañ‍ñai viharasi, apaviddhaṃva vanasmiṃ dārukaṃ|

tassa tai bahukā pihayanti, nairayikā viya saggagāmina’’nti||

atha khau sau bhikkhu tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

10. sajjhāyasuttaṃ

230. aikaṃ samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu yaṃ sudaṃ pubbai ativailaṃ sajjhāyabahulau viharati sau aparaina samayaina appaussuk‍kau tuṇhībhūtau saṅkasāyati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau dhammaṃ asuṇantī yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ  bhikkhuṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kasmā tuvaṃ dhammapadāni bhikkhu, nādhīyasi bhikkhūhi saṃvasantau|

sutvāna dhammaṃ labhatippasādaṃ, diṭṭhaiva dhammai labhatippasaṃsa’’nti||

‘‘ahu purai dhammapadaisu chandau, yāva virāgaina samāgamimha|

yatau  virāgaina samāgamimha, yaṃ kiñ‍ci diṭṭhaṃva sutaṃ mutaṃ vā|

añ‍ñāya nikkhaipanamāhu santau’’ti||

11. akusalavitak‍kasuttaṃ

231. aikaṃ samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu divāvihāragatau pāpakai akusalai vitak‍kai vitak‍kaiti, saiyyathidaṃ  – kāmavitak‍kaṃ, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau anukampikā atthakāmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘ayaunisau  manasikārā, sau vitak‍kaihi khaj‍jasi|

ayaunisau ayauniṃ (pī॰ ka॰) paṭinissaj‍ja, yaunisau anucintaya||

‘‘satthāraṃ  dhammamārabbha, saṅghaṃ sīlāni attanau|

adhigacchasi pāmauj‍jaṃ, pītisukhamasaṃsayaṃ|

tatau pāmauj‍jabahulau, dukkhassantaṃ karissasī’’ti||

atha khau sau bhikkhu tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

12. majjhanhikasuttaṃ

232. aikaṃ samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| atha khau tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā yaina sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tassa bhikkhunau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘ṭhitai majjhanhikai kālai, san‍nisīvaisu san‍nisin‍naisu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pakkhisu|

saṇataiva brahārañ‍ñaṃ, taṃ bhayaṃ paṭibhāti maṃ||

‘‘ṭhitai majjhanhikai kālai, san‍nisīvaisu pakkhisu|

saṇataiva brahārañ‍ñaṃ, sā rati paṭibhāti ma’’nti||

13. pākatindriyasuttaṃ

233. aikaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā bhikkhū kausalaisu viharanti añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā  muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā  pākatindriyā| atha  khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anukampikā atthakāmā tai bhikkhū saṃvaijaitukāmā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘sukhajīvinau purai āsuṃ, bhikkhū gautamasāvakā|

anicchā piṇḍamaisanā, anicchā sayanāsanaṃ|

laukai anic‍cataṃ ñatvā, dukkhassantaṃ akaṃsu tai||

‘‘duppausaṃ katvā attānaṃ, gāmai gāmaṇikā viya|

bhutvā bhutvā nipaj‍janti, parāgāraisu mucchitā||

‘‘saṅghassa  añ‍jaliṃ katvā, idhaikac‍cai vadāmahaṃ|

apaviddhā apaviṭṭhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) anāthā tai, yathā paitā tathaiva tai||

‘‘yai khau pamattā viharanti, tai mai sandhāya bhāsitaṃ|

yai appamattā viharanti, namau taisaṃ karaumaha’’nti||

atha khau tai bhikkhū tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitā saṃvaigamāpādunti|

14. gandhatthainasuttaṃ

234. aikaṃ samayaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu kausalaisu viharati añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai| taina khau pana samayaina sau bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā padumaṃ upasiṅghati| atha khau yā tasmiṃ vanasaṇḍai adhivatthā daivatā tassa bhikkhunau anukampikā atthakāmā taṃ bhikkhuṃ saṃvaijaitukāmā yaina  sau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘yamaitaṃ vārijaṃ pupphaṃ, adin‍naṃ upasiṅghasi|

aikaṅgamaitaṃ thaiyyānaṃ, gandhatthainausi mārisā’’ti||

‘‘na  harāmi na bhañ‍jāmi, ārā siṅghāmi vārijaṃ|

atha kaina nu vaṇṇaina, gandhatthainauti vuc‍cati||

‘‘yvāyaṃ bhisāni khanati, puṇḍarīkāni bhañ‍jati|

aivaṃ ākiṇṇakammantau, kasmā aisau na vuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘ākiṇṇaluddau  purisau, dhāticailaṃva makkhitau|

tasmiṃ mai vacanaṃ natthi, tvañ‍cārahāmi vattavai||

‘‘anaṅgaṇassa pausassa, nic‍caṃ sucigavaisinau|

vālaggamattaṃ pāpassa, abbhāmattaṃva khāyatī’’ti||

‘‘addhā maṃ yakkha jānāsi, athau mai anukampasi|

punapi yakkha vaj‍jāsi, yadā passasi aidisa’’nti||

‘‘naiva  taṃ upajīvāma, napi tai bhatakāmhasai|

tvamaiva bhikkhu jānaiyya, yaina gacchaiyya suggati’’nti||

atha khau sau bhikkhu tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau saṃvaigamāpādīti|

vanasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

vivaikaṃ  upaṭṭhānañ‍ca, kassapagauttaina sambahulā|

ānandau anuruddhau ca, nāgadattañ‍ca kulagharaṇī||

vaj‍jiputtau ca vaisālī, sajjhāyaina ayaunisau|

majjhanhikālamhi pākatindriya, padumapupphaina cuddasa bhavaiti||


10. yakkhasaṃyuttaṃ

1. indakasuttaṃ

235. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati indakūṭai pabbatai, indakassa yakkhassa bhavanai| atha khau indakau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘rūpaṃ na jīvanti vadanti buddhā, kathaṃ nvayaṃ vindatimaṃ sarīraṃ|

kutassa aṭṭhīyakapiṇḍamaiti, kathaṃ nvayaṃ saj‍jati gabbharasmi’’nti||

‘‘paṭhamaṃ kalalaṃ hauti, kalalā hauti abbudaṃ|

abbudā jāyatai paisi, paisi nibbattatī ghanau|

ghanā pasākhā jāyanti, kaisā laumā nakhāpi ca||

‘‘yañ‍cassa bhuñ‍jatī mātā, an‍naṃ pānañ‍ca bhaujanaṃ|

taina sau tattha yāpaiti, mātukucchigatau narau’’ti||

2. sak‍kanāmasuttaṃ

236. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau sak‍kanāmakau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘sabbaganthappahīnassa, vippamuttassa tai satau|

samaṇassa na taṃ sādhu, yadañ‍ñamanusāsasī’’ti yadañ‍ñamanusāsatīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)||

‘‘yaina kainaci vaṇṇaina, saṃvāsau sak‍ka jāyati|

na taṃ arahati sappañ‍ñau, manasā anukampituṃ||

‘‘manasā cai pasan‍naina, yadañ‍ñamanusāsati|

na taina hauti saṃyuttau, yānukampā sānukampā (sī॰ pī॰) anuddayā’’ti||

3. sūcilaumasuttaṃ

237. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā gayāyaṃ viharati ṭaṅkitamañ‍cai sūcilaumassa yakkhassa bhavanai| taina khau pana samayaina kharau ca yakkhau sūcilaumau  ca yakkhau bhagavatau avidūrai atik‍kamanti| atha khau kharau yakkhau sūcilaumaṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisau samaṇau’’ti! ‘‘naisau samaṇau, samaṇakau aisau’’| ‘‘yāva jānāmi yadi vā sau samaṇau yadi vā pana sau samaṇakau’’ti|

atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau kāyaṃ upanāmaisi| atha khau bhagavā kāyaṃ apanāmaisi| atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhāyasi maṃ samaṇā’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, bhāyāmi; api ca tai samphassau pāpakau’’ti| ‘‘pañhaṃ taṃ, samaṇa pucchissāmi| sacai mai na byākarissasi, cittaṃ vā tai khipissāmi, hadayaṃ vā tai phālaissāmi, pādaisu  vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya pāraṃ gaṅgāya (ka॰) khipissāmī’’ti| ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yau mai cittaṃ vā khipaiyya hadayaṃ vā phālaiyya pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipaiyya; api ca tvaṃ, āvusau, puccha yadā kaṅkhasī’’ti| atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi – ( ) (atha khau sūcilaumau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi|) (sī॰)

‘‘rāgau  ca dausau ca kutaunidānā,

aratī ratī laumahaṃsau kutaujā|

kutau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā,

kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajantī’’ti||

‘‘rāgau ca dausau ca itaunidānā,

aratī ratī laumahaṃsau itaujā|

itau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā,

kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajanti||

‘‘snaihajā attasambhūtā, nigraudhassaiva khandhajā|

puthū visattā kāmaisu, māluvāva vitatā vitthatā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) vanai||

‘‘yai  naṃ pajānanti yataunidānaṃ,

tai naṃ vinaudainti suṇauhi yakkha|

tai duttaraṃ aughamimaṃ taranti,

atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāyā’’ti||

4. maṇibhaddasuttaṃ

238. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā magadhaisu viharati maṇimālikai caitiyai maṇibhaddassa yakkhassa bhavanai| atha khau maṇibhaddau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘satīmatau  sadā bhaddaṃ, satimā sukhamaidhati|

satīmatau suvai saiyyau, vairā ca parimuc‍catī’’ti||

‘‘satīmatau sadā bhaddaṃ, satimā sukhamaidhati|

satīmatau suvai saiyyau, vairā na parimuc‍cati||

‘‘yassa sabbamahaurattaṃ rattiṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), ahiṃsāya ratau manau|

maittaṃ sau sabbabhūtaisu, vairaṃ tassa na kainacī’’ti||

5. sānusuttaṃ

239. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarissā upāsikāya sānu nāma puttau yakkhaina gahitau hauti| atha khau sā upāsikā paridaivamānā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘cātuddasiṃ  pañ‍cadasiṃ, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ||

‘‘upausathaṃ upavasanti, brahmacariyaṃ caranti yai|

na taihi yakkhā kīḷanti, iti mai arahataṃ sutaṃ|

sā dāni aj‍ja passāmi, yakkhā kīḷanti sānunā’’ti||

‘‘cātuddasiṃ  pañ‍cadasiṃ, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī|

pāṭihāriyapakkhañ‍ca, aṭṭhaṅgasusamāgataṃ|

upausathaṃ  upavasanti, brahmacariyaṃ caranti yai||

‘‘na taihi yakkhā kīḷanti, sāhu tai arahataṃ sutaṃ|

sānuṃ pabuddhaṃ vaj‍jāsi, yakkhānaṃ vacanaṃ idaṃ|

mākāsi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, āvi vā yadi vā rahau||

‘‘sacai  ca sacaiva (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), yañ‍caiva (sī॰) pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, karissasi karausi vā|

na tai dukkhā pamutyatthi, uppac‍cāpi palāyatau’’ti||

‘‘mataṃ vā amma raudanti, yau vā jīvaṃ na dissati|

jīvantaṃ amma passantī, kasmā maṃ amma raudasī’’ti||

‘‘mataṃ vā putta raudanti, yau vā jīvaṃ na dissati|

yau ca kāmai cajitvāna, punarāgacchatai idha|

taṃ vāpi putta raudanti, puna jīvaṃ matau hi sau||

‘‘kuk‍kuḷā ubbhatau tāta, kuk‍kuḷaṃ kuk‍kuḷai (sī॰) patitumicchasi|

narakā ubbhatau tāta, narakaṃ patitumicchasi||

‘‘abhidhāvatha  bhaddantai, kassa ujjhāpayāmasai|

ādittā nīhataṃ nibbhataṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), nibhataṃ (pī॰ ka॰) bhaṇḍaṃ, puna ḍayhitumicchasī’’ti||

6. piyaṅkarasuttaṃ

240. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā anuruddhau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā anuruddhau rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya dhammapadāni bhāsati| atha khau piyaṅkaramātā yakkhinī puttakaṃ aivaṃ tausaisi –

‘‘mā saddaṃ kari piyaṅkara, bhikkhu dhammapadāni bhāsati|

api api (sī॰) ca dhammapadaṃ vijāniya, paṭipaj‍jaima hitāya nau siyā||

‘‘pāṇaisu ca saṃyamāmasai, sampajānamusā na bhaṇāmasai|

sikkhaima susīlyamattanau susīlamattanau (sī॰ ka॰), api muc‍caima pisācayauniyā’’ti||

7. punabbasusuttaṃ

241. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina  khau pana samayaina bhagavā bhikkhū nibbānapaṭisaṃyuttāya dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai ca bhikkhū aṭṭhiṃ katvā manasi katvā sabbacaitasā  saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇanti| atha khau punabbasumātā yakkhinī puttakai aivaṃ tausaisi –

‘‘tuṇhī uttarikai hauhi, tuṇhī hauhi punabbasu|

yāvāhaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, dhammaṃ saussāmi satthunau||

‘‘nibbānaṃ bhagavā āha, sabbaganthappamaucanaṃ|

ativailā ca mai hauti, asmiṃ dhammai piyāyanā||

‘‘piyau laukai sakau puttau, piyau laukai sakau pati|

tatau piyatarā mayhaṃ, assa dhammassa magganā||

‘‘na hi puttau pati vāpi, piyau dukkhā pamaucayai|

yathā saddhammassavanaṃ, dukkhā maucaiti pāṇinaṃ||

‘‘laukai dukkhaparaitasmiṃ, jarāmaraṇasaṃyutai|

jarāmaraṇamaukkhāya, yaṃ dhammaṃ abhisambudhaṃ|

taṃ dhammaṃ sautumicchāmi, tuṇhī hauhi punabbasū’’ti||

‘‘ammā na byāharissāmi, tuṇhībhūtāyamuttarā|

dhammamaiva nisāmaihi, saddhammassavanaṃ sukhaṃ|

saddhammassa anañ‍ñāya, ammā dukkhaṃ carāmasai||

‘‘aisa  daivamanussānaṃ, sammūḷhānaṃ pabhaṅkarau|

buddhau antimasārīrau, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā’’ti||

‘‘sādhu khau paṇḍitau nāma, puttau jātau uraisayau|

puttau mai buddhasaiṭṭhassa, dhammaṃ suddhaṃ piyāyati||

‘‘punabbasu  sukhī hauhi, aj‍jāhamhi samuggatā|

diṭṭhāni ariyasac‍cāni, uttarāpi suṇātu mai’’ti||

8. sudattasuttaṃ

242. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati sītavanai| taina khau pana samayaina anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati rājagahaṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausi khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati – ‘‘buddhau kira laukai uppan‍nau’’ti| tāvadaiva ca pana bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitukāmau hauti| athassa  anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa aitadahausi – ‘‘akālau khau aj‍ja bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamituṃ| svai dānāhaṃ kālaina bhagavantaṃ dassanāya gamissāmī’’ti buddhagatāya satiyā nipaj‍ji| rattiyā sudaṃ tikkhattuṃ vuṭṭhāsi pabhātanti mañ‍ñamānau| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina sivathikadvāraṃ sīvathikadvāraṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) tainupasaṅkami| amanussā dvāraṃ vivariṃsu| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa nagaramhā nikkhamantassa ālaukau antaradhāyi, andhakārau pāturahausi, bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau udapādi, tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| atha khau sivakau sīvakau (sī॰ pī॰) yakkhau antarahitau saddamanussāvaisi –

‘‘sataṃ hatthī sataṃ assā, sataṃ assatarīrathā|

sataṃ kañ‍ñāsahassāni, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā|

aikassa padavītihārassa, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

‘‘abhik‍kama  gahapati, abhik‍kama gahapati|

abhik‍kamanaṃ tai saiyyau, nau paṭik‍kamana’’nti||

atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa andhakārau antaradhāyi, ālaukau pāturahausi, yaṃ ahausi bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau, sau paṭippassambhi| dutiyampi khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa ālaukau antaradhāyi, andhakārau pāturahausi, bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau udapādi, tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| dutiyampi khau sivakau yakkhau antarahitau saddamanussāvaisi –

‘‘sataṃ  hatthī sataṃ assā…pai॰…

kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

‘‘abhik‍kama gahapati, abhik‍kama gahapati|

abhik‍kamanaṃ tai saiyyau, nau paṭik‍kamana’’nti||

atha  khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa andhakārau antaradhāyi, ālaukau pāturahausi, yaṃ ahausi bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau, sau paṭippassambhi| tatiyampi khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa ālaukau antaradhāyi, andhakārau pāturahausi, bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau udapādi, tatauva puna nivattitukāmau ahausi| tatiyampi khau sivakau yakkhau antarahitau saddamanussāvaisi –

‘‘sataṃ hatthī sataṃ assā…pai॰…

kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

‘‘abhik‍kama gahapati, abhik‍kama gahapati|

abhik‍kamanaṃ tai saiyyau, nau paṭik‍kamana’’nti||

atha khau anāthapiṇḍikassa gahapatissa andhakārau antaradhāyi, ālaukau pāturahausi, yaṃ  ahausi bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau, sau paṭippassambhi| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina sītavanaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami|
