taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya abbhaukāsai caṅkamati| addasā khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna caṅkamā aurauhitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi sudattā’’ti| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati, nāmaina maṃ bhagavā ālapatīti, haṭṭhau udaggau tatthaiva bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci, bhantai, bhagavā sukhamasayitthā’’ti?

‘‘sabbadā vai sukhaṃ saiti, brāhmaṇau parinibbutau|

yau na limpati kāmaisu, sītibhūtau nirūpadhi||

‘‘sabbā āsattiyau chaitvā, vinaiyya hadayai daraṃ|

upasantau sukhaṃ saiti, santiṃ pappuyya caitasā’’ti caitasauti (sī॰)||

9. paṭhamasuk‍kāsuttaṃ

243. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina suk‍kā bhikkhunī mahatiyā parisāya parivutā dhammaṃ daisaiti| atha khau suk‍kāya bhikkhuniyā abhippasan‍nau yakkhau rājagahai rathikāya  rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘kiṃ mai katā rājagahai manussā, madhupītāva saiyarai|

yai suk‍kaṃ na payirupāsanti, daisaintiṃ amataṃ padaṃ||

‘‘tañ‍ca pana appaṭivānīyaṃ, asaicanakamaujavaṃ|

pivanti mañ‍ñai sappañ‍ñā, valāhakamiva panthagū’’ti valāhakamivaddhagūti (sī॰)||

10. dutiyasuk‍kāsuttaṃ

244. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau upāsakau suk‍kāya bhikkhuniyā bhaujanaṃ adāsi| atha khau suk‍kāya bhikkhuniyā abhippasan‍nau yakkhau rājagahai rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘puñ‍ñaṃ vata pasavi bahuṃ, sappañ‍ñau vatāyaṃ upāsakau|

yau suk‍kāya adāsi bhaujanaṃ, sabbaganthaihi vippamuttiyā’’ti vippamuttāyāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

11. cīrāsuttaṃ

245. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau upāsakau cīrāya cirāya (ka॰) bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ adāsi| atha  khau cīrāya bhikkhuniyā abhippasan‍nau yakkhau rājagahai rathikāya rathikaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘puñ‍ñaṃ  vata pasavi bahuṃ, sappañ‍ñau vatāyaṃ upāsakau|

yau cīrāya adāsi cīvaraṃ, sabbayaugaihi vippamuttiyā’’ti vippamuttāyāti (syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

12. āḷavakasuttaṃ

246. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā āḷaviyaṃ viharati āḷavakassa yakkhassa bhavanai| atha khau āḷavakau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā nikkhami| ‘‘pavisa, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā pāvisi| dutiyampi khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā nikkhami| ‘‘pavisa, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā pāvisi| tatiyampi  khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā nikkhami| ‘‘pavisa, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti bhagavā pāvisi| catutthampi khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, samaṇā’’ti| ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, nikkhamissāmi| yaṃ tai karaṇīyaṃ taṃ karauhī’’ti| ‘‘pañhaṃ taṃ, samaṇa, pucchissāmi| sacai mai na byākarissasi, cittaṃ vā tai khipissāmi, hadayaṃ vā tai phālaissāmi, pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipissāmī’’ti| ‘‘na khvāhaṃ taṃ, āvusau, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai  sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yai mai cittaṃ vā khipaiyya hadayaṃ vā phālaiyya, pādaisu vā gahaitvā pāragaṅgāya khipaiyya| api ca tvaṃ, āvusau, puccha yadā kaṅkhasī’’ti (atha khau āḷavakau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi|) (sī॰)|

‘‘kiṃsūdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, kiṃsu suciṇṇaṃ sukhamāvahāti|

kiṃsu havai sādutaraṃ rasānaṃ, kathaṃjīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭha’’nti||

‘‘saddhīdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, dhammau suciṇṇau sukhamāvahāti|

sac‍caṃ havai sādutaraṃ rasānaṃ, pañ‍ñājīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭha’’nti||

‘‘kathaṃsu tarati aughaṃ, kathaṃsu tarati aṇṇavaṃ|

kathaṃsu dukkhamac‍caiti, kathaṃsu parisujjhatī’’ti||

‘‘saddhāya  tarati aughaṃ, appamādaina aṇṇavaṃ|

vīriyaina dukkhamac‍caiti, pañ‍ñāya parisujjhatī’’ti||

‘‘kathaṃsu labhatai pañ‍ñaṃ, kathaṃsu vindatai dhanaṃ|

kathaṃsu kittiṃ pappauti, kathaṃ mittāni ganthati|

asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ, kathaṃ paic‍ca na saucatī’’ti||

‘‘saddahānau arahataṃ, dhammaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

sussūsaṃ  sussūsā (sī॰ pī॰) labhatai pañ‍ñaṃ, appamattau vicakkhaṇau||

‘‘patirūpakārī  dhuravā, uṭṭhātā vindatai dhanaṃ|

sac‍caina  kittiṃ pappauti, dadaṃ mittāni ganthati|

asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ, aivaṃ paic‍ca na saucati||

‘‘yassaitai caturau dhammā, saddhassa gharamaisinau|

sac‍caṃ dammau dhiti cāgau, sa vai paic‍ca na saucati||

‘‘iṅgha añ‍ñaipi pucchassu, puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇai|

yadi sac‍cā dammā cāgā, khantyā bhiyyaudha vij‍jatī’’ti||

‘‘kathaṃ nu dāni pucchaiyyaṃ, puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇai|

yauhaṃ sauhaṃ (sī॰), svāhaṃ (ka॰) aj‍ja pajānāmi, yau atthau samparāyikau||

‘‘atthāya vata mai buddhau, vāsāyāḷavimāgamā māgatau (pī॰ ka॰)|

yauhaṃ sauhaṃ (sī॰) aj‍ja pajānāmi, yattha din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ||

‘‘sau ahaṃ vicarissāmi, gāmā gāmaṃ purā puraṃ|

namassamānau sambuddhaṃ, dhammassa ca sudhammata’’nti||

yakkhasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

indakau sak‍ka sūci ca, maṇibhaddau ca sānu ca|

piyaṅkara punabbasu sudattau ca, dvai suk‍kā cīraāḷavīti dvādasa||


11. sak‍kasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. suvīrasuttaṃ

247. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, asurā daivai abhiyaṃsu| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau suvīraṃ daivaputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘aitai, tāta suvīra, asurā daivai abhiyanti| gaccha, tāta suvīra, asurai pac‍cuyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, suvīrau daivaputtau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā pamādaṃ āpādaisi āharaisi (katthaci) navaṅguttarai sīhanādasuttaipi| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau suvīraṃ daivaputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘aitai, tāta suvīra, asurā daivai abhiyanti| gaccha, tāta suvīra, asurai pac‍cuyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, suvīrau daivaputtau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā dutiyampi pamādaṃ āpādaisi| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau suvīraṃ daivaputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘aitai, tāta suvīra, asurā daivai abhiyanti| gaccha, tāta suvīra, asurai pac‍cuyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, suvīrau daivaputtau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā tatiyampi pamādaṃ āpādaisi| atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau suvīraṃ daivaputtaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘anuṭṭhahaṃ avāyāmaṃ, sukhaṃ yatrādhigacchati|

suvīra tattha gacchāhi, mañ‍ca tatthaiva pāpayā’’ti||

‘‘alasvassa  alasa’ssa (sī॰ pī॰), alasvāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) anuṭṭhātā, na ca kic‍cāni kārayai|

sabbakāmasamiddhassa, taṃ mai sak‍ka varaṃ disā’’ti||

‘‘yatthālasau  anuṭṭhātā, ac‍cantaṃ sukhamaidhati|

suvīra tattha gacchāhi, mañ‍ca tatthaiva pāpayā’’ti||

‘‘akammunā akammanā (sī॰ pī॰) daivasaiṭṭha, sak‍ka vindaimu yaṃ sukhaṃ|

asaukaṃ anupāyāsaṃ, taṃ mai sak‍ka varaṃ disā’’ti||

‘‘sacai atthi akammaina, kauci k‍vaci na jīvati|

nibbānassa hi sau maggau, suvīra tattha gacchāhi|

mañ‍ca tatthaiva pāpayā’’ti||

‘‘sau hi nāma, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sakaṃ puñ‍ñaphalaṃ upajīvamānau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraintau uṭṭhānavīriyassa vaṇṇavādī bhavissati| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, saubhaitha, yaṃ tumhai aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā uṭṭhahaiyyātha  ghaṭaiyyātha vāyamaiyyātha appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

2. susīmasuttaṃ

248. sāvatthiyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, asurā daivai abhiyaṃsu| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau susīmaṃ susimaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) daivaputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘aitai, tāta susīma, asurā daivai abhiyanti| gaccha, tāta susīma, asurai pac‍cuyyāhī’ti| ‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, susīmau daivaputtau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā pamādaṃ āpādaisi| dutiyampi  khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau  daivānamindau susīmaṃ daivaputtaṃ āmantaisi…pai॰… dutiyampi pamādaṃ āpādaisi| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau susīmaṃ daivaputtaṃ āmantaisi…pai॰… tatiyampi pamādaṃ āpādaisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau susīmaṃ daivaputtaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘anuṭṭhahaṃ  avāyāmaṃ, sukhaṃ yatrādhigacchati|

susīma tattha gacchāhi, mañ‍ca tatthaiva pāpayā’’ti||

‘‘alasvassa  anuṭṭhātā, na ca kic‍cāni kārayai|

sabbakāmasamiddhassa, taṃ mai sak‍ka varaṃ disā’’ti||

‘‘yatthālasau anuṭṭhātā, ac‍cantaṃ sukhamaidhati|

susīma tattha gacchāhi, mañ‍ca tatthaiva pāpayā’’ti||

‘‘akammunā daivasaiṭṭha, sak‍ka vindaimu yaṃ sukhaṃ|

asaukaṃ anupāyāsaṃ, taṃ mai sak‍ka varaṃ disā’’ti||

‘‘sacai atthi akammaina, kauci k‍vaci na jīvati|

nibbānassa hi sau maggau, susīma tattha gacchāhi|

mañ‍ca tatthaiva pāpayā’’ti||

‘‘sau hi nāma, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sakaṃ puñ‍ñaphalaṃ upajīvamānau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraintau uṭṭhānavīriyassa vaṇṇavādī bhavissati| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, saubhaitha, yaṃ tumhai aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā uṭṭhahaiyyātha ghaṭaiyyātha vāyamaiyyātha appattassa pattiyā, anadhigatassa adhigamāya, asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāyā’’ti|

3. dhajaggasuttaṃ

249. sāvatthiyaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai āmantaisi –

‘sacai, mārisā, daivānaṃ saṅgāmagatānaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya  bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, mamaiva tasmiṃ samayai dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha| mamañhi vau dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissati’|

‘nau cai mai dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha, atha pajāpatissa daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha| pajāpatissa hi vau daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissati’|

‘nau  cai pajāpatissa daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha, atha varuṇassa daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha| varuṇassa hi vau daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissati’|

‘nau cai varuṇassa daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha, atha īsānassa daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukaiyyātha| īsānassa hi vau daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissatī’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kassa vā daivānamindassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ, pajāpatissa vā daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ, varuṇassa vā daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ, īsānassa vā daivarājassa dhajaggaṃ ul‍laukayataṃ yaṃ  bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyaithāpi naupi pahīyaitha nau pahīyaitha (ka॰)|

‘‘taṃ  kissa haitu? sak‍kau hi, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau avītarāgau avītadausau avītamauhau bhīru chambhī utrāsī palāyīti|

‘‘ahañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘sacai tumhākaṃ, bhikkhavai, arañ‍ñagatānaṃ vā rukkhamūlagatānaṃ vā suñ‍ñāgāragatānaṃ vā uppaj‍jaiyya bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, mamaiva tasmiṃ samayai anussaraiyyātha – itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| mamañhi vau, bhikkhavai, anussarataṃ yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissati|

‘‘nau  cai maṃ anussaraiyyātha, atha dhammaṃ anussaraiyyātha – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti| dhammañhi vau, bhikkhavai, anussarataṃ yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissati|

‘‘nau cai dhammaṃ anussaraiyyātha, atha saṅghaṃ anussaraiyyātha – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau  ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti| saṅghañhi vau, bhikkhavai, anussarataṃ  yaṃ bhavissati bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā laumahaṃsau vā, sau pahīyissati|

‘‘taṃ kissa haitu? tathāgatau hi, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vītarāgau vītadausau vītamauhau abhīru acchambhī anutrāsī apalāyī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘arañ‍ñai rukkhamūlai vā, suñ‍ñāgāraiva bhikkhavau|

anussaraitha anussaraiyyātha (ka॰) padasiddhi pana cintaitabbā sambuddhaṃ, bhayaṃ tumhāka nau siyā||

‘‘nau  cai buddhaṃ saraiyyātha, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

atha dhammaṃ saraiyyātha, niyyānikaṃ sudaisitaṃ||

‘‘nau cai dhammaṃ saraiyyātha, niyyānikaṃ sudaisitaṃ|

atha saṅghaṃ saraiyyātha, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ||

‘‘aivaṃ buddhaṃ sarantānaṃ, dhammaṃ saṅghañ‍ca bhikkhavau|

bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā, laumahaṃsau na haissatī’’ti||

4. vaipacittisuttaṃ

250. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau asurai āmantaisi – ‘sacai, mārisā, daivānaṃ asurasaṅgāmai samupabyūḷhai asurā  jinaiyyuṃ daivā parājinaiyyuṃ parājaiyyuṃ (sī॰ pī॰), yaina naṃ sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi bandhitvā mama santikai ānaiyyātha asurapura’nti| sak‍kaupi khau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai āmantaisi – ‘sacai, mārisā, daivānaṃ asurasaṅgāmai samupabyūḷhai daivā jinaiyyuṃ asurā parājinaiyyuṃ, yaina naṃ vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi bandhitvā mama santikai ānaiyyātha sudhammasabha’’’nti| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, saṅgāmai daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiniṃsu parājiṃsu (sī॰ pī॰)| atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi bandhitvā sak‍kassa daivānamindassa santikai ānaisuṃ sudhammasabhaṃ| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau kaṇṭhapañ‍camaihi bandhanaihi baddhau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ sudhammasabhaṃ pavisantañ‍ca nikkhamantañ‍ca asabbhāhi pharusāhi vācāhi ak‍kausati paribhāsati| atha khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘bhayā nu maghavā sak‍ka, dubbalyā nau titikkhasi|

suṇantau pharusaṃ vācaṃ, sammukhā vaipacittinau’’ti||

‘‘nāhaṃ  bhayā na dubbalyā, khamāmi vaipacittinau|

kathañhi mādisau viñ‍ñū, bālaina paṭisaṃyujai’’ti||

‘‘bhiyyau bālā pabhij‍jaiyyuṃ, nau cassa paṭisaidhakau|

tasmā bhusaina daṇḍaina, dhīrau bālaṃ nisaidhayai’’ti||

‘‘aitadaiva  ahaṃ mañ‍ñai, bālassa paṭisaidhanaṃ|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammatī’’ti||

‘‘aitadaiva titikkhāya, vaj‍jaṃ passāmi vāsava|

yadā naṃ mañ‍ñati bālau, bhayā myāyaṃ titikkhati|

ajjhāruhati dummaidhau, gauva bhiyyau palāyina’’nti||

‘‘kāmaṃ  mañ‍ñatu vā mā vā, bhayā myāyaṃ titikkhati|

sadatthaparamā atthā, khantyā bhiyyau na vij‍jati||

‘‘yau havai balavā santau, dubbalassa titikkhati|

tamāhu paramaṃ khantiṃ, nic‍caṃ khamati dubbalau||

‘‘abalaṃ taṃ balaṃ āhu, yassa bālabalaṃ balaṃ|

balassa dhammaguttassa, paṭivattā na vij‍jati||

‘‘tassaiva taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ  carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati||

‘‘ubhin‍naṃ  tikicchantānaṃ, attanau ca parassa ca|

janā mañ‍ñanti bālauti, yai dhammassa akauvidā’’ti||

‘‘sau hi nāma, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sakaṃ puñ‍ñaphalaṃ upajīvamānau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraintau khantisaurac‍cassa vaṇṇavādī bhavissati| idha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, saubhaitha yaṃ  tumhai aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai pabbajitā samānā khamā ca bhavaiyyātha sauratā cā’’ti|

5. subhāsitajayasuttaṃ

251. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘hautu, daivānaminda, subhāsitaina jayau’ti| ‘hautu, vaipacitti, subhāsitaina jayau’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivā ca asurā ca pārisaj‍jai ṭhapaisuṃ – ‘imai nau subhāsitadubbhāsitaṃ ājānissantī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacittiṃ asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhaṇa, daivānaminda, gātha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaipacitti asurindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘tumhai khvaittha, vaipacitti, pubbadaivā| bhaṇa, vaipacitti, gātha’nti| aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘bhiyyau bālā pabhij‍jaiyyuṃ, nau cassa paṭisaidhakau|

tasmā bhusaina daṇḍaina, dhīrau bālaṃ nisaidhayai’’ti||

‘‘bhāsitāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, vaipacittinā asurindaina gāthāya asurā anumaudiṃsu, daivā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhaṇa, daivānaminda, gātha’nti| aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘aitadaiva  ahaṃ mañ‍ñai, bālassa paṭisaidhanaṃ|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammatī’’ti||

‘‘bhāsitāya  khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthāya, daivā anumaudiṃsu, asurā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhaṇa, vaipacitti, gātha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘aitadaiva titikkhāya, vaj‍jaṃ passāmi vāsava|

yadā naṃ mañ‍ñati bālau, bhayā myāyaṃ titikkhati|

ajjhāruhati dummaidhau, gauva bhiyyau palāyina’’nti||

‘‘bhāsitāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, vaipacittinā asurindaina gāthāya asurā anumaudiṃsu, daivā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhaṇa, daivānaminda, gātha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘kāmaṃ mañ‍ñatu vā mā vā, bhayā myāyaṃ titikkhati|

sadatthaparamā atthā, khantyā bhiyyau na vij‍jati||

‘‘yau havai balavā santau, dubbalassa titikkhati|

tamāhu paramaṃ khantiṃ, nic‍caṃ khamati dubbalau||

‘‘abalaṃ  taṃ balaṃ āhu, yassa bālabalaṃ balaṃ|

balassa dhammaguttassa, paṭivattā na vij‍jati||

‘‘tassaiva taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ  carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati||

‘‘ubhin‍naṃ  tikicchantānaṃ, attanau ca parassa ca|

janā mañ‍ñanti bālauti, yai dhammassa akauvidā’’ti||

‘‘bhāsitāsu khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthāsu, daivā anumaudiṃsu, asurā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivānañ‍ca asurānañ‍ca pārisaj‍jā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘bhāsitā khau vaipacittinā asurindaina gāthāyau| tā ca khau sadaṇḍāvacarā sasatthāvacarā, iti bhaṇḍanaṃ iti  viggahau iti kalahau| bhāsitā khau bhāsitā khau pana (sī॰) sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthāyau| tā ca khau adaṇḍāvacarā asatthāvacarā, iti abhaṇḍanaṃ iti aviggahau iti akalahau| sak‍kassa daivānamindassa subhāsitaina jayau’ti| iti khau, bhikkhavai sak‍kassa daivānamindassa subhāsitaina jayau ahausī’’ti|

6. kulāvakasuttaṃ

252. sāvatthiyaṃ| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, saṅgāmai asurā jiniṃsu, daivā parājiniṃsu| parājitā ca khau, bhikkhavai, daivā apāyaṃsvaiva uttarainamukhā, abhiyaṃsvaiva nai asurā| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau mātali saṅgāhakaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kulāvakā mātali simbalismiṃ,

īsāmukhaina parivaj‍jayassu|

kāmaṃ cajāma asuraisu pāṇaṃ,

māyimai dijā vikulāvakā vikulāvā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ahaisu’’nti||

‘‘‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā sahassayuttaṃ ājañ‍ñarathaṃ pac‍cudāvattaisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, asurānaṃ aitadahausi  – ‘pac‍cudāvattau khau dāni sak‍kassa daivānamindassa sahassayuttau ājañ‍ñarathau| dutiyampi khau daivā asuraihi saṅgāmaissantīti bhītā asurapuramaiva pāvisiṃsu| iti khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kassa daivānamindassa dhammaina jayau ahausī’’’ti|

7. nadubbhiyasuttaṃ

253. sāvatthiyaṃ| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kassa daivānamindassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘yaupi mai assa supac‍catthikau tassapāhaṃ na dubbhaiyya’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya  yaina sak‍kau daivānamindau tainupasaṅkami| addasā  khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘tiṭṭha, vaipacitti, gahitausī’’’ti|

‘‘yadaiva tai, mārisa, pubbai cittaṃ, tadaiva tvaṃ mā pajahāsī’’ti tadaiva tvaṃ mārisa pahāsīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)|

‘‘sapassu ca mai, vaipacitti, adubbhāyā’’ti adrubbhāya (ka॰)|

‘‘yaṃ musā bhaṇatau pāpaṃ, yaṃ pāpaṃ ariyūpavādinau|

mittaddunau ca yaṃ pāpaṃ, yaṃ pāpaṃ akatañ‍ñunau|

tamaiva pāpaṃ phusatu phusati (sī॰ pī॰), yau tai dubbhai sujampatī’’ti||

8. vairaucanaasurindasuttaṃ

254. sāvatthiyaṃ jaitavanai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā divāvihāragatau hauti paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau sak‍kau ca daivānamindau vairaucanau ca asurindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pac‍caikaṃ dvārabāhaṃ nissāya aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau vairaucanau asurindau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘vāyamaithaiva  purisau, yāva atthassa nipphadā|

nipphan‍nasaubhanau saubhinau (sī॰), saubhaṇau (pī॰ ka॰) atthau atthā (sī॰), vairaucanavacau ida’’nti||

‘‘vāyamaithaiva  purisau, yāva atthassa nipphadā|

nipphan‍nasaubhanau atthau nipphan‍nasaubhinau atthā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), khantyā bhiyyau na vij‍jatī’’ti||

‘‘sabbai  sattā atthajātā, tattha tattha yathārahaṃ|

saṃyaugaparamā tvaiva, sambhaugā sabbapāṇinaṃ|

nipphan‍nasaubhanau atthau, vairaucanavacau ida’’nti||

‘‘sabbai sattā atthajātā, tattha tattha yathārahaṃ|

saṃyaugaparamā tvaiva, sambhaugā sabbapāṇinaṃ|

nipphan‍nasaubhanau atthau, khantyā bhiyyau na vij‍jatī’’ti||

9. arañ‍ñāyatanaisisuttaṃ

255. sāvatthiyaṃ| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sambahulā isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā arañ‍ñāyatanai paṇṇakuṭīsu sammanti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau ca daivānamindau vaipacitti ca asurindau yaina tai isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau paṭaliyau aṭaliyau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), āṭaliyau (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.410 upāhanā ārauhitvā khaggaṃ aulaggaitvā chattaina dhāriyamānaina aggadvāraina assamaṃ pavisitvā tai isayau sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai apabyāmatau karitvā atik‍kami| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau paṭaliyau upāhanā aurauhitvā khaggaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ datvā chattaṃ apanāmaitvā dvārainaiva assamaṃ pavisitvā tai isayau sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai anuvātaṃ pañ‍jalikau namassamānau aṭṭhāsi’’| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tai isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsiṃsu –

‘‘gandhau  isīnaṃ ciradikkhitānaṃ,

kāyā cutau gacchati mālutaina|

itau  paṭik‍kamma sahassanaitta,

gandhau isīnaṃ asuci daivarājā’’ti||

‘‘gandhau isīnaṃ ciradikkhitānaṃ,

kāyā cutau gacchatu gacchati (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) mālutaina,

sucitrapupphaṃ sirasmiṃva mālaṃ|

gandhaṃ  aitaṃ paṭikaṅkhāma bhantai,

na haittha daivā paṭikūlasañ‍ñinau’’ti||

10. samuddakasuttaṃ

256. sāvatthiyaṃ| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sambahulā isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā samuddatīrai paṇṇakuṭīsu sammanti| taina khau pana samayaina daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ isīnaṃ sīlavantānaṃ kalyāṇadhammānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘dhammikā khau daivā, adhammikā asurā| siyāpi nau asuratau bhayaṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sambaraṃ asurindaṃ upasaṅkamitvā  abhayadakkhiṇaṃ yācaiyyāmā’’’ti| ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, tai isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā – saiyyathāpi  nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – samuddatīrai paṇṇakuṭīsu antarahitā sambarassa asurindassa sammukhai pāturahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tai isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā sambaraṃ asurindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsiṃsu –

‘‘isayau sambaraṃ pattā, yācanti abhayadakkhiṇaṃ|

kāmaṃkarau hi tai dātuṃ, bhayassa abhayassa vā’’ti||

‘‘isīnaṃ abhayaṃ natthi, duṭṭhānaṃ sak‍kasaivinaṃ|

abhayaṃ yācamānānaṃ, bhayamaiva dadāmi vau’’ti||

‘‘abhayaṃ  yācamānānaṃ, bhayamaiva dadāsi nau|

paṭiggaṇhāma tai aitaṃ, akkhayaṃ hautu tai bhayaṃ||

‘‘yādisaṃ vapatai bījaṃ, tādisaṃ haratai phalaṃ|

kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇaṃ, pāpakārī ca pāpakaṃ|

pavuttaṃ tāta tai bījaṃ, phalaṃ pac‍canubhaussasī’’ti||

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, tai isayau sīlavantau kalyāṇadhammā sambaraṃ asurindaṃ abhisapitvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – sambarassa asurindassa sammukhai antarahitā samuddatīrai paṇṇakuṭīsu pāturahaisuṃ| atha  khau, bhikkhavai, sambarau asurindau taihi isīhi sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihi abhisapitau rattiyā sudaṃ tikkhattuṃ ubbij‍jī’’ti|

paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

suvīraṃ susīmañ‍caiva, dhajaggaṃ vaipacittinau|

subhāsitaṃ jayañ‍caiva, kulāvakaṃ nadubbhiyaṃ|

vairaucana asurindau, isayau arañ‍ñakañ‍caiva|

isayau ca samuddakāti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. vatapadasuttaṃ

257. sāvatthiyaṃ| ‘‘sak‍kassa, bhikkhavai, daivānamindassa pubbai manussabhūtassa satta vatapadāni  vattapadāni (ka॰) samattāni samādin‍nāni ahaisuṃ, yaisaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā| katamāni satta vatapadāni? yāvajīvaṃ mātāpaittibharau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyī assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ saṇhavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ apisuṇavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasaiyyaṃ muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau, yāvajīvaṃ sac‍cavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ ak‍kaudhanau assaṃ – sacaipi mai kaudhau uppaj‍jaiyya, khippamaiva naṃ paṭivinaiyya’’nti| ‘‘sak‍kassa, bhikkhavai, daivānamindassa pubbai manussabhūtassa imāni satta vatapadāni samattāni samādin‍nāni ahaisuṃ, yaisaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā’’ti|

‘‘mātāpaittibharaṃ  jantuṃ, kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinaṃ|

saṇhaṃ sakhilasambhāsaṃ, paisuṇaiyyappahāyinaṃ||

‘‘macchairavinayai yuttaṃ, sac‍caṃ kaudhābhibhuṃ naraṃ|

taṃ vai daivā tāvatiṃsā, āhu sappurisau itī’’ti||

2. sak‍kanāmasuttaṃ

258. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘sak‍kau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau maghau nāma māṇavau ahausi, tasmā maghavāti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau purai purai purai (sī॰ pī॰) dānaṃ adāsi, tasmā purindadauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau sak‍kac‍caṃ dānaṃ adāsi, tasmā sak‍kauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau āvasathaṃ adāsi, tasmā vāsavauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau sahassampi atthānaṃ muhuttaina cintaiti, tasmā sahassakkhauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kassa, bhikkhavai, daivānamindassa sujā nāma asurakañ‍ñā pajāpati, tasmā sujampatīti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, bhikkhavai, daivānamindau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, tasmā daivānamindauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kassa, bhikkhavai daivānamindassa pubbai manussabhūtassa satta vatapadāni samattāni samādin‍nāni ahaisuṃ, yaisaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā| katamāni satta vatapadāni? yāvajīvaṃ mātāpaittibharau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyī assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ saṇhavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ apisuṇavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasaiyyaṃ muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau, yāvajīvaṃ sac‍cavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ ak‍kaudhanau assaṃ – sacaipi mai kaudhau uppaj‍jaiyya, khippamaiva naṃ paṭivinaiyya’’nti| ‘‘sak‍kassa, bhikkhavai, daivānamindassa pubbai manussabhūtassa imāni satta vatapadāni samattāni samādin‍nāni ahaisuṃ, yaisaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā’’ti|

‘‘mātāpaittibharaṃ  jantuṃ, kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinaṃ|

saṇhaṃ sakhilasambhāsaṃ, paisuṇaiyyappahāyinaṃ||

‘‘macchairavinayai yuttaṃ, sac‍caṃ kaudhābhibhuṃ naraṃ|

taṃ vai daivā tāvatiṃsā, āhu sappurisau itī’’ti||

3. mahālisuttaṃ

259. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau mahāli licchavī yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau mahāli licchavī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘diṭṭhau khau, bhantai, bhagavatā sak‍kau daivānamindau’’ti?

‘‘diṭṭhau khau mai, mahāli, sak‍kau daivānamindau’’ti|

‘‘sau hi nūna, bhantai, sak‍kapatirūpakau bhavissati| duddasau hi, bhantai, sak‍kau daivānamindau’’ti|

‘‘sak‍kañ‍ca khvāhaṃ, mahāli, pajānāmi sak‍kakaraṇai ca dhammai, yaisaṃ dhammānaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā, tañ‍ca pajānāmi|

‘‘sak‍kau, mahāli, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau maghau nāma māṇavau ahausi, tasmā maghavāti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, mahāli, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau sak‍kac‍caṃ dānaṃ adāsi, tasmā sak‍kauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, mahāli, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau purai dānaṃ adāsi, tasmā purindadauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, mahāli, daivānamindau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau āvasathaṃ adāsi, tasmā vāsavauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, mahāli, daivānamindau sahassampi atthānaṃ muhuttaina cintaiti, tasmā sahassakkhauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kassa, mahāli, daivānamindassa sujā nāma asurakañ‍ñā pajāpati, tasmā sujampatīti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kau, mahāli, daivānamindau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ issariyādhipac‍caṃ  raj‍jaṃ kāraiti, tasmā daivānamindauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘sak‍kassa, mahāli, daivānamindassa pubbai manussabhūtassa satta  vatapadāni samattāni samādin‍nāni ahaisuṃ, yaisaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā| katamāni satta vatapadāni? yāvajīvaṃ mātāpaittibharau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyī assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ saṇhavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ apisuṇavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasaiyyaṃ muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau, yāvajīvaṃ sac‍cavācau assaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ ak‍kaudhanau assaṃ – sacaipi mai kaudhau uppajjaiyya, khippamaiva naṃ paṭivinaiyya’’nti| ‘‘sak‍kassa, mahāli, daivānamindassa pubbai manussabhūtassa imāni  satta vatapadāni samattāni samādin‍nāni ahaisuṃ, yaisaṃ samādin‍nattā sak‍kau sak‍kattaṃ ajjhagā’’ti|

‘‘mātāpaittibharaṃ jantuṃ, kulai jaiṭṭhāpacāyinaṃ|

saṇhaṃ sakhilasambhāsaṃ, paisuṇaiyyappahāyinaṃ||

‘‘macchairavinayai yuttaṃ, sac‍caṃ kaudhābhibhuṃ naraṃ|

taṃ vai daivā tāvatiṃsā, āhu sappurisau itī’’ti||

4. daliddasuttaṃ

260. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau purisau imasmiṃyaiva  rājagahai manussadaliddau manussadaḷiddau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) ahausi manussakapaṇau  manussavarākau| sau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai saddhaṃ samādiyi, sīlaṃ samādiyi, sutaṃ samādiyi, cāgaṃ samādiyi, pañ‍ñaṃ samādiyi| sau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai saddhaṃ samādiyitvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā sutaṃ samādiyitvā cāgaṃ samādiyitvā pañ‍ñaṃ samādiyitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ  saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍ji daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahabyataṃ| sau añ‍ñai daivai atiraucati vaṇṇaina caiva yasasā ca| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! ayañhi daivaputtau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau manussadaliddau ahausi manussakapaṇau manussavarākau; sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahabyataṃ| sau añ‍ñai daivai atiraucati vaṇṇaina caiva yasasā cā’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai āmantaisi – ‘mā khau tumhai, mārisā, aitassa daivaputtassa ujjhāyittha| aisau khau, mārisā, daivaputtau pubbai manussabhūtau samānau tathāgatappavaiditai  dhammavinayai saddhaṃ samādiyi, sīlaṃ samādiyi, sutaṃ samādiyi, cāgaṃ samādiyi, pañ‍ñaṃ samādiyi| sau tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai saddhaṃ samādiyitvā sīlaṃ samādiyitvā sutaṃ samādiyitvā cāgaṃ samādiyitvā pañ‍ñaṃ samādiyitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nau daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsānaṃ sahabyataṃ| sau añ‍ñai daivai atiraucati vaṇṇaina caiva yasasā cā’’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau daivai tāvatiṃsai anunayamānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘yassa saddhā tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

‘‘saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘tasmā saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca, pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ|

anuyuñ‍jaitha maidhāvī, saraṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

5. rāmaṇaiyyakasuttaṃ

261. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, bhantai, bhūmirāmaṇaiyyaka’’nti?

‘‘ārāmacaityā  vanacaityā, paukkharañ‍ñau sunimmitā|

manussarāmaṇaiyyassa, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

‘‘gāmai vā yadi vārañ‍ñai, nin‍nai vā yadi vā thalai|

yattha arahantau viharanti, taṃ bhūmirāmaṇaiyyaka’’nti||

6. yajamānasuttaṃ

262. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘yajamānānaṃ manussānaṃ, puñ‍ñapaikkhāna pāṇinaṃ|

karautaṃ aupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, kattha din‍naṃ mahapphala’’nti||

‘‘cattārau ca paṭipan‍nā, cattārau ca phalai ṭhitā|

aisa saṅghau ujubhūtau, pañ‍ñāsīlasamāhitau||

‘‘yajamānānaṃ manussānaṃ, puñ‍ñapaikkhāna pāṇinaṃ|

karautaṃ aupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, saṅghai din‍naṃ mahapphala’’nti||

7. buddhavandanāsuttaṃ

263. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā divāvihāragatau hauti paṭisal‍līnau| atha khau sak‍kau ca daivānamindau brahmā ca sahampati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā pac‍caikaṃ dvārabāhaṃ nissāya aṭṭhaṃsu| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavatau santikai imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘uṭṭhaihi  vīra vijitasaṅgāma,

pan‍nabhāra anaṇa vicara laukai|

cittañ‍ca tai suvimuttaṃ,

candau yathā pan‍narasāya ratti’’nti||

‘‘na  khau, daivānaminda, tathāgatā aivaṃ vanditabbā| aivañ‍ca khau, daivānaminda, tathāgatā vanditabbā –

‘‘uṭṭhaihi vīra vijitasaṅgāma,

satthavāha anaṇa vicara laukai|

daisassu bhagavā dhammaṃ,

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’’ti||

8. gahaṭṭhavandanāsuttaṃ

264. sāvatthiyaṃ| tatra…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau mātaliṃ saṅgāhakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘yaujaihi, samma mātali, sahassayuttaṃ  ājañ‍ñarathaṃ| uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma subhūmiṃ dassanāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā sahassayuttaṃ ājañ‍ñarathaṃ yaujaitvā sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘yuttau khau tai, mārisa, sahassayuttau ājañ‍ñarathau| yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaijayantapāsādā aurauhantau  añ‍jaliṃ katvā pañ‍jalikau (pī॰), pañ‍jaliṃ katvā (ka॰) sudaṃ puthuddisā namassati| atha  khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘taṃ namassanti taivij‍jā, sabbai bhummā ca khattiyā|

cattārau ca mahārājā, tidasā ca yasassinau|

atha kau nāma sau yakkhau, yaṃ tvaṃ sak‍ka namassasī’’ti||

‘‘maṃ namassanti taivij‍jā, sabbai bhummā ca khattiyā|

cattārau ca mahārājā, tidasā ca yasassinau||

‘‘ahañ‍ca sīlasampan‍nai, cirarattasamāhitai|

sammāpabbajitai vandai, brahmacariyaparāyanai||

‘‘yai gahaṭṭhā puñ‍ñakarā, sīlavantau upāsakā|

dhammaina dāraṃ pausainti, tai namassāmi mātalī’’ti||

‘‘saiṭṭhā hi kira laukasmiṃ, yai tvaṃ sak‍ka namassasi|

ahampi tai namassāmi, yai namassasi vāsavā’’ti||

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna maghavā, daivarājā sujampati|

puthuddisā namassitvā, pamukhau rathamāruhī’’ti||

9. satthāravandanāsuttaṃ

265. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau mātaliṃ saṅgāhakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘yaujaihi, samma mātali, sahassayuttaṃ ājañ‍ñarathaṃ, uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma subhūmiṃ dassanāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā  sahassayuttaṃ ājañ‍ñarathaṃ yaujaitvā sak‍kassa daivānamindassa  paṭivaidaisi  – ‘yuttau khau tai, mārisa, sahassayuttau ājañ‍ñarathau| yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaijayantapāsādā aurauhantau añ‍jaliṃ katvā sudaṃ bhagavantaṃ namassati| atha khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘yañhi daivā manussā ca, taṃ namassanti vāsava|

atha kau nāma sau yakkhau, yaṃ tvaṃ sak‍ka namassasī’’ti||

‘‘yau idha sammāsambuddhau, asmiṃ laukai sadaivakai|

anaumanāmaṃ satthāraṃ, taṃ namassāmi mātali||

‘‘yaisaṃ rāgau ca dausau ca, avij‍jā ca virājitā|

khīṇāsavā arahantau, tai namassāmi mātali||

‘‘yai rāgadausavinayā, avij‍jāsamatik‍kamā|

saikkhā apacayārāmā, appamattānusikkharai|

tai namassāmi mātalī’’ti||

‘‘saiṭṭhā hi kira laukasmiṃ, yai tvaṃ sak‍ka namassasi|

ahampi tai namassāmi, yai namassasi vāsavā’’ti||

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna maghavā, daivarājā sujampati|

bhagavantaṃ namassitvā, pamukhau rathamāruhī’’ti||

10. saṅghavandanāsuttaṃ

266. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| tatra khau…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau mātaliṃ saṅgāhakaṃ āmantaisi – ‘yaujaihi, samma mātali, sahassayuttaṃ ājañ‍ñarathaṃ, uyyānabhūmiṃ gacchāma subhūmiṃ dassanāyā’ti| ‘aivaṃ  bhaddantavā’ti khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭissutvā, sahassayuttaṃ ājañ‍ñarathaṃ yaujaitvā sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paṭivaidaisi – ‘yuttau khau tai, mārisa, sahassayuttau ājañ‍ñarathau, yassa dāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaijayantapāsādā aurauhantau añ‍jaliṃ katvā sudaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ namassati| atha khau, bhikkhavai, mātali saṅgāhakau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘tañhi aitai namassaiyyuṃ, pūtidaihasayā narā|

nimuggā kuṇapamhaitai, khuppipāsasamappitā||

‘‘kiṃ nu taisaṃ pihayasi, anāgārāna vāsava|

ācāraṃ isinaṃ brūhi, taṃ suṇauma vacau tavā’’ti||

‘‘aitaṃ taisaṃ pihayāmi, anāgārāna mātali|

yamhā gāmā pak‍kamanti, anapaikkhā vajanti tai||

‘‘na taisaṃ kauṭṭhai aupainti, na kumbhi na kumbhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) na kaḷaupiyaṃ khaḷaupiyaṃ (sī॰)|

paraniṭṭhitamaisānā paraniṭṭhitamaisanā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), taina yāpainti subbatā||

‘‘sumantamantinau  dhīrā, tuṇhībhūtā samañ‍carā|

daivā viruddhā asuraihi, puthu mac‍cā ca mātali||

‘‘aviruddhā viruddhaisu, attadaṇḍaisu nibbutā|

sādānaisu anādānā, tai namassāmi mātalī’’ti||

‘‘saiṭṭhā hi kira laukasmiṃ, yai tvaṃ sak‍ka namassasi|

ahampi tai namassāmi, yai namassasi vāsavā’’ti||

‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna maghavā, daivarājā sujampati|

bhikkhusaṅghaṃ namassitvā, pamukhau rathamāruhī’’ti||

dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

daivā pana va tapadaina (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) tayau vuttā, daliddañ‍ca rāmaṇaiyyakaṃ|

yajamānañ‍ca vandanā, tayau sak‍kanamassanāti||

3. tatiyavaggau

1. chaitvāsuttaṃ

267. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavantaṃ gāthāya ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘kiṃsu chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kiṃsu chaitvā na saucati|

kissassu aikadhammassa, vadhaṃ raucaisi gautamā’’ti||

‘‘kaudhaṃ chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kaudhaṃ chaitvā na saucati|

kaudhassa visamūlassa, madhuraggassa vāsava|

vadhaṃ ariyā pasaṃsanti, tañhi chaitvā na saucatī’’ti||

2. dubbaṇṇiyasuttaṃ

268. sāvatthiyaṃ  jaitavanai| tatra khau…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarau yakkhau dubbaṇṇau aukauṭimakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa āsanai nisin‍nau ahausi| tatra sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! ayaṃ yakkhau dubbaṇṇau aukauṭimakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa āsanai nisin‍nau’’’ti! yathā  yathā khau, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, tathā tathā sau yakkhau abhirūpatarau caiva hauti dassanīyatarau ca pāsādikatarau ca|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivā tāvatiṃsā yaina sak‍kau daivānamindau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘idha tai, mārisa, añ‍ñatarau yakkhau dubbaṇṇau aukauṭimakau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa āsanai nisin‍nau| tatra sudaṃ, mārisa, daivā tāvatiṃsā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti – acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! ayaṃ yakkhau dubbaṇṇau aukauṭimakau  sak‍kassa daivānamindassa āsanai nisin‍nauti| yathā yathā khau, mārisa, daivā ujjhāyanti khiyyanti vipācainti, tathā tathā sau yakkhau abhirūpatarau caiva hauti dassanīyatarau  ca pāsādikatarau cāti| sau hi nūna, mārisa, kaudhabhakkhau yakkhau bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina sau kaudhabhakkhau yakkhau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā dakkhiṇajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ pathaviyaṃ nihantvā yaina sau kaudhabhakkhau yakkhau tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā tikkhattuṃ nāmaṃ sāvaiti – ‘sak‍kauhaṃ mārisa, daivānamindau, sak‍kauhaṃ, mārisa, daivānamindau’ti| yathā yathā khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau nāmaṃ sāvaisi, tathā tathā sau yakkhau dubbaṇṇatarau caiva ahausi aukauṭimakatarau ca| dubbaṇṇatarau caiva  hutvā aukauṭimakatarau ca tatthaivantaradhāyī’’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sakai āsanai nisīditvā daivai tāvatiṃsai anunayamānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘na  sūpahatacittaumhi, nāvattaina suvānayau|

na vau cirāhaṃ kujjhāmi, kaudhau mayi nāvatiṭṭhati||

‘‘kuddhāhaṃ na pharusaṃ brūmi, na ca dhammāni kittayai|

san‍niggaṇhāmi attānaṃ, sampassaṃ atthamattanau’’ti||

3. sambarimāyāsuttaṃ

269. sāvatthiyaṃ…pai॰… bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau ābādhikau ahausi dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina vaipacitti asurindau tainupasaṅkami gilānapucchakau| addasā khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘tikiccha maṃ daivānamindā’ti| ‘vācaihi  maṃ, vaipacitti, sambarimāya’nti| ‘na tāvāhaṃ vācaimi, yāvāhaṃ, mārisa, asurai paṭipucchāmī’’’ti| ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau asurai paṭipucchi – ‘vācaimahaṃ, mārisā, sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ sambarimāya’nti? ‘mā khau tvaṃ, mārisa, vācaisi sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ sambarimāya’’’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ gāthāya  ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘māyāvī  maghavā sak‍ka, daivarāja sujampati|

upaiti nirayaṃ ghauraṃ, sambarauva sataṃ sama’’nti||

4. ac‍cayasuttaṃ

270. sāvatthiyaṃ…pai॰… ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina dvai bhikkhū sampayaujaisuṃ| tatraikau bhikkhu ac‍casarā| atha khau sau bhikkhu tassa bhikkhunau santikai ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau daisaiti; sau bhikkhu nappaṭiggaṇhāti| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idha, bhantai, dvai bhikkhū sampayaujaisuṃ, tatraikau bhikkhu ac‍casarā| atha khau sau, bhantai, bhikkhu tassa bhikkhunau santikai ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau daisaiti, sau bhikkhu nappaṭiggaṇhātī’’ti|

‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, bālā| yau ca ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau na passati, yau ca ac‍cayaṃ daisaintassa yathādhammaṃ nappaṭiggaṇhā’’ti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dvai bālā| ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitā| yau ca ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau passati, yau ca ac‍cayaṃ daisaintassa yathādhammaṃ paṭiggaṇhā’’ti – imai khau, bhikkhavai, dvai paṇḍitā|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sudhammāyaṃ sabhāyaṃ daivai tāvatiṃsai anunayamānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘kaudhau  vau vasamāyātu, mā ca mittaihi vau jarā|

agarahiyaṃ mā garahittha, mā ca bhāsittha paisuṇaṃ|

atha pāpajanaṃ kaudhau, pabbatauvābhimaddatī’’ti||

5. ak‍kaudhasuttaṃ

271. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū…pai॰… bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sudhammāyaṃ sabhāyaṃ daivai tāvatiṃsai anunayamānau tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘mā  vau kaudhau ajjhabhavi, mā ca kujjhittha kujjhataṃ|

ak‍kaudhau avihiṃsā ca, ariyaisu ca paṭipadā vasatī sadā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)|

atha pāpajanaṃ kaudhau, pabbatauvābhimaddatī’’ti||

tatiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

chaitvā dubbaṇṇiyamāyā, ac‍cayaina akaudhanau|

daisitaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhaina, idañhi sak‍kapañ‍cakanti||

sak‍kasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


sagāthāvaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

daivatā  daivaputtau ca, rājā mārau ca bhikkhunī|

brahmā brāhmaṇa vaṅgīsau, vanayakkhaina vāsavauti||

sagāthāvaggasaṃyuttapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
